106.	“iraṇṭām āṇṭikketiril muppattāṟām āṇṭu” eṉṟa toṭarit tiru. viṣ (āca. ṟuhiḷā) (paracurāmar) vaṭṭattiṉ iraṇṭāvatil” eṉṟu moḻipeyarttuk ki.mu. 139 eṉṟu koṇṭār. āṉāl sar vālṭṭar eliyaṭ (ḷuci ṟuyaṭavanaca nuṭaṭiṭiva)” iraṇṭāvataṟku etirāṉa atāvatu nērmāṟāṉa 36 ām āṇṭu atāvatu 861 eṉṟu koṇṭār. ṭākṭar paṉl (ṉuca. kṣaracanēṭaṭa) mutal āṇṭu eṇ āṭciyāṇṭiyum iraṇṭāvatu āṇṭu eṇ maṉṉaṉ vayatāṇṭiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟu karutiṉār. ṭākṭar kālṭuvel (ṉuca. ūyaṭaṉaṟanaṭaṭa) iraṇṭām āṇṭu eṇ maṉṉaṉ āṭci āṇṭiyum, mutalāvatu āṇṭu eṇ viyāḻa vaṭṭam aṟupatil varum eṇṇiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. ṭākṭar halṭs (ṉuca. ḻaraṭavaṇaḷuh), intiyap paḻami ēṭu (aiṉēyin havēiḷūrayacala) ēṭu ōō, pak. 288 kāṇka. ṭākṭar halṭs karutukiṟapaṭi, mutal āṇṭu maṉṉaṉ aracurimiyāḷaṉāka urimi peṟṟu iḷavaracuppaṭṭamēṟṟa āṇṭik kuṟikkumeṉṟum, iraṇṭātu avaṉ āṭci āṇṭik kuṟikkumeṉṟum koṇṭār.
107.	90 āṇṭukaḷaṭaṅkiya kirakap parivirutti vaṭṭam: tīvak kuṟiyiṉ teṟkiluḷḷa makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum 90 āṇṭu vaṭṭam peritum aṟiyappaṭāta oṉṟu eṉṟu karnāṭakattiluḷḷa vāraṉ (ṟuyacacanan) karutukiṟār. maturiyil nāṟpatu āṇṭu vāḻnta kālattil pōrccukkīciya camaya kuruvākiya peski (kṣanaḷuhi) itip pakuttārāyntār. aṅkuḷḷa tāyaka vāṉūlār karuttuppaṭi atu cevvāyiṉ 15 valañcuṟṟu maṇṭalaṅkaḷ, putaṉiṉ 22, viyāḻaṉiṉ 11, veḷḷiyiṉ 5 caṉiyiṉ 29, ñāyiṟṟiṉ 1 valañcuṟṟu vaṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nāḷ eṇṇikkiyiṉ perukkamāka amintatu eṉṟu teriya varukiṟatu. itaṉ toṭaṅkukaṭṭam kaliyūḻi 3078 ām āṇṭu muṭivil atāvatu ki.mu. 24il nikaḻntatu. āṇṭukkaṇippu ñāyiṟṟu muṟi allatu vāṉmīṉ muṟippaṭiyamintatu. enta mēliyāṇṭukkum etirāṉa ivvāṇṭu eḷitilē 24aik kūṭṭi 90 āl vakuttuk kiṭṭa muṭiyum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, ki.pi. 1830kku, 1830-uṭaṉ 24 kūṭṭa, 20 vaṭṭaṅkaḷ 54 āṇṭukaḷ kiṭikkum : piriṉseppiṉ intiyap paḻamikaḷ tāmas (yīciḷēunayī’ḷa aiṉēyin havēi ḷūraviniḷa le kūhṭiayaḷa) ēṭu aiai, pakkam 158)
	kirakapparivirutti vaṭṭam tamiḻakattil āṇṭu `iraṭṭi eṇkaḷākat tarappaṭṭa ellāk kalveṭṭukaḷukkum pattiraṅkaḷukkum poruntuvatākum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka (malapār kiyēṭu, ēṭu aiai, pak. 122il) kaḷḷikkōṭṭi arukiluḷḷa tiruvaṇṇūril kāṇappaṭṭa kēraḷa aracaṉ irāmaṉuṭiya kalveṭṭiṉ āṇṭu 4 ām āṇṭukku etirāṉa 4 ām āṇṭu ākum itaṟkuc cariyāṉa kiṟistuva āṇṭu 4 o 90 + 4 - 24 = ki.pi. 340 kulacēkara tēvaṉiṉ tiruppuvaṉam paṭṭayam 13 ām āṇṭukku etirāṉa 12-ām āṇṭu ākum. atu 13 o 90 + 12 - 24 = ki.pi. 1158. kulacēkaratēvaṉ ilaṅkiyaracaṉ mutalām parākkirama pāku (1146-1179) kālattavaṉ (makāvamcō - iyal 76 - 77).
108.	paṭṭayattiṉ mūlamoḻi tamiḻāṉālum āṅkila mutaṉūl āciriyariṉ āṅkila moḻipeyarppiṉ maṟu tamiḻ moḻipeyarppē iṅkē kīḻē tarappaṭukiṟatu. mūlappaṭṭayattiṉ vācakam tamiḻāka āciriyar kikkuk kiṭṭavilli. atu kiṭṭiṉāl, mūlattamiḻē ivviṭattil ciṟappum payaṉum taruvatāka amiyum eṉpatil aiyamilli. ēṉeṉil 1800 āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa, caṅkakālattukkuriya, pēccuvaḻakkut tamiḻukkum atu oru ciṟanta cāṉṟu ākum.
109.	`pala nūṟāyirattāṇṭu ceṅkōl naṭatti āḷaniṉṟa’ eṉpataṉ kuṟippu ippattirattil oru vāḻttākat terikiṟatu.
110.	`piṭiyalum payantalum’ eṉṟa toṭarukku ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭṭiṉāl (ṉuca. ḻuraṉēnacava)’yāṉi mutaliya ūrtikaḷil cellutal’ eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatum ceṉṉi ilakkiya iyal nūl nāḷēṭu (āyaṉacayaḷa tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika ṭuvinacayavaracana & ḷuuninuna) ōaiaiai pakuti ai pakkam 137; āṉāl cariyāṉa poruḷ “paṇamālikaḷum poruḷākavum” eṉṟē tōṟṟukiṟatu. piṭi eṉpatu uṭaṉaṭikkācu eṉṟum, peṇyāṉi eṉṟum iru poruḷpaṭum. payaṇam allatu pacaṉam `paṅku’ eṉṟu poruḷ tarukiṟatu. `iṭuvaṭi’ eṉṟa col ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭṭāl `vaḷivukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya vāyil  eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl entap paṇṭi nūlilum atu intap poruḷil vaḻaṅkavilli. cariyāṉa poruḷ pātukikaḷ allatu mitiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatē eṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.
111.	kūnaṭaṭiuhnacacala.
112.	ippattirattiṉ nērpaṭam oṉṟum moḻipeyarppum ṭākṭar halṭhiṉāl (ṉuca. ḻaraṭavaṇaḷuh) veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. intiyap paḻimi ēṭu (aiṉēyin havēiḷūrayacala) ēṭu ōō pakkam 285.
113.	`ivvāṟu nūṟṟuvar nāṭṭiṉ mēṟpārviyāḷarum kāppāḷarum āvar’ malapārk kiyēṭu, (āyaṭayayeca āyarēyaṭa) ēṭu ai, pakkam 267. iṉṉoru pattiram irāmavaḷa nāṭṭiṉ 600 paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. āṅkilēyar malapāri veṉṟu koḷḷum vari inta ūrk kuṭiyaracukaḷ toṭarntu nilavi vantaṉa. “nāṭu allatu maṇṭalam pala ūrk kuṭiyaracukaḷ allatu tarikaḷiṉ tokutiyā yiruntatu. nāṭṭiṉ kuṭṭam allatu pēravi mikka pērāṟṟaluṭiya pērāṇmi vāynta ōr aviyāyiruntatu. tēvi ēṟpaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil atu aracaṉ āṟṟali otukkivittu viṭṭu avarkaḷiṉ amiccarkaḷi avarkaḷatu `varampumīṟiya ceyalkaḷukkut taṇṭikka muṭiyum!” ivvācakaṅkaḷ uṇmiyil 1746il kōḻikkōṭṭil naṭipeṟṟa uḷnāṭṭuk kalavara niliyi viḷakki eḻutumpaṭi kēṭṭuk koḷḷappaṭṭa pōtu, avviṭattil āṅkilak kiḻakkintiya vaṇikak kuḻāttiṉariṉ pērāḷāyiruntavar kuḻāttiṉarukku aṉuppiya viḷakkattiṉ vācakaṅkaḷēyākum. inta aṟivippu muḻuvatum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvataṟkuriya taṉi mukkiyattuvam uṭiyatu. ēṉeṉil aṉṟu naṭi muṟiyilirunta ceytikaḷi atu tiṟampaṭa viḷakkukiṟatu. avar eḻutiyatāvatu: “inta nāyakarkaḷ kōḻikkōṭṭu makkaḷiṉ talivarkaḷātalāl, āṅkilac caṭṭa maṉṟattip pōṉṟa niliyiluḷḷavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ ellāc ceytikaḷilumē aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷikaḷi ēṟṟup paṇipavarkaḷallar. aracaṉiṉ amiccarkaḷ takāc ceyalkaḷ puriyumpōtu aracarkaḷ kaṇṭittu oṟukkiṟārkaḷ.” malapārk kiyēṭu (āyaṭayayeca āyarēyaṭa) ēṭu ai pakkam. 89.
5. cōḻar
cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya maṇṭalaṅkaḷi āṇṭa aracariṉ taṉippaṭṭa vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷilē, āṅkāṅkē tuṇṭut tuṇūkkāṉa kuṟippukkaḷē iṉṟu namakku vantu eṭṭiyuḷḷa paṇṭi ilakkiya ēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟilum cila camayam orē aracaṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷil pala paṭṭappeyarkaḷāl kuṟikkappeṟukiṟāṉ. orē marapiṉ pala aracarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi orē peyarāl cuṭṭappaṭavum nērkiṟatu. intat tuṇṭut tuṇukkāṉa kuṟippukkaḷiyellām tokuttuk kōttu, avaṟṟiliruntu toṭarpāṉa varalāṟu kāṇpateṉpatu eḷitāṉa ceytiyaṉṟu. atilum itil nāṉ mutal taṭaviyāka muṉiyavēṇṭiyi ruppatāl, putiya ārāyccikaḷ mūlam nāṉ kūṟiya takavalkaḷ piḻipaṭṭaviyāka nēriṉum nēralām. inniliyil nāṉ piṉpavarum tamiḻaracar āṭci varalāṟṟuk kōviyi ki.pi. 50-kkum 150-kkum iṭippaṭṭa kālattukkuḷḷāka variyaṟuttuk koḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ.
it taṟuvāyip payaṉpaṭutti, paṇṭit tamiḻ kavitiyiṉ pakutikaḷ palavaṟṟi moḻipeyarttut tantuḷḷēṉ. ēṉeṉil, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkak kōṭṭupāṭukaḷ paṟṟi nāṉ eṉ coṟkaḷāl evvaḷavu varuṇittālum, av varuṇaṉiyik kāṭṭilum paṉmaṭaṅku teḷivāṉa, viḷakkamāṉa karuttōviyatti innūṟpakutikaḷ taramuṭiyum.
nāṉ ēṟkeṉavē kuṟittuḷḷapaṭi, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṟa imayavarampaṉ ilaṅkik kayavākuvuṭaṉum makata nāṭṭuk karṇaruṭaṉum camakālattavaṉ eṉṟa cilappatikārak kuṟippu, tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟil oru mukkiyamāṉa varalāṟṟu viḷakkak kal ākum. atē nūlil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉ talinakarāṉa vañciyil kaṇṇakikkuk kōyileṭukkum viḻāvukkuk kayavākuvi aḻitta pōtu avaṉ 50 āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭavaṉā yiruntāṉeṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1 kayavāku cērar talinakarukku avaṉ āṭciyiṉ kaṭici āṇṭilēyē ceṉṟāṉ. accamayam ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ 50 āṇṭuṭiyavaṉā yiruntamiyāl, avaṉ ki.pi. 75-il piṟantavaṉākavēṇṭum eṉalām.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tāy karikālacōḻaṉ makaḷ. eṉavē karikālaṉ piṟanta āṇṭu avaṉ peyaraṉāṉa ceṅkuṭvaṉ piṟanta āṇṭi viṭa 40 āṇṭukaḷukkāvatu muṟpaṭṭatāyirukka vēṇṭum. avaṉ kiṭṭattaṭṭa ki.pi. 35-il piṟantavaṉ eṉṟu koḷḷalām. avaṉ mikavum ciṟu vayatilēyē aracirukki ēṟi, vaḻakkamīṟiya nīṇṭa kālam cōḻanāṭṭi āṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē avaṉ āṭcikkālam ēṟattāḻa ki.pi. 50 mutal 95 vari nīṭittirukka iṭamuṇṭu.
karikālaṉ uruvappaḵṟēr iḷiyōṉ allatu iḷañ cēṭceṉṉi eṉṟa iḷavaracaṉ putalvaṉ2. avaṉ piṟappataṟku muṉpē avaṉ tanti iṟantuviṭṭāṉ. pala urimiyāḷar pōṭṭiyiṭṭuc cōḻa aracirukkiyik kippaṟṟiyatuṭaṉ, tīyāl avaṉi aḻittuviṭavum cati ceytaṉar. avaṉ ivaṟṟiliruntu tappiṉālum, avaṉ kālalkaḷ tīppuṇ paṭṭuk karukiviṭṭaṉa. itaṉālēyē avaṉ karikālaṉ atāvatu kariya kāliyuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ. taṉ muṉṉōr kaḷukkuriya araca pīṭatti mīṭpatil avaṉ māmaṉ piṭart taliyaṉ avaṉukkup pērutaviyāyiruntāṉ.4
iṭarkaḷiṭiyē vaḷarntu avaṉ vāḻviṉ paṭippiṉi kaḷāl meyyaṟivu vāykkap peṟṟavaṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉ kāla aracarkaḷiṭiyē avaṉē aṟiviṟ ciṟantavaṉākavum, āṟṟalil mikkavaṉākavum viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉ iḷamik kālam paṟṟiya oru nikaḻcci avaṉ camaya vāyppāṉa kūraṟiviyum maṉa urattiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
an nāṭkaḷil ellāk kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷilum urimi vaḻakkukaḷilum maṉṉaṉē mutaṉmi muṟiyāḷaṉāyiruntāṉ. immuṟiyil karikālaṉiṭam oru cikkal vāynta vaḻakkut tīrppukku vantiruntatu.5 ataṉ muṭivu paṟṟi mutiyōrākiya avaṉ amiccarkaḷ kavalippaṭṭaṉar. iḷavēntaṉ avarkaḷ mukat tōṟṟattil karanta eṇṇaṅkaḷi uṇarntukoṇṭāṉ. avaṉ uṭaṉ tāṉē taṉ taṉiyaṟikkuc ceṉṟu, pōli nari mayirk kōlampūṇṭu, kiḻavaṉuruvilē mīṇṭu vantāṉ. aracirukkiyil mīṇṭum amarntu irutiṟattāriyum mikat tiṟamiyuṭaṉ kuṟukkucāvi, avarkaḷ vāymoḻiyiliruntē meynnili kāṭṭic cariyaṉa tīrppu varuvittāṉ. meyyākavē narittaliyuṭiya avaṉ mutumi vāynta amiccarkaḷiṉ ārvap pārāṭṭukku itu urittāyiṟṟu.
karikālaṉ oru periya vīraṉ. veṇṇip peruveḷiyil naṭipeṟṟa mutal pōril, avaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ, cēraṉ ākiya iru maṉṉarkaḷiṉ iṇippaṭikaḷiyum veṉṟāṉ. taṉpaṭikaḷit tāṉē naṭattiya cēraṉ mutalām ātaṉ ippōril mutukil puṇpaṭṭāṉ. itu an nāṭkaḷil kōḻimiyiṉ māṟāc ciṉṉamākak karutappaṭṭataṉāl, inta avamatippip poṟāta tūya vīraṉāṉa cēraṉ tāṉāka  māḷvi etirēṟkat tuṇintāṉ. pōrnikaḻcciyiṉ pōtu avaṉuṭaṉirunta kaḻāt taliyār eṉṉum pulavar tam maṉṉaṉ tōlvikku maṉam nivuṟṟu, ataṉāl avaṉ kuṭikaḷukku nērnta maṉamaṟukkattiyum cūḻtuyar iruṭciyiyum kīḻvarumpākkaḷil tīṭṭiyuḷḷār.6
“muracu muḻaṅkavilli. yāḻ iciyi maṟantatu. akaṉṟa pālvaṭṭilkaḷ pāliṉṟi varaṇṭu kiṭantaṉa. cuṟucuṟuppuvāynta tēṉīkkaḷ tiraṭṭiya tēṉit tīṇṭuvārilli. uḻavar kaḻaṉikaḷil uḻutalit tavirntaṉar. ūrppuṟa veḷikaḷil viḻā payil kūṭṭaṅkaḷ etuvumilli. muḻu nilāk kali niṟivuṭaṉ eḻum camayattil ñāyiṟu malikaḷiṉ piṉ maṟivatupōla, em vīra maṉṉaṉ mutukil puṇpaṭṭataṉāl māṉam poṟāmal vāḷip puṟatteṟintu (paṭṭiṉi kiṭantu) iṟantu viṭat tuṇintāṉ. antō, innāṭkaḷ evvaḷavu kaḷippiṟanta tuyaram kappiya nāṭkaḷ!”
iṉṉoru kaviñar veṇṇil kuyattiyār cōḻa aracaṉuṭaṉ pōril iruntār. cēraṉiṉ nalaṅkeṭṭa nili avar uḷḷattiyum tittatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. karikālaṉi viḷittu avar piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār.7
“paranta kaṭalil cellumpōtu kappaliṉ pāykaḷi ōṭṭumpaṭi kāṟṟiyē ēvum tiṟam vāynta vīraṉiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalē! karikālvaḷava! ip pōril vīram piṟakkap pōrāṟṟiya valimi vāynta yāṉippaṭikaḷiṉkōvē! uṉṉi etirttu niṉṟu pōrāṭi mutukil puṇ uṟṟa māṉakkēṭu tāṅkāmal pukaḻ māḷvu peṟum paṭi uṇavu maṟuttup paṭṭiṉi kiṭakkum avvaracaṉ uṉṉiṉum mēmpaṭṭavaṉ allaṉō?”
maṟṟoru pōril karikālaṉ oṉpatu maṉṉarkaḷ cērnta kūṭṭuppaṭiyi etirttu muṟiyaṭittāṉ8. āyariṉ maṉṉar marapi vērōṭaḻittu, oḷinākar, aruvāḷar ākiya marapiṉarit taṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭuttiṉāṉ9. nāṭōṭī marapiṉarāṉa kuṟumpari avaṉ kīḻaṭakkiṉāṉ10. avanti vēntar. vaccira maṉṉar, makatap pēraracar ākiyavarkaḷuṭaṉ avaṉ naṭpuṭiyavaṉāy iruntāṉ11. piṟkālap pulavarkaḷ avaṉi vāṉaḷāvap pukaḻntu puṉinturit tuḷḷārkaḷ. avaṉ taṉ paṭiyāṟṟali intiya purāṇaṅkaḷil ulakiṉ naṭuviṭamākak kūṟappaṭṭa poṉmali allatu mēruvariyil koṇṭu ceṉṟatākavum, ataṉ ucciyil taṉ pulicciṉṉam poṟittākavum avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.12
kāviriyil āṇṭutōṟum varum paruva maḻi veḷḷam cōḻanāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyi veḷḷak kāṭākki vantatu. itu mika aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntu vantataṉālēyē an nāṭṭukkup puṉal nāṭu allatu veḷḷam perukiya nāṭu eṉṟa peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu. taṉ etirikaḷi ellām vīḻttit taṉ pēraracil amiti nāṭṭiya karikālaṉ ippōtu āṭcip pakutiyiluḷḷa nāṭukaḷic cīramip patil karuttuc celuttiṉāṉ. nāṭṭil aḻivu parappi aṭikkaṭi tolli tarum ivveḷḷattiṉ aḻivit taṭuttu niṟuttum māperun tiṭṭamoṉṟi mēṟkoṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ kāviri, irukarikaḷilum neṭuka uyar kari eḻuppa muṉintatuṭaṉ, ataṉ nīrvaḷattip parappi oḻuṅku paṭuttumpaṭi aṇikaḷum kālvāykaḷum kaṭṭa muṟpaṭṭāṉ.13 itaṉāl kāviriyiṉ nīr paraviya iṭaṅkaḷilellām ataṉpayaṉāka, vitimaṇi ovvoṉṟum an nilattil ōrāyiramāka viḷiyum eṉṟa paḻamoḻi ēṟpaṭalāyiṟṟu.
cōṇāṭṭiṉ talinakaram kaṭaṟkariyiliruntu miku toli vilaki uṟiyūriliruppatu nāṭṭiṉa vāṇika vaḷattukku ēṟṟataṉṟu eṉṟu matiyārnta ivvaracaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. ākavē avaṉ kāviriyiṉ kaṭal mukattiliruntu pukaḻvāynta vāṇikakkaḷatti araṇceytu atit taṉ talinakaram ākkiṉāṉ.14
vāṇikattukkum uḻavukkum avaṉ ivvāṟu taṉ aṟivārnta tiṭṭaṅkaḷāl tanta ūkkam virintu viyattaku payaṉ tantatu. avaṉ nāṭṭiṉ celvamum vaḷamum paṉmaṭaṅku perukkamuṟṟatu. naṉṟimikka avaṉ kuṭikaḷ ikkāraṇattāl avaṉi eṉṟum karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ allatu peruñcōḻaṉ karikālaṉ eṉṟaḻittaṉar.
karikālaṉukkup peruntirumāvaḷavaṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu15. avaṉ āṭciyiṉ mālippōtil, pāṇṭiyaṉ veḷḷiyampalattut tuñciya peruvaḻuti naṭpumuṟiyil karikālaṉik kāṇacceṉṟiruntatākat terikiṟatu. irumaṉṉar kaḷum oruṅkirunta camayam, kāvirippaṭṭiṉattup pulavar kārikkaṇṇaṉār iruvariyum nōkkip pāṭiyatu varumāṟu.16
“(palanāṭukaḷukkum)vaḷam koḻikkum puṉaliyuṭiya kāviriyaṉ kōmāṉ nī!”
“iv varacaṉō, vīram ceṟinta iṉattavarāṉa pañcavar kaḷiṭiyē oru ciṅka ēṟu. mūttōr kaḷampaṭiṉum taḷarāmal kaḷamveṉṟu kuṭipurantāṭci ceyyum kuricil. tāymaram paṭṭālum ataṉ kiḷiyiṉiṉṟu viḻutāka nilattilūṉṟi vērkkoṇṭu ataṉi vāḻvikkum kiḷimaram pōṉṟavaṉ avaṉ. iḷiyōṉāyiṉum nākak kūṭṭattiṭiyē viḻum iṭiyaṟu pōla etirikaḷiṭiyē ceṉṟu tākkic citaṟaṭittuḷḷāṉ.”
“nī aṟat taṅkum uṟanti vīraṉ. iv varacaṉō, ēṉiya aracarkaḷippōla nīrvaḷam vāynta kaḻaṉikaḷiyum payir vaḷaṅkaḷiyum maṭṭum uṭiyavaṉallaṉ. avaṟṟuṭaṉ mali taru vaḷamākac cantaṉa maraṅkaḷiyum kaṭal taru vaḷamāka muttukkaḷi yum uṭiyavaṉ. iṭimuḻakkamiṭum muracumūṉṟuṭaṉ tamiḻk kalvikkirup piṭamākiya kūṭal nakari aḷiceytu āḷupavaṉ. paṉikkoṭi ēntiya veḷḷiyōṉum (palatēvaṉum) āḻi ēntiya kariyōṉum (tirumālum) ākiya iru tēvarkaḷip pōla vīṟuṭiyavarkaḷāy nīṅkaḷ iruvarum etirikaḷukku naṭukkantarattakkavarkaḷ. uṅkaḷi yotta ittakiya iru peruvēntarkaḷiṉ naṭpippōla iṉitāvatu ētēṉum uṇṭā?”
“eṉ urikaḷi iruvīrum cevikoṭuttuk kēḷuṅkaḷ! nīṅkaḷ nīṭu vāḻka. uṅkaḷuḷ ovvoruvarum maṟṟavaruṭaṉ etirttu niṟkum vallamiyuṭiyavarkaḷ. āṉāl uṅkaḷ naṭpaṟā viṭṭāl, nīṅkaḷ intak kaṭal cūḻnta viya muḻutum veṉṟu koḷḷat taṭiyirātu. ākavē oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar nallavarāy, nērmiyuṭiya varāy iruṅkaḷ. uṅkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ vaḻi niṉṟu uṅkaḷip pirikkum tīyōr urikaḷukkuc cevicāyk kātiruppīrkaḷāka.”
“iṉṟirukkum ārvam kuṉṟāmal, uṅkaḷ naṭpu eṉṟum nīṭippatāka. pōrtoṟum pōrtoṟum uṅkaḷ vēlkaḷ veṟṟi kāṇpatāka. varippuliyiṉ koṭiyum, mīṉ koṭiyum uṅkaḷ etirikaḷ nilattiluḷḷa malitōṟum aliyāṭuva tāka.”
avaṉ īkippaṇpum, avaṉ aviyi aṇukiya kaviñar kaḷiṭam avaṉ kāṭṭiya vaḷḷaṉmiyum avaṉatu āṭkiyiṉ kaṭippakutiyil muṭattāmakkaṇṇiyārāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa porunarāṟṟuppaṭiyil naṉku viritturikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.17 āciriyarik karikālaṉ nallaṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟa ceytiyi virittu avar maṟṟōru kaviñariṭam akkarikālaṉic ceṉṟu kāṇumpaṭi kūṟuvatupōlap pāṭal tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ip paṇṭik kālattil tamiḻaracarkaḷ kaviñarkaḷiṭam koṇṭirunta naṭpiṇakkattik kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟa muṟiyil itu namakku iṟumpūtu taruvatākum. potu vācakarkaḷukkuc cuvipaṭāta pakutikaḷi maṭṭum viṭṭuviṭṭu, pāṭṭiṉ moḻipeyarpik kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ.18
“viḻāvayarum nakarantoṟum ceṉṟu, nakaril viḻā muṭintatum ati viṭṭuccellum pāṇaṉē!”
“uṉ maṉivi peṭimayilpōlum cāyaliṉaḷ. avaḷ kuḻal neytōyntu karumiyuṭiyavi. avaḷ nutal piṟimati ottatu. kūṟṟuvaṉ cilipōl vaḷinta puruvattiṉaṭiyilē meṉmi vāynta kaṇkaḷi uṭiyavaḷ avaḷ. avaḷ itaḻkaḷ ilaviṉ pūvitaḻkaḷ ottavi. avaḷ kural ici pōṉṟiṉiyatu. avaḷ paṟkaḷ muttuvaricipōṉṟavi. mayir kattirikkum kattiriyiṉ piṭipōṉṟu toṅkum kātaṇikaḷi aṇintavaḷ. nāṇattiṉāl caṟṟē vaḷinta kaḻuttiṉaḷ. uyar malikaḷiṉ       ucciyil malarum ceṅkāntaḷ pōṉṟa melviral kaḷi uṭiyavaḷ avaḷ...
“ittakiya uṉ maṉivi yāḻiṉ narampukaḷi meṉmi yākat toṭṭum, tivantum, virintu, payiṉṟum, iṉṉici pāṭa, nī tirunīr teḷittu vaḻipaṭṭu vaṉa teyvaṅkaḷip pūcittāy. nontu kaḷitta kāluṭaṉ naṭakkum vaḻippōk karukku niḻaliṉṟi ilitaḷiyaṟṟa maraṅkaḷi uṭiyatāy, kāṭṭu yāṉikaḷ tiriyum kāṭukaḷ vaḻiyākac cellum nī iṭiyūṟiṉṟic ceṉṟu mīḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu vēṇṭutal ceytāy.”
“vāḻi, pāṇarkaḷil ciṟanta pāṇaṉē vāḻi! uṉ naṟpēṟu uṉṉi iṅkē koṇṭuvantu viṭṭatu. iṉi ittakiya īyāk kaṭuvaḻikaḷil nī cellavēṇṭuvatilli. iṉi nī vāḻvil vaḷappaṭuvāy! ēḻu narampukaḷi iyakkum tiṟaluṭiyavaṉē! uṉṉiyum uṉ kuṭumpattiyum vāṭṭi vatakkiya vaṟumiyi nī ōṭṭi oḻikka virumpuvāyāṉāl, iṉṉē eḻuka!”
“paḻutta kaṉi niṟinta maraṅkaḷip paciyuṭaṉ tēṭum paṟaviyip pōla, nāṉ orunāḷ viṭiyaṟkaliyil ōr aracaṉ araṇmaṉi vāyilukku ceṉṟēṉ. vāyil kāppōriṉ iṇakkampeṟṟuc cuṇaṅkāmal nēraṭiyākavē uḷḷē nuḻintēṉ. naṭantu kaḷittu aluttuc ceṉṟapōtilum, uṭaṉē eṉ kaḷi tīrntatu. makiḻcci nirampiṟṟu. maṉṉaṉi nāṉ uṭaṉē aṇūkiṉēṉ. kiyiṟ koṇṭuceṉṟa ciṟu muraci iyakki, vaṇṇam pāṭiṉēṉ.”
“eṉṉi nīṇṭa nāḷ terintu paḻakkappaṭṭavaṉ pōla avvaracaṉ kaṉivuṭaṉ pēciṉāṉ. attakiya aracaṉ vāyilil ceṉṟu irappatukūṭa oru māṉak kēṭāṉa ceyalalla eṉṟu nāṉ uṇarntēṉ. avaṉ eṉṉit taṉ muṉṉē amarumpaṭi kūṟiṉāṉ. avvaḷavu karuṇiyuṭaṉ nōkkiṉāṉ. eṉ ākam muḻuvatum colla muṭiyāta kaḷippiṉāl pullarittatu.”
“viyarviyil naṉintu puḻuṅki, īrum pēṉum āṭci ceyta eṉ āṭikaḷi akaṟṟi, uyariya pūvēliyiṭṭa malmal āṭiyāl eṉṉip pōrttumpaṭi avaṉ uttara viṭṭāṉ. pūṇaṇinta puṉmuṟuval pūt mukattiṉarāṉa avaṉ paṇippeṇṭir poṟ kalaṅkaḷil veṟi yūṭṭum matuvi ūṟṟi ūṟṟit tara, nāṉ kuṭittuk kuṭittu eṉ kaḷi ayarccikaḷiyum tuyarkaḷiyum, muṟṟilum maṟantēṉ. paṭukkiyil cāyntu ōyvu koṇṭēṉ.”
“viḻittu eḻuntavuṭaṉ maṭṭaṟṟa kuṭiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa veṟi mayakka maṉṟi vēṟu entak kuṟiyum eṉakku illi eṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ. eṉ iṉpam elliyaṟṟatāyiruntatu. āṉāl atu uṇmiyeṉṟu nampa muṭiyavilli. kaṉavō eṉṟu tayaṅkiṉēṉ. āṉāl virivil atu muṟṟilum naṉavutāṉ eṉpatu eṉakku viḷaṅkiṟṟu.”
“eṉ māṇavarkaḷ avaṉ pukaḻ pāṭikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. atukēṭṭu avaṉ eṅkaḷi varavaḻittāṉ. nāṅkaḷ ceṉṟu attakiya taṟuvāykaḷukkēṟṟa vaḻakkappaṭi vaṇakkam terivittōm. avaṉ kampiyiliṭṭu vāṭṭappeṟṟa veḷḷāṭṭiṟicciyi eṅkaḷmuṉ vittāṉ. iṉi vayiṟu koḷḷa muṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟumvari eṅkaḷi avaṉ uṇpittāṉ. iv viruntayarum camayattilēyē aḻakiya āṭal naṅkiyarkaḷ yāḻ kiyil ēnti eṅkaḷmuṉ āṭalkaḷ nikaḻttiṉar.”
“palanāṭkaḷ viruntayarntu kuṭitta piṉṉum, iṉiya cōṟṟuṇavu aruntumpaṭi avaṉ eṅkaḷi vēṇṭiṉāṉ. pālil camitta cōṟuṭaṉ iṉiya pala paṇṭaṅkaḷum eṅkaḷ muṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. toṇṭi nirampumpaṭi nāṅkaḷ uṇṭōm.”
“immātiri pala nāṭkaḷ kaḻittapiṉ, nīṇṭa nāḷ kaṭunilam peyartta koḻumēḻipōla, eṅkaḷ paṟkaḷ tiṉṟu tiṉṟu maḻuṅkiṉa. uṇavum kuṭiyum eṅkaḷukku veṟuttuppēyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ oru nāḷ nāṉ aracaṉiṭam mellap pēcceṭuttēṉ.”
“etirikaḷiṭamiruntu tiṟipeṟum iṟivaṉē! eṉ ūrukkuc cella eṉakku viṭitara vēṇṭukiṟēṉ” eṉṟēṉ. eṉṉiṭam veṟuppuṟṟavaṉ pōṉṟa pārviyuṭaṉ eṉṉip pārttu, ivvaḷavu virivilā emmi viṭṭup pōkiṟīrkaḷ? eṉṟu avaṉ kēṭṭāṉ. atē camayam eṉakku avaṉ kaḷiṟukaḷum, piṭikaḷum, maṭanaṭip pōtakaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkiya yāṉit tiraḷkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. takutivāynta pariceṉa avaṟṟi nāṉ peṟṟu, vaṟumikku mīḷā viṭitantaṉuppiviṭṭu viṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭēṉ.
“variyā vaḷḷalākiya ivaṉ pōrtteyvamāṉa murukaṉ pōṉṟa veṅkaṭuñcīṟṟamuṭiya veṟṟi vēlēntiya uruvap paḵṟēr iḷiyōṉiṉ mintaṉāvāṉ. tāyiṉ karuviliruntē avaṉ aracirukkik kuriyavaṉākap piṟantāṉ. etirikaḷi yellām avaṉ taṉṉaṭipaṇintu ūḻiyam ceyyap paṇittavaṉ. avaṉuṭaṉ naṭappukkoḷḷāta vēntar nāṭukaḷilellām avaṉ tuṉpam paṭarvittāṉ.”
kaṭalakattilirunteḻum katiravaṉ uyara uyara oḷimikkup piṟaṅkuvatupōla, iḷamiyiliruntē vīrattilum valumiyilum vīṟumikkuyarntu, ippōtu kūṟṟuvaṉip pōla valimiyuṭi yavaṉāy, īṭeṭuppaṟṟa oru pēraracit tōḷkaḷil tāṅki yuḷḷāṉ. mutal vēṭṭiyilēyē valam poruntiya yāṉiyik kollum iḷañciṅka ēṟupōla, veṇṇil kaḷattil āṟṟappaṭṭa avaṉ kaṉṉippōrilē, avaṉ pāṇṭiya cēra aracarkaḷi vīḻttiṉāṉ.
“āttiyiṉ oṇpūmālikaḷi aṇinta pēraracaṉ karikālvaḷavaṉ ivaṉē.”
“perumi mikka ivvaracaṉi aṇūki nī avaṉ aṭi vaṇaṅkiṉāyāṉāl, avaṉ putitu piṟanta kaṉṟi nōkkum āveṉa uṉṉi nōkkuvāṉ. uṉ pāṭṭukkaḷik kēṭpataṟku muṉṉāl, avaṉ uṉakkup paṭṭāṭi aṇivittup poṟkalaṅkaḷil matu aḷippāṉ, nī taṅku nāḷaḷavum avaṉ uṉṉi viruntayarvittu, viṭi koḷḷum camayam uṉ mayir muṭimītu poṟṟāmarikaḷic cūṭṭuvatuṭaṉ, uṉ maṉivikkup poṉ mālikaḷum, muttu mālikaḷum, paricaḷippāṉ. cempiṭari mayir vāynta pālveḷḷik kutirikaḷ nāṉku iḻuttuc cellum tantattālāṉa mukaṭukaḷi yuṭiya tērkaḷiyum, pala yāṉikaḷiyum, nīrvaḷamuṭiya ūrkaḷiyum uṉakkup paricaḷippāṉ.”
“peṟṟa paricilkaḷi uṉ uḻiyarkaḷukkup pakirin taḷittu viṭṭu nī vīṭu cella virivāyāṉāl, appōtum ivvulakil nilavum etuvum nillā eṉpatuṇarntum uṉṉi viṭṭuppiriya maṉamillātavaṉāy, kaṉivuṭaṉ uṉṉit toṭarntu ēḻaṭi naṭantu, piṉ uṉṉit tērēṟṟi uṉakku viṭikoṭut taṉuppuvāṉ.”
“cōḻa nāṭṭil nīṇṭa nāḷ āṭci ceytuvarum maṉṉaṉ ittakiyavaṉ. iḷiyōṉā yirukkumpōtē mutiyōr maṉam niṟivuṟa nīti vaḻaṅkiyavaṉ avaṉ. kuṭikaḷiṭam aṉpum aruḷum niṟinta kuṟṟamaṟṟa avaṉ nīṇṭa āṭci ulakeṅkum pukaḻ parappiya oṉṟu. avaṉ nīṭu vāḻka!”
“neṟpōriṭiyē tēṉīkkaḷ kūṭu kaṭṭi vāḻum vaḷamuṭiya puṉal nāṭṭu vēntaṉ karikālaṉ kālaṭikaḷ mūvulakum vella uyarntavi. āṉāl antō, atu inta oru ulakattit tāṉ tāṇṭa muṭintatu; ataṟkuḷ tīp paṭṭuviṭṭatu.”
uṟiyūr maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār cōṇāṭṭu ericcalūr māṭalaṉ, maturikkumaraṉ ākiya kaviñarkaḷ immaṉṉaṉip pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷum ‘puṟanāṉūṟu’ eṉṟa peyariya paṇṭik kaviti nūlil uḷḷaṉa.19
karikāla cōḻaṉ makaḷ naṟcōṇi cēra aracaṉ iraṇṭām ātaṉukku maṇam ceyvikkappaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ ivvāṟu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cēraṉukkum, cilappatikāra āciriyar iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷukkum tāy āṉāḷ.20
karikālaṉ maṟiviṉpōtu uṭaṉirunta karuṅkuḻalātaṉār tam tuyarattik kīḻvarum pāṭalkaḷil kuṟikkiṉṟār:-
“etirikaḷ kōṭṭikaḷi añcātu muṟṟuki ceytavaṉ; pāṇarkaḷukkum avarkaḷ kuṭikaḷukkum viruntaḷittu, elliyaṟṟa matu āṟu perukki avarkaḷi ūṭṭiyavaṉ; kaṭami terinta kurukkaḷ vaḻi niṉṟu, peruntaṉmi mikka naṟkuṇa araciyal tuṇitara, tirumaṟi viṉimuṟi vaḻātu matil cūḻnta vēḷvic cālikaḷilē vēḷvikaḷ āṟṟi, avvēḷvikkaḷattiṉ neṭiya tūṇkaḷmītu kaḻukukaḷ kuḻumumpaṭi ceytavaṉ.”
“ittakiya aṟivum tiṟaṉum vāynta pēraracaṉ, antō, akaṉṟāṉ. avaṉi iḻantu ulakam veṟitākiyuḷḷatu. vēṉiliṉ kōṭiyilē, kāl naṭikaḷi aruntuvataṟkāka āyarkaḷāl taḻi kuṟikkappaṭṭa vēṅki marak kiḷikaḷip pōla, aṇiyiḻantu avaṉ aḻakiya mātaraciyar niṟkiṉṟaṉar.21”
karikālaṉi aṭuttu ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 95-il nalaṅkiḷḷi aracaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ kalittiṟam ceṟinta nalliṇakkamuṭiya aracaṉ. ataṉālēyē ‘nalaṅkiḷḷi’ allatu nalla kiḷḷi eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ muṉṉōṉ nāṭṭic celva vaḷamōṅkiya niliyilē viṭṭuc ceṉṟataṉāl, amitiyum oḻuṅkum kāppatu tavira avaṉukku vēṟu tēviyillā tiruntatu. āyiṉum avaṉ ayalaracukaḷit tākkavum taṉ āṭciyi virivupaṭuttavum āvaluṭiyavaṉāy iruntāṉ.
avaṉuṭaya pōrp paṭi eḻuccikaḷuḷ oṉṟil avaṉ paṭiyuṭaṉ pāḷiyamiṭṭirukkum camayattil kōvūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavar avaṉiṭam ceṉṟu ivvāṟu pāṭiṉār :22
“aṟattiṉ piṉṉē poruḷum, iṉpamum, celvatu pōla, uṉ māṟṟār iruvar (pāṇṭiyaṉ, cēraṉ) kuṭikaḷum muḻunilā veṉat tuḷaṅkum uṉ kuṭiyiṉ piṉ varukiṉṟaṉa. pukaḻavāvuṭiya nī uṉ paṭivīṭṭil taṅkuvatu tavira vēṟu eṅkē cellamuṭiyum?” 
“etirikaḷiṉ kōṭṭimatilkaḷit tākki muṉi maḻuṅkiya tantaṅkaḷiyuṭiya uṉ yāṉikaḷ veṟitē cōmpiyirukka muṭiyāmal paṭapaṭakkiṉṟaṉa. kālil vīrak kaḻalkaḷaṇinta uṉ paṭivīrarkaḷ akaṉṟaṭarnta kāṭukaḷik kaṭantēṉum etirikaḷ nāṭṭil nuḻiya irukkiṉṟaṉar. uṉ pōrkkutirikaḷ kīḻkaṭaliliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mēl kaṭalil taṅkaḷ kuḷampukaḷi alampumvari niṟpatilli. itaṉāl tuṇakkuṟṟu naṭuṅki nī tampakkam paṭi naṭattiviṭak kūṭumeṉṟu añci, vaṭapula aracarkaḷ kaṇṇimiyātu viḻittiruntu (tam ellikaḷik) kākkiṉṟaṉar.”
avaṉ talinakariliruntu aṭikkaṭi veḷiyē ceṉṟu taṅkiyiruntataṉāl, maṉṉar kuṭiyiṉ iḷiyōrmītu avaṉ āṟṟal taḷarntiruntatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. neṭuṅkiḷḷi eṉṟa cōḻa iḷavaracaṉ avaṉukketirākak kiḷarnteḻumpaṭi tūṇṭap paṭṭāṉ. talinakaramākiya uṟiyūṟi avaṉ kippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ.
ik kiḷarcci paṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟa aracaṉ uṟiyūrukku virintu ceṉṟu ati muṟṟukiviṭṭāṉ. muṟṟukiyiṉ pōtu, iḷantattaṉ eṉṟa oru pāṭaṟ pulavaṉ uṟiyūr ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉi ōr oṟṟaṉ eṉṟu aiyuṟṟu, neṭuṅkiḷḷi avaṉik kippaṟṟik koli ceyya muṉintiruntāṉ. accamayam kōvūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavar avaṉiṭam ceṉṟu iḷantattaṉ uyirukku maṉṟāṭi avaṉik kāttār.
ittaṟuvāyil kōvūrkiḻār neṭuṅkiḷḷiyi nōkkip pāṭiya pāṭal varumāṟu :23
“pulavarkaḷ paṟavikaḷippōla eṅkum paṟappavarkaḷ. puravalarkaḷi nāṭik kāṭukaḷik kaṭappavarkaḷ. nāvaṟinta aḷavu avarkaḷip pukaḻpavarkaḷ. peṟṟa paricil koṇṭu makiḻntu. tam uḻiyarkaḷuṭaṉ viruntu ayarpavarkaḷ. mīttuvitta laṟiyātu uṇpavarkaḷ. koṭuttalaṉṟip pēṇal aṟiyātavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ avāvuvatu pukaḻaṉṟi vēṟilli.”
“piṟar virumpi aḷippataṉṟi vāḻvukku vēṟu vakiyaṟṟa iṉam pāṇar iṉam. ittakiyōr eppōtāvatu piṟarukkut tīṅku ceyya eṇṇūvārkaḷā? orupōtum māṭṭārkaḷ. tampōṉṟa peru maṉṉarkaḷip puravalaṉākappeṟṟa kāraṇattāl, maṟṟap pāṭakarkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu avarkaḷ taliyiṟaṅkak kaṇṭu tarukkuṭaṉ naṭappavarkaḷ avarkaḷ.”
uṟiyūriliruntu kiḷarcci ceyta iḷavaracaṉ cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ maṟṟōr araṇvāynta nakarāṉa āvūrukku ōṭiṉāṉ. nalaṅkiḷḷi avaṉi aṅkum piṉtoṭarntu āvūriyum muṟṟuki yiṭṭāṉ. muṟṟuki ellikaṭantu mikavum nīṭittataṉāl, nakaril vāḻvōr uṇaviṉṟip paṭṭiṉik kuḷḷāṉārkaḷ. āvūr makkaḷum paṭi vīrarum aṭinta iṉṉalkaḷ kīḻvarum pāṭalil viritturikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.24
“kaḷiṟukaḷ tattam piṭikaḷuṭaṉē kōṭṭikku veḷiyē yuḷḷa ērikaḷil nīrāṭuvataṟku neṭunāḷ iṭṭuc cellappaṭā tirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaḻakkamāka avaṟṟiṟku ūṭṭappaṭum veṇṇey kalanta cōṟṟukkavaḷamum ūṭṭappaṭātirukkiṉṟaṉa. inniliyil avi tām caṅkiliyāl piṇippuṇṭa kaṭṭut taṟikaḷiṉmītu cīṟukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantikaḷ pālillāmal aḻukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ malariṉṟik kūntal muṭikkiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ kuṭikka nīriṉṟi alamarukiṉṟaṉar.”
“ipṭiyē iṉṉum iṅkē nīṭittiruppatu pēriṭar taruvatu. virinaṭik kutirikaḷiyuṭiya vēntaṉē! nī takkapaṭi naṭappatāṉāl vāyilkaḷit tiṟantu um maṉṉaṉiṭam kōṭṭiyi oppaṭikka! allatu pōr ceyyavē karutiṉāyāṉāl. pōrvīrarkaḷi naṭāttic ceṉṟu etiriyuṭaṉ pōriṭuka! iraṇṭum ceyyāmal kōṭṭiyiṉ valimi vāynta katavukaḷi aṭittukkoṇṭu uyar matilkaḷukkup piṉ patuṅkiyiruppatu māṉakkēṭāṉa ceyalēyākum.”
maṉṉaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭiyāmaliruppatupaṟṟik kaviñar neṭuṅkiḷḷiyi iṭitturittataṉ payaṉāka, kiḷarcci ceyta iḷavaracaṉiyum maṉṉaṉiyum iṇakkuvippatil veṟṟi kaṇṭār. im muyaṟciyil avar kūṟiya pāṭal oṉṟu varumāṟu.25
“avaṉ paṉiyiṉ veṇpū aṇintavaṉallaṉ. kariya vēmpiṉ pūvum aṇintavaṉallaṉ. nī ātti māli cūṭiyuḷḷāy. avaṉu m āttimāliyē cūṭi varukiṟāṉ. uṅkaḷil evar tōṟṟālum, tōṟpatu uṅkaḷ cōḻar kuṭiyē. nīṅkaḷ iruvarum veṟṟipeṟuvīr eṉpatum muṭiyāta oṉṟu. uṅkaḷiruvariṭiyē pōr nikaḻvatu uṅkaḷ paḻaṅkuṭikku aḻivu taruvatu. antō, uṅkaḷ pakivar makiḻumpaṭi naṭipeṟum ivvuḷ vīṭṭuk kalakam evvaḷavu koṭiyatu!”
an nāṭkaḷil ovvoru aracaṉum paṭit talivaṉum pōrkkaḷattil vaḻakkamāka ovvoruvaki māliyic cūṭiṉar. itaṉmūlam etirikaḷiṭamiruntu avarkaḷ tammi vēṟu pirittuk kāṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ vēmpiṉ malarkaḷi vēyntukoṇṭāṉ. cēraṉ paṉimalariyum cōḻaṉ ātti malariyum aṇintāṉ. mēlē tarappaṭṭa pāṭṭil, cōḻaṉuṭaṉ pōriṭuvatu pāṇṭiyaṉumalla, cēraṉumalla eṉpatik kaviñar ivvāṟu viḷakkiyirukkiṟār. iruvarum ātti māliyē aṇintiruppataṉāl, iruvarum cōḻarēyāyiṉar.
uḷnāṭṭup pōrkaḷāl alippuṇṭu, nalaṅkiḷḷi āṭciyil cōḻa aracu celva vaḷamikkatāy amiyavilli. veṟṟikaramāṉa āṭciyāḷaṉāka viḷaṅkuvataṟkuriya takutikaḷil cila avaṉiṭam illātiruntaṉa. avaṉ naṟkuṇamuṭiyavaṉē yāṉālum, taṉ āṭci vaḷam paṟṟik taṟperumi koṇṭi runtāṉ. avaṉē pāṭiya kīḻvarum ippāṭal kāṭṭukiṟatu.26
“mellavantu eṉṉaṭi taḻuvi oruvaṉ kuṟiyirantu kēṭpatāṉāl, nāṉ ip paḻam pēraracikkūṭak koṭupnṉ. atumaṭṭumō? eṉ uyirikkūṭa avaṉukkāka iṭarppaṭuttuvēṉ. āṉāl, eṉ vallamiyik kuṟivāka matittu, aṟiviḻantu eṉ taṉṉāṇmiyi etirttālō, tūṅkum pulimītu iṭaṟiviḻunta mūrkkaṉ pōla, avaṉ uyiriḻantu pōvāṉ. pāriya yāṉi iḷa mūṅkil taḷirik kālaṭiyiliṭṭut tuvittalpōla, attaki yōrit tākki aḻikkāviṭṭāl, nāṉ nallōr nāṭātu nīṇṭa kūntali ūṭiya potu makaḷiri aṇintēṉākuka.”
avaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil araṇaminta ēḻu nakaraṅkaḷik kippaṟṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. araciṉ elliyi virivupaṭutta vēṟecceyalum avaṉ ceytatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli. tolli nirampiya kuṟukiya kāla āṭcikkupiṉ avaṉ iṟantatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. aracavikku varuvōriṭam aṉpiṇakkam kāṭṭumpaṭi avaṉukku mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttāṉ aṟivuṟuttiya kīḻvarum pāṭṭuc cuvikaramāṉatu.27
“nūṟitaḻkaḷiyuṭiya kaṟiyaṟṟa tāmari malarip pōla, ivvulakiyāṇṭa peruṅkuṭiyil piṟantavarkaḷik kūrntu kavaṉittuppār. avarkaḷil mikac cilarē pāṭṭilum, katiyilum pukaḻ parappiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tāmariyiṉ ularnta iliccarukukaḷ pōla avarkaḷ pukaḻiṉṟi vīḻntuḷḷārkaḷ. āṉāl kaviñar pāṭalkaḷukku ilakkāṉa ceyalkaḷ ceyta periyōrkaḷō, mēlulakaṅkaḷil vāṉa vūrtikaḷil cellukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm.”
“aṇṇal cēṭceṉṉi nalaṅkiḷḷiyē! etuvum māṟimāṟi vāḻntum maṭintumē varuvatik kāṇpāy. ellām aḻintu mīṇṭum piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. kallātavarkaḷ kūṭat taṇmatiyattip pārttu iti aṟintukoḷḷalām. atu yāvarum kāṇa (vaḷarntu tēyntu) māṟi varukiṟatu.”
“ākavē, uṉ tayavi nāṭivaruvōr evarāyiṉum, avar valintōrāyiṉumcari, melintōrāyiṉumcari, ellāriṭamum aṉpiṇakkamuḷḷavaṉāyiru. piṟarukku eṉṟum utavāmal taṉakkeṉa vāḻum kayavarkaḷ uṉakku etirikaḷāyirukkaṭṭum.”
nalakkiḷḷi maṟivuṟṟapiṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ cōḻa aracirukki ēṟiṉāṉ. āyiṉum avaṉ urimi cōḻar kuṭikkuriya maṟṟa iḷavaracarkaḷiṉ etirppukkāḷāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷil oṉpatiṉ marukkuk kuṟiyātavarkaḷ kiḷarnteḻuntu nāṭṭit tuṇṭāṭa viruntaṉar. āṉāl kiḷḷivaḷavaṉiṉ mittuṉaṉāṉa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉcēraṉ oṉpatiṉmariyum orucēra nērivāyilil muṟiyaṭittuk kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ āṭciyurimiyi nili nāṭṭiṉāṉ.28
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ itaṉpiṉ malāṭṭut talivaṉ maliya māṉit tākkiṉāṉ. im maṉṉaṉi etirtta oṉpatu iḷavaracar kaḷukku avaṉ ātaravu tantiruntatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. ik kāraṇattāl kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ avaṉ āṟṟali aṭakka muṭivu ceytāṉ. avaṉ maliyamāṉi muṟiyaṭittukkoṉṟu, iḷa vayatiṉarāṉa avaṉ ciṟuvarkaḷik kippaṟṟi avarkaḷi yāṉik kālaṭiyiliṭṭuttuvittu aḻikka muṟpaṭṭāṉ.
acciṟuvar cārpil kōvūrkiḻār taliyiṭṭu aracaṉuṭaṉ ivvāṟu vātāṭiṉār.
“iṭukkaṭpaṭṭōrik kāttup puṟāvukkut taṇṇaḷi ceytavaṉ marapil vantavaṉ nī! intac ciṟuvar pulavarkaḷi ātirittup pukaḻpeṟṟa kuṭiyil vantavarkaḷ. cīṟiya yāṉiyik kaṇṭu añciya avarkaḷ, ippōtu maṉṉaviyik kaṇṭu maruḷukiṉṟaṉar. eṉ coṟkaḷukkuc cevi cāyttapiṉ, uṉakku etu viruppamō atu ceyka!”29
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttu maturivari ceṉṟāṉ. āṉāl, pāṇṭiyap paṭiyiṉ talivaṉāṉa paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉāl annakar matil puṟattē muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.30
taṉ mittuṉaṉāṉa cēraṉceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ maṟivuṟṟapiṉ, cēra nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyāyirunta kōṅku nāṭṭukkuḷ avaṉ paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. cērar paṭikaḷiṉ etirppu mikavum valimi yaṟṟatāyiruntataṉāl, cōḻa aracaṉ taṉ paṭikaḷic cērar talinakarāṉa vañcimānakariṉ vāyilukkē koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. cēra aracaṉ kōṭṭikku veḷiyilvaravē tuṇiyavilli. itaṉāl nakar matilukku veḷiyilirunta kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ, vayalkaḷ tōṭṭaṅkaḷik kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ aḻittāṉ.
cōḻaṉ paṭiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟirunta cila kaviñarkaḷ aracaṉi nōkkip pāṭiya cila pāṭalkaḷ varumāṟu:- 
“veṟṟiyuṭiya vēliyum vīramikka paṭiyiyum uṭiya aracē! peruntaṉmiyuṭaṉ puṟāvik kātta cempiyaṉ marapiṉaṉē!”
“malimēluḷḷa maraṅkaḷellām kaṭṭerittuc cāmpa lākumpaṭi viḻum iṭiyēṟupōla, nī ik kōṭṭikku veḷiyē nallaṉa yāvaṟṟiyum aḻikkiṟāy.”
“maliyiṉaṭiyil kukiyiṉuḷḷē oḷintu aintaliyuṭiya naccup pāmpu uṟivatupōla, pittaḷi vēynta vella muṭiyāta kōṭṭikkuḷ pala yāṉikaḷiyuṭiya vēntaṉ patuṅkiyirukkiṟāṉ.”31
maṟṟoru pāṭṭu varumāṟu:-
“oru puṟāvik kāppaṟṟa eṇṇit taṉṉiyē niṟi kōliṉ taṭṭukaḷil ēṟṟi eṭiyiṭṭavaṉ marapil vantavaṉē! ettakiya aṉpaṟac ceyalkaḷi nī ceytālum avaṟṟāl uṉ pukaḻmarapi nī mikutippaṭutta muṭiyātu.”
“uṉ pukaḻmikka muṉṉōrkaḷi eṇṇumpōtu, avarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ vāṉakattil ilaṅkiya viyattaku māyak kōṭṭikaḷiyē etirttaḻittāṉ! nī eṉṉa veṟṟi peṟṟālum uṉ veṟṟi marapukku eṉṉa muṉṉēṟṟamum ēṟpaṭātu.”
“vēkamikka pōrkkutirikaḷiyuṭiya vaḷavaṉē! ātti māli aṇintavaṉē! etirikaḷit tākkuvatil irumpupōṉṟa karam uṭiyavaṉē! poṉ aḷikkum uyar imaya malimītu taṉ viṟkoṭi poṟitta periya aḻakiya tēriyuṭiya vāṉavaṉi vella vañci muṟṟukiyilnī muṉintuḷḷāy. uṉ vallamiyi nāṉ evvāṟu pāṭuvēṉ?”32
maṟṟum oru pulavaratu pāṭal varumāṟu:-
“nī koṉṟaḻippāyō! kāppāṟṟuvāyō! etu nallateṉpati nīyē aṟivāy.”
“poṉṉiṟak kaḻaṟkāykaḷuṭaṉ vaḷiyum cilampu maṇinta iḷamakaḷir viḷiyāṭum maṇal mikunta porunik kariyilē,  kollaṉ vāḷarattāl arāvik kūrākkappaṭṭa nīṇṭa piṭiyuṭiya kōṭalikaḷ matiṟpuṟattē maraṅkaḷi veṭṭic cāykkum ōci kōṭṭikkuḷ araṇmaṉiyilēyē etirolittuk koṇṭi runtum (kōḻiyippōlat), taṉ talinakarattukkuḷ aṭipaṭṭuk kiṭakkum ōr aracaṉuṭaṉ pōrpurivataṟku (uṉ paṭicūḻa), māliyaṇinta muracu muḻaṅka nī celvatu eṉpatu māṉakkēṭāṉa ceyalōkum.”33
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ mutalil ilaṅkiyil mēlkarip pakutiyil āṭci ceyta nācu aracaṉ vālivaṉaṉ makaḷākiya pīlivaḷiyi maṇantukoṇṭāṉ34. itaṉiyaṭuttu avaṉ taṉ maṉiviyāka, tirumālē vāmaṉaṉākap piṟantu vella muṉinta purāṇa aracaṉāṉa māvaliyiṉ marapil piṟantavaṉāka urimi kōriya (perumpālum mahiṣamaṇṭalam allatu micūrukku uriyavaṉāy irukkak kūṭiya) ōr aracaṉ makaḷāṉa cītattakkiyi ēṟṟāṉ35. cītattakki mūlam avaṉukku utayakumāraṉ eṉṟu oru makaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ aḻakum, pukaḻum, vāynta iḷiñaṉā yiruntāṉ. āṉāl, ōriravu taṟceyalāka avaṉ kolik kāḷāṉāṉ.36
itaṉpiṉ aracaṉ nāka iḷavaracimūlam piṟanta taṉ maṟṟa makaṉukkuc colliyaṉuppiyatākat terikiṟatu. avaḷ taṉ makaṉi maṇipallavattiliruntu kāvirippaṭṭiṉam vanta oru vaṇikak kappalil aṉuppivittāḷ. vaḻiyil, iruṇṭu puyalārnta ōr iravil, kappal celvōr oru tīvil iṟaṅkiṉārkaḷ. iḷavaracaṉi aṅkēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu, puyalamintatum kappalil ceṉṟu viṭṭārkaḷ. iḷavaracaṉ illātatu kaṇṭatum, avarkaḷ tirumpic ceṉṟu, attīviyum aṇimik kaṭaṟkariyiyum turuvi eṅkum tēṭiṉārkaḷ. etuvum payaṉillātupōyiṟṟu.
iḷavaracaṉ kāṇamaṟpōṉa tuyaramikka ceyti intira viḻā toṭaṅkappaṭum camayattilēyē kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku eṭṭiṟṟu. aracaṉ viḻāvik kavaṉikkātu viṭṭu viṭṭu makaṉit tēṭap puṟappaṭṭāṉ.
kaṭaliṉ oru pērali iccamayam kāvirippaṭṭiṉattiṉ mītu paravi annakari aḻittatu.
annāḷiya mata nampikiyuṭiya makkaḷāl, vāṉavar vēntaṉāṉa intiraṉuṭiya viḻāvi naṭattāta kuṟṟattiṟkāka makkaḷit taṇṭikkavē, kaṭal teyvamākiya maṇimēkali anta aliyi aṉuppiṉāḷ eṉṟu nampappaṭṭatu.37
itaṉiyaṭuttuc cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ, kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ kuḷamuṟṟam eṉṉum iṭattil uyir nīttāṉ. avaṉ uyir nīppiṉpōtu uṭaṉirunta kaviñarkaḷuḷ oruvar appōtu tamakku ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarccikaḷik kīḻvarum pāṭalil tantuḷḷār.38
“valimi vāynta tērkaḷiyum veṟṟip paṭikaḷiyum uṭiya vaḷavaṉ uyirik kavaravanta kūṟṟuvaṉ maṉṉaṉik kippaṟṟum tōṟṟattuṭaṉō āṭkoṇṭu cellum pāvaṉi yuṭaṉō vantiruntāl, am maṉṉaṉ cīṟṟattukkut tappiyirukka māṭṭāṉ. orupōtum avvāṟirātu. avaṉ pukaḻpāṭum pāṇaṉpōla ēntiya kiyuṭaṉ avaṉ uyiri irantu vēṇṭiyē vantirukkavēṇṭum.”
kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉukkuppiṉ āṇṭavaṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa maṟṟoru kiḷḷi āvāṉ. avaṉ pāriya vēḷvi naṭattiṉāṉ. ap peruviḻāviṟku avaṉ aṇṭiyayal aracarkaḷāṉa ukkirapperuvaḻutiyiyum cēra aracaṉ māveṇkō allatu vaṉappu vāynta aracaṉiyum aḻittāṉ. av viḻāvil kalantukoṇṭa avviyār oruṅkē vīṟṟirunta mūvaracarkaḷiyum nōkkik kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭiṉāṉ :- 39
“vēntarkaḷē! koṭiyaminta tērum veṇkoṟṟak kuṭisayam vāykkappeṟṟa kōmāṉkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ iṉṟupōl eṉṟum ēntiya kiyarāṉa pārppaṉarukku akkikaḷil poṟ pūkkaḷi nīruṭaṉ aḷittu, pūṇaṇintu, polivuṟṟa uṅkaḷ paṇippeṇṭir poṟkālattil ēntiyaḷitta iṉiya matuvuṇṭu uṅkaḷip pukaḻum pāvalarukku viliyēṟiya paricilkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvīrkaḷāka!”
“ivvulaka vāḻvi viṭṭakalumpōtu nīṅkaḷ ippōtu ceyyum aṟaviṉi maṭṭumē uṅkaḷukkut tuṇiniṟkum.”
“maṇṇurimiyuṭaṉ nīṅkaḷ mūvarum iṅkē oruṅkirukkum kāṭci irupiṟappāḷar ōyāviḻippuṭaṉ pakalumiravum pēṇum tiruvārnta muttīyiṉ kāṭcipōṉṟatu.”
“nīṅkaḷ nalamuṭaṉ vāḻvīrāka! vāṉattu mīṉkaḷ pōlavum maḻi nīrttuḷikaḷ pōlavum eṇṇaṟṟa palavāṉa nāṭkaḷ nīṅkaḷ vāḻvīrāka!”
pāṇṭiyarkula marapukkoṭi

6 pāṇṭiyar
māṇpukaḻk karikāla cōḻaṉuṭaṉ camakālattavaṉāṉa pāṇṭiya aracaṉ āriyappaṭikaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ. atāvatu āriyariṉ paṭikaḷiveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvāṉ.1
tekkaṇattiluḷḷa āriya aracarkaḷuḷ ētō oruvaṉ avaṉ āṭciyiṉpōtu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭutta tākat terikiṟatu. pāṇṭiya aracaṉ avarkaḷmītu kuṟippiṭṭat takka toru peruveṟṟipeṟṟu avarkaḷit tiruppit turattiṉāṉ. itaṉāl avaṉukku āriyappaṭi kaṭantavaṉ eṉṟa pukaḻppeyar kiṭṭiṟṟu.
ip peruveṟṟi paṟṟiya vēṟu vivaram etuvum ippōtu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. āyiṉum cilappatikāram eṉṟa peruṅkāppiyattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa putumi vāynta avaṉ muṭivu mūlam avaṉ niṉivu piṉ marapiṉariṭiyē nīṭittuḷḷatu.2
araciyiṉ cilampukaḷil oṉṟit tiruṭiyavaṉ eṉṟa aiyuṟaviṉ mītu, kāvirippaṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa vaṇikaṉāṉa kōvalaṉik koliceyyumpaṭi avaṉ araṇmaṉik kāvalarkaḷukku uttaraviṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl av vaṇimaṇi araciyiṉuṭiyataṉṟu eṉpati avvaṇikaṉ maṉivi kaṇṇaki maṉṉaṉ uḷaṅkoḷḷa meyppittu viṭṭāḷ.
kaṇṇaki taṉ tāyakattuc cōḻa maṉṉar marapik kuṟippiṭṭu, avarkaḷil oruvaṉ oru kaḻukiṉ avā niṟivēṟṟi oru puṟāvik kāppāṟṟat taṉ taciyiyē aṟuttut tantatiyum, tēri ōr āṉ kaṉṟiṉmītu celutti ataṉ uyiri māytta kuṟṟattukkāka maṟṟoruvaṉ taṉ makaṉiyē koṉṟatiyum eṭuttu iṭitturittiruntāḷ. māṉamum veṭkamum mīkkūra maṉṉaṉ uṇarviḻantu taviciliruntu kīḻē viḻuntāṉ. viḻuntavaṉ mīṇṭum eḻavēyilli. avaṉ araciyum avaṉ citiyēṟi uyir nīttāḷ.
iv varacaṉ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷil oṉṟu kalviyiṉ nalaṅkaḷip pukaḻntu, tam piḷḷikaḷik kalvi payiṟṟuvikku māṟu kuṭikaḷi valiyuṟutti vēṇṭukiṉṟatu. punāṉūṟṟil iṭampeṟṟu namakkuvanteṭṭiyuḷḷa ap pāṭal varumāṟu:- 3
“vēṇṭutal aṟintu āciriyarukku utavuka. avarukku niṟiyapporuḷ taruka, avari vāymiyuṭaṉ piṉpaṟṟik kalvipeṟuka, taṉ piḷḷikaḷuḷḷēyē kallātavaṉit tāy virumpamāṭṭāḷ. kuṭumpattil piṟantavaruḷ mutaṉmi mūttava ṉukkalla. aṟiviṉ mikkavaṉi aracaṉum virumpi aḻittu aṟivuri kōruvāṉ. nālāya vakuppukkaḷuḷ, iḻinilippaṭṭa oruvaṉ kaṟṟavaṉāṉāl, uyarvuṭiyavaṉ kūṭa avaṉiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉvaḻi niṟpāṉ.”
neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ tiṭīr muṭivukēṭṭu koṟkiyil maṇṭalat talivaṉāy amarntiruntu āṇṭa pāṇṭiya iḷavaracaṉ veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉ maturikku virintu vantu aracirukki ēṟiṉāṉ.4
mikac curuṅkiyakāla āṭcikkuppiṉ avaṉ iṟantu, avaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ ciṟuvaṉāyirukkumpōtē aracaṉāṉatākat terikiṟatu.
avaṉ paṭṭattukku vantavuṭaṉē, nāṭu cōḻarkaḷāl paṭiyeṭukkappaṭṭatu. cōḻar paṭi maturivari muṉṉēṟiṟṟu, āyiṉum pāṇṭiyap paṭittalivaṉāṉa paḻiyaṉmāṟaṉ oru mutaltaramāṉa veṟṟiyi aṭintu, paṭiyeṭuttavarkaḷit taṅkaḷ nāṭṭukkē ōṭṭiṉāṉ.
iḷiyōṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ ip pōr nikaḻcciyil uṭaṉiruntatākat terikiṟatu. paṭiyeṭuppip paṟṟik kēḷvip paṭṭapōtu, taṉ nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttavarkaḷit turattu vatākac cūḷ eṭuttukkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. akkālat tamiḻaracar palarip pōlavē, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟuvatip poḻutu pōkkākak koṇṭiruntavaṉ ivviḷamaṉṉaṉ. iccamayam avaṉ ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟiya cūḷuri kīḻvarumāṟu:-5
“eṉ māṟṟārākiya aracarkaḷ tammiṭam paṭikkala mēntiya vīrarkaḷum tērkaḷum pōrkkutirikaḷum tūṇkaḷi otta kālkaḷōṭu oli etiroliyuṭiya periya maṇikaḷ tūṅkum kaḻuttiyuṭiya pāri yāṉikaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu tarukki, iḷiyōṉ eṉṟu eṉṉi eḷimippaṭṭavaṉākak karuti, accuṟuttalum avamatippum kalanta coṟkaḷ vīci, eṉṉi etirttup paṭiyeṭukkat tuṇintuḷḷaṉar.”
“kaḷattil mōtukiṟa pōril nāṉ avarkaḷi muṟiyaṭittu, avarkaḷ pōrmuracaṅkaḷuṭaṉē avarkaḷic ciṟikoḷḷēṉāyiṉ, tammik kākkum āṟṟalaṟṟu etirikaḷiṭam cikkiviṭṭa koṭuṅkōlaṉ eṉṟu eṉkuṭikaḷ eṉṉit tūṟṟaṭṭum! kaliyum aṟivum nirampiya māṅkuṭi marutaṉit talivaṉākak koṇṭa ulakam pukaḻum kaviñar pāṭalukku nāṉ takutiyaṟṟava ṉākaṭṭum! ēḻiyarukkō, eṉ ātaravu nāṭupavarukkō illi eṉṟu kūṟattakka iḻiniliyi nāṉ aṭiyaṭṭum!”
pāṇṭiyar paṭiyuṭaṉ iccamayattilirunta kaviñar iṭikkuṉṟūrkiḻār pāṇṭiyaṉ it taṟuvāyil koṇṭirunta tōṟṟattik kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭukiṟār.6
“taṉ paḻimiyāṉa nakarukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa ēriyil kuḷittu vēmpiṉ oḷimikka taḻi māliyit taliyilaṇintu, vīramikka ceḻiyaṉ taṉ muṉṉē muracamuḻaṅka yāṉimītēṟip pōrkkaḷattukku vantuḷḷāṉ. avaṉetirē aṇivakuttu niṟuttappaṭṭa paṭivīrarō eṇṇilar.”
“antō! iṉṟiya vempōril ettaṉi pēr māḷātu piḻittirukkappōkiṟārkaḷō?”
“kiṇkiṇi aṇinta avaṉ kālkaḷil ippōtu vīrak kaḻalkaḷ oḷitarukiṉṟaṉa. vaḷamikka cikiyaṇiyirunta neṟṟimītu uḻiñit taḻiyuṭaṉ kalanta oḷimikka vēppantaḻikaḷ miṭiyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aḻakiya kāppuk kaḷaṇinta kikaḷil ippōtu valimi vāynta vil ēṟiyuḷḷatu. taṉicciṟappuṭaṉ ivvāṟu kāṭciyaḷikkum ivviḷiñaṉ yār?”
“vāḻka ivaṉ! mārpil malarmāli kāṇappaṭṭālum, ciṟuvaṉāka iruntapōtu aṇinta poṉmali iṉṉum kaḻaṟṟappaṭavilli. iṉṟutāṉ pālillā uṇavu avaṉukkup parimāṟappaṭṭatu.”
“etiriyiṉ paṭikaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka vantu tākkuvatik kaṇṭu avaṉ aciyavilli. avarkaḷikkaṇṭu avaṉ añca villi; ēḷaṉam ceyyavumilli. taṉ paṭivīrar avarkaḷit tākki aḻittu, avarkaḷ kataṟali vāṉam etirolikkumpaṭi ceytapōtum, avaṉ taṉ paṭikaḷil veṟṟi kaṇṭu makiḻntā ṉilli; perumi koṇṭāṉilli!”
paṭiyeṭuppāḷarkaḷit tiruppi ōṭṭuvatuṭaṉ avaṉ maṉaniṟivu aṭiyavilli. cōḻa nāṭṭukkē pōrit tiruppikkoṇṭu cellat tuṇivukoṇṭāṉ. tiṟami vāynta  vilvīrarum, vēlvīrarum aṭaṅkiya oru perumpaṭi tiraṭṭi avarkaḷic cōḻanāṭṭiṉ teṉ mākāṇamākiya miḻalik kūṟṟattiṉuḷ celuttiṉāṉ.7 avaṉ talivaṉ paḻimiyāṉa vēḷāḷar marapiṉic cārnta evvi. evviyi muṟiyaṭittu avaṉ miḻalik kūṟṟattit taṉ āṭciyuṭaṉ cērttuk koṇṭāṉ. attuṭaṉ avaṉ cēranāṭṭiṉ kīḻpuṟa mākāṇamāṉa muttūṟṟuk kūṟṟattiyum veṉṟu kikkoṇṭāṉ.
iv vīracceyalkaḷ aṇṭiyayal aracar aṉivariṉ poṟāmiyiyum kiḷaṟiṟṟu. cōḻarum, cērarum, cērntu oru māperiya kūṭṭu amippu uṇṭupaṇṇiṉar. atil avarkaḷ tam paṭikaḷi vēṟu aintu aracar paṭikaḷuṭaṉ iṇittaṉar. av varacarkaḷ potiyat talivaṉ titiyaṉ, takaṭūrt talivaṉ atiyamāṉ eḻiṉi, erumiyūraṉ atāvatu erumi nāṭṭiṉ (micūriṉ) āṭciyāḷaṉ, iruṅkōvēṇmāṉ, porunaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ.8
avarkaḷ pāṇṭiyaṉ valimiyi noṟukkiviṭa eṇṇi ṉārkaḷ. āṉāl pāṇṭiyaṉ nerukkaṭikkēṟṟa niṟitiṟami yuṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ avarkaḷ pōkkukaḷik kūrntu kavaṉittu vantāṉ. oṉṟupaṭṭuc ceyalāṟṟavō, taṉ nāṭṭiṉuḷ pukuntu nilaṅkaḷi aḻivu ceyyavō avarkaḷuk vāyppa ḷikkāmal avaṉ tuṇintu avarkaḷ cantippiṭamākiya taliyālaṅ kāṉattilēyēceṉṟu avarkaḷit tākkiṉāṉ.
tiṭumeṉak tākkappaṭṭālum, nēcappaṭikaḷ vīṟuṭaṉ pōriṭṭaṉa. pōr oru muḻuppakalum mummuramāka naṭi peṟṟa piṉṉarē pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷik kaḷattiliruntu turatta muṭintatu. tamiḻakattiluḷḷa ellā maṉṉarum, talivarkaḷum avarkaḷ paṭikaḷiṉ poṟukkiyeṭutta uyirppakutikaḷum ippōril īṭupaṭṭaṉa. ikkāraṇattiṉālēyē, pāṇṭiyaṉ itil aṭintaveṟṟi akkālattiṉ taliciṟanta vīrac ceyalākak karutappaṭṭatu. avaṉ kuṭikaḷ aṭinta makiḻccikku elli illi. avaṉ aracaviyil pulavarkaḷum, pāṇarkaḷum vantu kuḻumi, annāḷiya taliciṟanta vīraṉeṉṟu avaṉi vāyārap pukaḻntārkaḷ.
itaṉpiṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉē nēraṭiyākac cēra nāṭṭukkuḷ oru paṭiyeṭuppu naṭattiṉāṉ. taṉippaṭṭa kaṇṇiṉ vaṭivamiti kāraṇamāka yāṉikkaṇ eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭa maṉṉaṉ cēy ciṟippiṭikkappaṭṭāṉ. cēraṉ ciṟiyiliruntu tappiyōṭiṉāṉ9. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ avaṉi mēlkaṭaṟkarivari turatticceṉṟu muciṟi nakararukil avaṉi mīṇṭum tōṟkaṭittāṉ10. cēraṉiṉ kīḻ āṇṭa talivaṉāṉa aḻumpilvēḷiṉ āṭcip pakuti avaṉiṭa miruntu paṟikkappaṭṭatu.11
pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭṭanāṭṭut talivarkaḷi veṉṟu an nāṭṭiyum taṉ aracuṭaṉ cērttukkoṇṭāṉ. ‘tuḷuvar’ eṉṟa marapiṉariṭamiruntu muttuveḷḷil eṉṟa kaṭal tuṟimukattiyum, nākarkaḷiṭamiruntu (maṉṉar kuṭāviluḷḷa) cāliyūr eṉṟa peyarpeṟṟa vāṇikakkaḷattiyum veṉṟāṉ.
iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ karikālaṉ māḷvukkuc cila nāḷ muṉṉart tavicēṟiṉāṉ. taliyālaṅkāṉattu veṟṟikkup piṉ avaṉ apperuñcōḻaṉuṭaṉ naṭpuṟavu koṇṭiruntāṉ. kāvirippaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉ pāṭal itaṉik kāṭṭu kiṟatu. karikālaṉip paṟṟiya innūl kuṟippil itu muṉpē tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.12 neṭuñceḻiyaṉ ‘veḷḷiyampalam’ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭa maturiyiluḷḷa civaṉ kōyilil māṇṭāṉ. ik kāraṇattāl ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyattil avaṉ veḷḷiyampalattut tuñciya peruvaḻuti eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭṭāṉ. avaṉ āṭcikkālam peritum ki. pi. 90 mutal 128 vari āyirukkakkūṭum.
iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ mutaṉmiyāka matitta kaviñar māṅkuṭi marutaṉār āvar. avaṉip pukaḻntu maturikkāñci eṉṟa mullinilac ciṟu kāviyam13 iyaṟṟiyavar avarē. tam puravalaṉippaṟṟik kaviñar mikac cīriya paṇpōviyam tīṭṭiyuḷḷār.14
atu varumāṟu :
“naṭpiṉ nī eṉṟum uṟutiyuṭiyavaṉ. tēvarkaḷē uṉakku ācikāṭṭi mayakki, poṉṉulakattiyum, amutattiyum vaḻaṅkuvatākak kūṟiṉālum, nī avaṟṟil vacappaṭamāṭṭāy. mikavum valimi vāynta maṉṉavar uṉṉi etirttālum iṉṉoruvar āṇiyi nī eṟpatilli. teṉmalikaḷil vāṇaṉ putittuḷḷa taṅkam muḻutum uṉakkuk kiṭittāl kūṭa, nī tīcceyal ceyya iṇaṅkamāṭṭāy. naṉmi ceyvatilēyē nī eṉṟum makiḻvāy.”
ip pāṭaliṉ nōkkam maṉṉaṉ maṉatti ulakiyal avākkaḷiliruntu viṭuvittut taṉ uyirniliyiṉ naṉṉiliyil ava eḻuppuvatēyākum. teyvap paṟṟārvamuṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷi niṉivūṭṭi avarkaḷ muṉmātirikaḷip piṉpaṟṟumpaṭi aracaṉukkuk kūṟum iṟuti aṭikaḷ varumāṟu : 15
“pala vēḷvikaḷ ceyta mutukuṭumiyippōlavum, ulakiṉ paḻamperuṅ kalikaḷ payiṉṟa taliciṟanta kaviñar pārāṭṭiyuḷḷa nilantaru tiruviṉ neṭīyōṉippōlavum, pala aṟivarkaḷāl uṉ vīramum, naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum, pukaḻapperukiṉṟaṉa. vellutaṟ kariyavaṟṟi veṉṟu, āṭciyelli parappiyum; aṟiviṟ ciṟantavar aṟintaṉayāviyum aṟintu uṉ pukaḻi nili nāṭṭiyum nī kaṭal naṭuvē nilavum kāli eḻuñāyiṟṟiyum, vīṇmīṉkaḷukkiṭiyē tikaḻum muḻunilāviyum uṉ aracurimik kuṭikkuriya iḷavaracar cūḻtara, iṟavāp pukaḻvāynta māṇpumikka māṟaṉ uḷḷiṭṭa pōrppukaḻk kōcar tuṇitara, aimperuṅ kuḻuviṉ matippum, kuṭimaṉṉar kuḻāttiṉ matippum peṟṟu vāḻkiṟāy.”
“oḷimikka pūṇkaḷaṇinta uṉ paṇippeṇkaḷ poṟ kālattil ēntiya naṟumaṇamikka matuvi nāḷ tōṟum paruki apperumiyuṭaṉ muḻuniṟivāṇāḷ peṟṟu vāḻvāyāka!”
paṭivīrarkaḷiṭam avaṉ kāṭṭiya aṉpu, paṭi vīṭṭil puṇpaṭṭavariṭam avaṉ koṇṭa karicaṉi ākiyavi nakkīrar iyaṟṟiya neṭunalvāṭiyil viritturikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.16
atu varumāṟu:-
“naḷḷiravil kuḷir naṭukkukiṟa vāṭik kāṟṟu vīcukiṟatu. āyiṉum maṉṉaṉ pantam ēntiya orucila paṇi makkaḷuṭaṉ taṉ kūṭārattiviṭṭup puṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. cēṇamiṭṭuc ciṟumaṇi kaḷāl oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟappu mikka pōrkkutiri oṉṟu avaṉpiṉṉē celkiṟatu.”
“muttukkōvikaḷ tūkkiya veṇkuṭi oṉṟu maḻit tūṟaliliruntu kākkum muṟiyil aracaṉ talikku mēlākap piṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. iṭatu kiyāl nīṇṭu toṅkum taṉ āṭiyip piṭittukkoṇṭu, aracarimic ciṉṉamāṉa vāḷik kiyilēntiya kaṭṭuṭal vāynta ōr iḷiñaṉ tōḷmītu valakki iṭṭavaṇṇam avaṉ celkiṟāṉ.”
paṭittalivarkaḷil oruvaṉ vēppantār ‘cūṭṭiya vēlit tāṅkiyapaṭi muṉceṉṟu, muṉṉāḷ pōril puṇpaṭṭa vīrar ovvoruvariyum taṉittaṉi cuṭṭikkāṭṭa avaṉ nalamucāviṉāṉ.’
iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkuppiṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti aracaṉāṉāṉ. 48 pulavar muṉṉiliyil tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ māḷāp pukaḻ nūlāṉa tirukkuṟaḷ araṅkēṟṟap paṭṭa avik kaḷattiṉ aracaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil tamiḻ ilakkiya māṇavar ellārumē ivvaracaṉ peyari aṟivar. ivvaracaṉukkuk kāṉappēr tanta ukkirap peruvaḻuti eṉṟa paṭṭappeyar uṇṭu. itaṉ poruḷāvatu, vella muṭiyāta kōṭṭiyeṉṟu annāḷil karutappaṭṭa kāṉappēr eṉṟa araṇ vāynta nakaratti avaṉ veṉṟu taṉ āṭkikkīḻk koṇṭuvantāṉ eṉpatē.
ikkōṭṭiyi veṉṟa ceytiyi ivvaracaṉip pāṭiya pāṭaloṉṟu kīḻvarumāṟu pukaḻnturikkiṟatu.17
“cāvāp pukaḻuṭiya vēntē, vāḻi! māperuṅ kōṭṭiyākiya kāṉappēri veṉṟu kikkoṇṭa niṉ vīrattik kaviñar pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār.”
“akkēṭṭiyiṉ matilkaḷ vāṉattic ceṉṟeṭṭiṉa eṉṟu tōṟṟiṉa. ataṉ ñāyilkaḷ viṇmīṉkaḷpōla maṉṉiṉa. kōṭṭiyic cūḻnta akaḻi kaṭalpōṉṟa elliyaṟṟa āḻa akalamuṭiyatu. ataṟkappāluḷḷa muṭkāṭu ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirkaḷ eṉṟum nuḻintatilli eṉṟu kūṟumaḷavu aṭarttiyāṉatu.”
“ik kōṭṭiyi iḻanta vēṅkimārpaṉ appēriḻappi eṇṇi nāḷtōṟum varuntukiṟāṉ. ēṉeṉil paḻukkak kāycciya irumpil ūṟṟiya nīrpōla, atu eṉṟum mīṇṭum perutaṟkariyatu.”
“uṉ etirikaḷ aḻivuṟṟu maṟakkappaṭakkaṭavar! uṉ vēl eṉṟum veṟṟiyē kāṇakkaṭavatu!”
ivvaracaṉap paṟṟi vēṟu etuvum teriyavilli. avaṉ āṭci orucila āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭa nīṭittirukkavilli eṉṟē peritum eṇṇattakum. avaṉi aṭuttuvanta aracaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ allatu nalla pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ. avaṉ avik kaḷattiluḷḷa pulavar pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu varumāṟu:18

“malarmāliyaṇinta māṟaṉē! oru maliyi villākavum, oru pāmpi  nāṇākavum koṇṭu oppaṟṟa ōr ampeytu acurariṉ mūvaraṇkaḷi aḻittuc cāvilā vāṉavarkaḷukku veṟṟi tēṭittanta kariya miṭaṟuṭiya iṟivaṉiṉ piṟiyaṇiyap peṟṟa neṟṟiyil tulaṅkukiṉṟa anta oppaṟṟa kaṇṇippōla, maṉṉariṭiyē nī oppuyarvaṟṟavaṉāka viḷaṅkukiṟāy!”
“mēlīṭāyp pārppavarukku ōr aracaṉiṉ āṟṟal avaṉuṭiya mūrkkamāṉa pōr yāṉikaḷ, virinaṭiyuṭiya pōrkkutirikaḷ, neṭuṅkoṭiyuṭiya tērkaḷ, añcā neñcuṭiya vīrarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷip poṟuttateṉṟu tōṟṟakkūṭum. āṉāl uṇmiyil ataṉ kaṭikāl naṭunili nērmiyē.”
“ākavē uṉ kuṭimakkaḷiṭam orutaliccārpu kāṭṭātē. ayalnāṭṭavariṭam nērmi kēṭākavum naṭakkātē. veṅ katiravaṉippōla vīṟum, meṉmi vāynta veṇṇilāp pōṉṟa taṇṇaḷiyum uṉ nāṭṭil vaṟumiyoḻiya maḻipoḻiyum mukiliṉaṅkaḷpōṉṟu aṉpātaravum uṭiyavaṉāy iruppāyāka!”
“veṇṇuriyi mukaṭṭil koṇṭa kaṭalalikaḷ karimītu ōyātu mōtum murukaṉukkuriya tirunilivāynta centil eṉṉum aḻakiya tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉattiṉarukē vaṉpāṟṟukkaḷ koṭuvantu kuvikkum maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷiluḷḷa nuṇmaṇalkaḷ pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa āṇṭukaḷ nī vāḻvāyāka!”
maṟṟoru pāṭal varumāṟu :-
“kaṭuñciṉattil nī kūṟṟuvaṉ pōṉṟavaṉ. etiraṟṟa valimiyil nī palatēvaṉippōṉṟavaṉ. pukaḻil, taṉṉip pukaḻātavarkaḷi aḻikkum tirumāl pōṉṟavaṉ. tāṉ virumpiya aṉittum muṭikkum tiṟaṉil nī murukak kaṭavuḷ pōṉṟavaṉ. uṉṉāl iyalātatu yātu?”
“eṉavē uṉṉiṭam irappavarkkut taṅkutaṭiyiṉṟit tārāḷamāy vāri vaḻaṅku.”
“yavaṉar tam nalla kappalkaḷil koṇṭu varukiṟa kuḷinta matuvi, oḷimikka vaḷikaḷik kikaḷil aṇinta uṉ paṇip peṇkaḷ aḻakiya poṟkalattil ēnta, ataṉi nāḷtōṟum paruki uṉ nāṭkaḷi iṉpamākak kaḻippāyāka!”
“veṟṟi vāḷ ēntiya māṟa! iruḷakaṟṟum taḻal pōṉṟa katirparappum katiravaṉpōlavum, māliyil oḷi tarum taṇkatirparappum iṉiya veṇṇilāppōlavum nilavulakiṉ maṉṉaruḷ taliciṟantavaṉāka nīṭu vāḻvāyāka!”
immaṉṉaṉ ilavantikippaḷḷi eṉṟa iṭattil māḷvuṟṟa taṉāl avaṉ piṉ vanta pulavarkaḷāl ilavantikip paḷḷit tuñciya naṉmāṟaṉ eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭāṉ.
cērar kuṭimarapuk koṭi
mutalām ātaṉ
(ki. pi. 40-55)

(veṇṇil pōril mutukil puṇpaṭṭu vaṭakkiruntiṟantavaṉ)

iraṇṭām ātaṉ = cōṉi, (karikāla cōḻaṉ makaḷ)
(ki. pi. 55-90)

(vāṉavarampaṉ allatu celvakkaṭuṅkō)


aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
iyal - 6 pāṇṭiyar
1.	cilappatikāram, maturik kāṇṭam, 1:14
2.	cilappatikāram, kāti ōō
3.	puṟam 183.
4.	cilappatikāram ōōēaiai. aṭikaḷ, 127-135.
5.	puṟam 72.
6.	puṟam 77-79.
7.	puṟam 24,
8.	akam 36.
9.	puṟam 17.
10.	akam 57.
11.	maturikkāñci aṭikaḷ 344-345.
12.	mēlē iyal aiai pak, pārkka.
13.	aiṉalaṭaṭa.
14.	maturik kāñci aṭikaḷ 190-205.
15.	maturikkāñci aṭikaḷ 759-782.
16.	neṭunelvāṭi aṭikaḷ 172-188.
17.	puṟam. 21.
18.	puṟam 55, 56

7. cērar
nām eṭuttukkoṇṭa kālattiya mutal cēra aracaṉ mutalām ātaṉē. avaṉ karikāla cōḻaṉ kālattavaṉ. karikālaṉ varalāṟṟil ēṟkaṉavē kuṟittuḷḷapaṭi, veṇṇil pōril1 karikālaṉit taṉ paṭikaḷiṉ talimiyil etirttuc camarceyta pōtu, avaṉ mutukil puṇpaṭṭāṉ. itaṉ avamatippip poṟukkamāṭṭāmal, avaṉ vaṭakkiruntu iṟakkat tuṇintāṉ. māḷvilum avaṉi viṭṭuppiriya maṉamillāta pala ārvat tuṇivarkaḷ iccamayam tāmum maṉamāra avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟiruntu uyirviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.2
mutalām ātaṉiyaṭuttu aracirukki ēṟiyavaṉ celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭappeyar koṇṭa iraṇṭām ātaṉ āvāṉ. karikālaṉ makaḷ cōṇiyi maṇantataṉmūlam karikālaṉ naṭpip peṟṟāṉ3. akkālat tamiḻ aracarkaḷuḷ vallami mikka pēraracaṉ marumakaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil avaṉ āṭci amitiyum vaḷamum uṭiyatāyiruntatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu.
pārppaṉarāṉa kapilar inta aracaṉiṉ taṉi naṭappukkuriya varāyiruntār. iṉpamāka aḷavaḷāvikkoṇṭirukkum nērattil orunāḷ cēra aracaṉ kaviñar kiyip paṟṟi eṭuttu, atu evvaḷavu meṉmiyāyiruntatu eṉpatik kūrntu nōkki viyantāṉ. pukaḻccik kaliyil valla kapilar maṉṉaṉ kiyiṉ vīra valimiyip pukaḻntu, attakiya vīraṉ ātaravil vāḻum kaviñarkaḷiṉ kikaḷ melliyaṉavāka irāmal vēṟu eppaṭi irukka muṭiyum eṉṟu aracaṉ pārāṭṭi nayam paṭa avaṉ ticiyil tiruppiṉār.
iccamayam avar pāṭiya pāṭaliṉ karuttu varumāṟu 4:-
“vīramikka aracaṉē! uṉ taṭantōḷkaḷ aḻakumikka aṇaṅkukaḷukkuk kātalnōy ūṭṭupavi. atē camayam avi uṉ etirikaḷukku accam tarupavi. uṉ taṭakkikaḷō, poṉ vēynta aṅkucattāl yāṉiyi ūkki irumput tāḻiṭṭa uṉ etirikaḷiṉ camaṉ ceyyappaṭṭa karikaḷiyuṭiya āḻnta akaḻikaḷittāvumpaṭi vīṟumikka uṉ pērkkutiriyi naṭattic cellumvaṇṇam kaṭivāḷaṅkaḷip piṭittiḻup paviyum aviyē! tavira nī tēril cellumpōtu uṉ tōḷmītu toṅkum amput tūṇiyiliruntu ampukaḷi eṭuttu avaṟṟi eyyumpaṭi villivāṅki viṟalpaṭa vaḷippaviyum aviyē! ittakiya āṟṟal vāynta ceyalkaḷac ceyvataṉāl, uṉ tōḷkaḷ nīṇṭu tiraṇṭum, kikaḷ tuvaṉṟu valimi peṟṟum uḷḷaṉa.”
“āṉāl uṉṉip pāṭum pulavarkaḷukkō, ventu poṅkiya cōṟum, neyyil poritta iṟicciyum, maṇamūṭṭiya kaṟikaḷum, tiṉpataṉṟi vēṟu vēli kiṭiyātu. itaṉāl avarkaḷ kikaḷ meṉmi vāyntaviyāyviṭṭaṉa.”
maṟṟoru camayam pulavar aracaṉippaṟṟit tuṇikaramāṉa kaṟpaṉiyum uvami nayamum mikka oru pāṭalip pāṭiṉār. appāṭal katiravaṉi nōkkip pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. katiravaṉukkum maṉṉaṉukkumiṭiyē atu pala oppumi vēṟṟumikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, ovvoṉṟilum katiravaṉiviṭak kāvalaṉē mēmpaṭuvatik5 kaviñar nayampaṭa viḷakkiyuḷḷār.6
“viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu
iṭañciṟi teṉṉum ūkkam turappa
oṭuṅkā uḷḷat tōmpā īkik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēral ātaṉi
yāṅkaṉam ottiyō, vīṅkucelal maṇṭilam?
poḻutoṭu variti, puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti;
māṟivaruti, malimaṟin toḷitti;
akaliru vicumpiṉāṉum
pakalviḷaṅ kutiyāl, palkatir virittē!”
itu muṟṟilum collaṇit tiṟamāyiṉum, uvami oppīṭu muṟṟilum putumi nayamuṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. ippukaḻcci yāl aracaṉ mikavum makiḻntirukkavēṇṭumeṉpatil aiyamilli. pulavarukku avaṉ pala ūrkaḷi itaṟkup paricāka aḷittāṉ.
iraṇṭām ātaṉukku iru putalvarkaḷ iruntaṉar. mūttavaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. iḷiyavarē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, aḵtāvatu araca tuṟavi āvar. piṉ kūṟappaṭṭavarē cilappatikāra kāviyam pāṭiyavar. tam vāḻkki nikaḻcciyoṉṟi ivar ikkāviyattil varintuḷḷār.7
oru camayam iḷaṅkōvum avar uṭaṉ piṟantāṉum avarkaḷ tanti vīṟṟirunta aracirukkiyiṉ arukē kolu maṉṟattil amarntiruntaṉar. ac camayam ōr aṟivar aracaṉmuṉ tōṉṟiṉār. avar maṉṉaṉ mukappolivuṭaṉ iru putalvarkaḷiṉ mukappolivukaḷiyum kūrntu nōkkiṉār. maṉṉaṉ viriyil vāḻkki niṟiveytuvāṉ eṉṟum, iḷiya putalvaṉē aracaṉāvataṟkuriya aṟikuṟiyuṭiyava ṉeṉṟum varuṅkuṟiyurittār.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tam uṭaṉ piṟantāṉiṭam uṇmiyāṉa paṟṟuṭiyavarāyiruntār. ākavē aracirukkikkut tāṉ varakkūṭum eṉṟa nampikki muṟṟilum aṟum vaṇṇam, akkaṇamē ulaka vāḻkki tuṟantu nikaṇṭavātikaḷukkuriya tuṟavaṟam mēṟkoḷḷat tuṇintār.
maṉṉaṉ cikkaṟpaḷḷiyil uyirnīttāṉ. itu mutal avaṉ cikkaṟpaḷḷit tuñciya celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ, atāvatu cikkaṟpaḷḷiyil uyirnītta vīra maṟam vāynta maṉṉaṉ ātaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṟa imayavarampaṉ tantiyākiya iraṇṭām ātaṉukkuppiṉ ēṟattāḻak ki.pi.90-il tavicēṟiṉāṉ. paṭṭayattukku vanta ciṟitu nāḷilellām avaṉ viyalūrik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. innakaram iruṅkōvēṇ māṉiṉ talinakaram. avaṉ nāṭu maliyaṭarntatu. atil taṅkam veṭṭi yeṭukkappaṭṭatu.8
itaṉiyaṭuttuc cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ cōḻar tavicu karikāla cōḻaṉiṉ peyaraṉum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cēraṉiṉ mittuṉaṉu māṉa kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku urimiyāyiṟṟu. cōḻa iḷavaracar oṉpatiṉmar avaṉukketirākak kiḷarnteḻuntaṉar. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ oṉpatu iḷavaracariyum nērivāyil eṉṉumiṭattil muṟiyaṭittut taṉ mittuṉaṉ aracurimi kāttāṉ.9
itaṉpiṉ avaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyil āṇṭa paḻiyaṉmāṟaṉiṉ talinakarāṉa mōkūrit tākkiṉāṉ. paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉāl tīṅkukku āḷāṉa aṟuki eṉṟa talivaṉukkākavē avaṉ inta naṭavaṭikkiyilīṭupaṭṭāṉ10. patiyiḻanta tāy cōṇi kaṅki nīrāṭuvataṟkāka avaṉ vaṭatici ceṉṟāṉ. pala āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittup putumivāynta cūḻniliyil avaṉ mīṇṭum kaṅki cellavēṇṭi vantatu. annikaḻcci cilappatikārattil kīḻvarumāṟu viritturikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu11:-
“vāṉavarkōṉ kōti kūrvāḷ ēntiyavaṉ. kaṭal cūḻnta kaṭampu eṟintavaṉ. imayamaliyiluḷḷa vāṉavar kaṇṭu viyakkum paṭi taṉ viṟkoṭiyi imayamalimītu poṟittavaṉ.”
“orunāḷ avaṉ taṉ araciyuṭaṉ veḷḷimāṭattē vīṟṟiruntāṉ. appōtu avaṉ pacuṅkāṭukaḷ niṟinta malikaḷik kāṇacceṉṟāṉ. ammalikaḷiṉmītu mukilkaḷ nīṅkātu taṅkiṉa. ataṉ aruvikaḷ iṭiviṭātu iṉṉōci yuṭaṉ āravārittaṉa. avaṉuṭaṉ pūñcōlikaḷil āṭi viḷiyāṭum eṇṇaṅkoṇṭa vāṉavar makaḷir palar vañci nakariliruntu ceṉṟiruntaṉar. aṉivarum periyāṟṟiṉ kariyilirunta oru maṇaṟkuṉṟiṉ mītu taṅkiṉārkaḷ.”
“periyāṟu ivviṭattilēyē malikaḷiviṭṭiṟaṅkit tāḻ nilaṅkaḷi nōkkip pāykiṟatu. ataṉ nīr paravic ciṟu tīvukaḷiyum, cōlikaḷiyum, māṭakūṭaṅkaḷiyum, ampalaṅ kaḷiyum, aḷāvic ceṉṟatu. iṅkiruntu aracaṉ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷik kaṇṭu makiḻntāṉ. āṟu taṉ parappiṉmītu palvaki malarkaḷi ēntikkoṇṭu ceṉṟatu. malikkuṟavar āṭalpāṭalkaḷ kaṇṭu maṉṉaṉum maṅkiyarum makiḻntārkaḷ.”
“kāṭṭu yāṉikaḷik kuḻippoṟiyil vīḻttiyum, tēṉ kūṭukaḷik kiḻittum vēṭarkaḷ ceyta āravāramum, aruviyiṉ ārpparippum, yāṉikaḷiṉ piḷiṟalum, aracaṉuṭaṉ vanta vīrarkaḷiṉ kavacaṅkaḷiṉ calacalappum cērntu avarkaḷ cevikaḷil olittavaṇṇam iruntaṉa.”
“aracaṉum avaṉ tuṇivarum ivvāṟu iṉpuṟṟirunta vēḷiyil vēṭarkaḷ yāṉiyiṉ veṇ tantaṅkaḷ, maṇaṅ kamaḻum akil kaṭṭikaḷ, kavarimayir viciṟikaḷ, tēṉ kuṭam, cantaṇak kaṭṭikaḷ, cātiliṅkam, vīram, kentaka añcaṉakkal, ēlakkāy, miḷakukkuvikaḷ, kūvalkiḻaṅku māvu, iṉiya kavalik kiḻaṅkukaḷ, tēṅkāy, māmpaḻam, palāppaḻaṅkaḷ, pacuntaḻi, uḷḷi, karumpu, taḻikkoṭi mālikaḷ, pākku, vāḻik kulikaḷ, ciṅkak kuruḷikaḷ, yāḷikkuruḷikaḷ; pulikkuṭṭikaḷ, yāṉikkaṉṟukaḷ, kuraṅkuk kuṭṭikaḷ, karaṭikkuṭṭikaḷ, mali yāṭukaḷ, mali māṉkaḷ; kattūrimāṉ kuḻavikaḷ, kīrikaḷ, mayilkaḷ, puṉukuppūṉikaḷ, kāṭṭuk kōḻikaḷ, kiḷikaḷ ākiya vaṟṟic cumantavaṇṇam avaṉik kāṇa vantaṉar.”
“mali vaḷaṅkaḷāṉa taṅkaḷ viḷiporuḷkaḷik kāṇikkiyāka avaṉmuṉ vittu avarkaḷ vaṇaṅki vāḻttiṉārkaḷ.”
“vāḻka, nīṭu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ vāḻka, aracē!”
“eḻupiṟappum nāṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuṭiyurimiyuṭiyōm.”
“kāṭṭilē vēṅki marattiṉ niḻalilē oru mārpakamiḻanta aḻakār aṇaṅku orutti taṉiyē kiṭantu tuṉpuṟṟu uyirnīttāḷ. avaḷ ennāṭṭiṉaḷ. ekkuṭiyiṉaḷ eṉṟu yām aṟiyōm.”
“paṉṉūṟāyiram āṇṭunī aḷuti, aracē!”
“maṉṉaṉuṭaṉirunta iṉtamiḻ ācāṉ cāttaṉ maṉṉaṉ nalamārnta kāṭci kaṇṭu makiḻntu, maṉṉaṉukkum, maṉṉaracikkum tuyarārnta avvaṇaṅkiṉ varalāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.”
“avaṉ urittāvatu.”
“avaḷ kāvirippaṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa oru vaṇikaṉ maṉivi. an nakarattiluḷḷa ōr āṭal aḻakiyiṭam taṉ celvam yāvum pōkkik kaṇavaṉ taṉ kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyuṭaṉ maturikku vantāṉ. ap peṇṇiṭam appōtu irunta orē celvam avaḷ kāl cilampukaḷ iraṇṭumē. kaṇavaṉ avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi viṟṟu, ataṟku viliyākak kiṭitta paṇattikkoṇṭu putitāka vaṇika vāḻkki toṭaṅka eṇṇiṉāṉ.”
“taṉ pōtākkālamāka, avaṉ maṉṉaṉ poṟkollaṉi aṭintāṉ. appoṟkollaṉ ēṟkeṉavē araciyiṉ       cilampukaḷil oṉṟit tiruṭiyiruntavaṉ. ākavē ayalāṉoru vāṉal tiruṭupōṉa cilampē viṟpaṉi nāṭi vantirukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. tiruṭaṉik koṇṭēkavanta kāvalariṭam vaṇikaṉik kāṭṭiṉāṉ.”
“tiṭumeṉa nērnta nikaḻcci māṟāṭṭattāl tikittu niṉṟa vaṇikaṉ cilampu taṉ kiyil vanta vakiyik kāvalariṭam viḷakkikkūṟa muṭiyātavaṉāy niṉṟāṉ. avaṉiyē tiruṭaṉeṉṟu aiyuṟṟa kāvalar avviṭattilēyē avaṉik koliceytu cilampi araṇmaṉikkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.”
“vaṇikaṉ kollappaṭṭa avalac ceyti avaṉ maṉivi cevikaḷil paṭṭatu. ac camayam avaḷ āyarpāṭiyil taṅki yiruntāḷ. kaṇkaḷiliruntu nīr oḻukiya vaṇṇam, avaḷ kaṇavaṉ uṭal kuṟṟuyirāyk kurutipaṭintu kiṭanta iṭam ceṉṟāḷ. araciyiṉ cilampukaḷuḷ oṉṟit tiruṭiyatākak kuṟṟañcāṭṭappaṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉ koliyuṇṭāṉeṉṟa ceytiyi makkaḷiṭamiruntaṟintu ventuyarāṟāta patiyiḻanta anta aṇaṅku maṉṉaṉik kāṭci nāṭi avaṉmuṉ ceṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ kiyilirunta cilampu pāṇṭiya aracikkuriya taṉṟeṉṟum, taṉatē eṉṟum avaḷ maṉṉaṉaṟiyak kāṭṭiṉāḷ.*”
“ceyta paḻikkañciya maṉṉaṉ uṇarviḻantu, taviciliruntu viḻuntu tirumpa eḻātu uyirviṭṭāṉ. araciyum avaṉ īma aḻalil pāyntu māṇṭāḷ.”
“vāḻviḻanta naṅki taṉ mārpakaṅkaḷil oṉṟit tiruki ati maturit terukkaḷil eṟintāḷ. tīya paḻi cūḻnta annakaram tīyil aḻika eṉṟu varaṅkōriṉāḷ. itaṉpaṭi araṇmaṉiyum nakariṉ oru pakutiyum tīkkiriyāyiṉa.”
“avaḷ itaṉpiṉ maturiyi viṭṭakaṉṟu mēl tici vaḻiyākacceṉṟu cēranāṭaṭintu aṅkē māṇṭāḷ.”
“ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum avaṉ araciyum ivvaralāṟu kēṭṭu mikavum urukiṉar. avappēṟuṟṟu kaṟpuṭiya an naṅki teyvamāka vaḻipaṭuvataṟkuriyavaḷ eṉṟu araci kuṟittāḷ.”
“maṉṉaṉ ik karutti ēṟṟāṉ. taṉṉic cūḻntirunta aṟivāḷari nōkki avarkaḷ aṟivuri kōriṉāṉ. potiya maliyiliruntu oru kaṟcili peyartturuvākki, atik kāvariyil nīrāṭṭalām allatu imaya maliyiliruntu kaṟcili peyartturuvākkik kaṅkiyil nīrāṭṭalām eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ.”
“potiyattil kalleṭuttuk kāviriyil nīrāṭṭuvatu maṟakkuṭiyil vanta taṉpōṉṟavarkku aḻakalla eṉṟu maṉṉaṉ cāṟṟiṉāṉ. imayamaliyiliruntē kalkoṇaruvateṉṟu tuṇintāṉ.”
“avaṉ amiccaṉ villavaṉkōti avaṉi nōkki ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.”
“pallāṇṭu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ vāḻvīrāka, aracē!”
“koṅku nāṭṭup pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟōṭiya uṉ māṟṟaracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ pulikkoṭiyiyum mīṉ koṭiyiyum tuṟantuviṭṭucceṉṟuḷḷaṉar. um veṟṟiyiṉ pukaḻ ulakeṅkum parantuḷḷatu.”
“um aṉṉiyārākiya araciyar kaṅki nīrāṭiya camayam, tām koṅkaṇar, kaliṅkar, karunāṭar, paṅkaḷar, kaṅkar, kaṭṭiyar, vaṭavāriyar ākiyōr kūṭṭuppaṭimītu tākkiya veṟṟit tākkutali eṉ kaṇkaḷ eṉṟum maṟakkamāṭṭā.”
“ākavē teyvac cilikkuk kal peṟumpaṭi imaya malikkuc celvatu tam viruppamāyiṉ, atu paṟṟiya ceyti eḻuttu mūlam vaṭapula aracarukku aṉuppappaṭutal naṉṟu eṉṟāṉ.”
aḻumpil vēḷ ippōtu eḻuntu pēciṉāṉ.
“veḷināṭṭu nilavarat tūtarkaḷ vañciyiṉ vāyililēyē taṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tam paṭiccelaviṉ ceytiyi avvaracarkaḷ avarkaḷ mūlamē aṟintukoḷḷaṭṭum. payaṇa vivaratti nam talinakarattilēyē muracaṟivittāl pōtumāṉatu eṉṟāṉ.”
maṉṉaṉum avaṉ kuḻāttiṉarum itaṉ piṉṉar virivil vañcikkut tirumpivantārkaḷ.
maṉṉaṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi kaṅkikkuc cellavirukkum paṭippōkku vivaram maṉṉurimi yāṉimītu koṇṭu cellappaṭṭa muracaṅkaḷāl nakareṅkum paṟicāṟṟappaṭṭatu.
“maṉṉaṉ tiruōlakkamaṇṭapam ēṟi, arimāṉuruvaṅkaḷāl tāṅkappaṭṭa irukkiyil amarntāṉ. avaṉi varavēṟkak kuḻumik kāttirunta talimik kuruvum, talimikkaṇiyum, araciyaṟpēramiccarkaḷum, paṭipperuntalivarkaḷum, “nīṭuvāḻka maṉṉavar maṉṉaṉ,” eṉṟu vāḻtteṭuttup piṉṉar, paṭippōkkup paṟṟiya avaṉ kaṭṭaḷi yāteṉak kēṭṭaṉar.”
“paṭit talivarkaḷiyum, vīrappaṭi maṟavariyum nōkki maṉṉaṉ muḻaṅkiṉāṉ.”
“vaṭavaracar tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷippaṟṟi ēḷaṉamākap pēciṉar eṉṟu imayamaliyiliruntuvanta yāttirikarkaḷ kūṟiṉar. ākavē avvaracarkaḷi muṟiyaṭittu, avarkaḷ tam taliyiṉmītu teyvac cilikkuriya kalli ēṟṟivarumpaṭi valukkaṭṭāyappaṭuttiyaṉṟi ip paṭippōkkiṉiṉṟu mīḷēṉ” eṉṟāṉ.
“talimikkuru maṉṉaṉi nōkkik kaṭṭurittāṉ.”
“kaṭuñciṉamāṟṟuka, imayavarampaṉē! vaṭavaracar ēḷaṉamākak pēciyatu uṉṉiyeṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. uṉṉip pōrk kaḷattil etirēṟṟu niṟkattakkavar pāṇṭiyarum, cōḻarum aṉṟi vēṟu yār innilattil uḷar?”
“tañcameṉṟu vantōrkaḷukku ellām nīyēyaṉṟō takka kāppu?”
“vāṉakamaṉikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭiyum, kōḷiṉaṅkaḷ, mīṉiṉaṅkaḷ, nilikaḷiyum vāṉkaṇippiṉ aintu uṟuppuk kaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya vāṉūluṇarnta talimakkaṇi eḻuntu pēciṉāṉ:”
“vallami poruntiya vēntē! veṟṟi eṉṟum niṉatākuka! ticiyil intak kaṇamē puṟappaṭat tiruvuḷaṅkūruti yāṉāl, uṉ māṟṟaracarkaḷaṉivarum vantu aṭivīḻvar” eṉṟāṉ.
“itukēṭṭu maṉṉaṉ, taṉ maṉṉurimi vāḷum, koṟṟak kuṭiyum, vaṭatici celka eṉak kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ.”
“maṟavar makiḻntu ārpparittārkaḷ. peru muracaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkiṉa. koṭikaḷ paṟantaṉa. aimperuṅkuḻuviṉarum, eṇ pērāyattārum, kurumāṟum, kaṇimāṟum, muṟivarum amiccarum, oruṅkukūṭi, ‘maṉṉaṉ vāḻka’ eṉṟu vāḻttiṉar. pūmālivēynta maṉṉurimi vāḷum, koṟṟakkuṭiyum maṉpērurimiyuṭaṉ araca yāṉimītu ēṟṟi nakarppuṟattiluḷḷa oru kōṭṭikkuk koṇṭucellappaṭṭaṉa.”
“tiruvōlakka maṉṟamēṟi maṉṉaṉ itaṉpiṉ taṉpaṭit talivarkaḷukkum araṇmaṉi vāyil talivarkaḷukkum virun tāṭṭaḷittāṉ. ataṉpiṉ talimītu vañcimāliyaṇintu puṟappāṭṭāṉ. tirumālkōyil ceṉṟu vaḻivāṭu ceytapiṉ avaṉ taṉ yāṉi ēṟiṉāṉ. kōyil kuruviṭamiruntu civa perumāṉ tiruvaṭic ciṉṉam peṟṟu ataṉip paṟṟārvattuṭaṉ taṉ taliyilum, tōḷilum aṇintāṉ. poṟkōyililuḷḷa tirumāliṉ kōyilkuravar nalvāḻttuṭaṉ, kālāḷ, kutiri, tēr, yāṉi ākiya nāluṟuppukkaḷaminta paṭi puṭicūḻa, paṭittalivar tuṇivara, veṇkoṟṟak kuṭi niḻaliṉkīḻ yāṉimītamarntu maṉṉaṉ vañciyiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭāṉ.”
“avaṉ nīlakirik kuṉṟukaḷil mutal taṅkal amittāṉ.”
inta nīlakiri taṟkāla nīlakiriyāyt tōṉṟavilli. atu oricā mākāṇattil palacōr12 arukil vaṅkavirikuṭāviliruntu 16 allatu 18 kaltolivil ceṅkuttāka niṉṟa oru mali. annāḷiya paḻiya kaṭalōṭikaḷāl atu nīlakiri eṉṟaḻikkap paṭṭatu.
cēra nāṭṭiliruntu oricāvukkuc cellum payaṇam kaṭal vaḻiyē naṭipeṟṟatākak kāviyattiṉ muṭivil kūṟappaṭum kuṟippāl terikiṟatu. paṭiyuṭaṉ kaṭal kaṭantu kaṅkik kariyi aṭintatāka aṅkē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pukaḻap peṟukiṟāṉ.
“cēra aracaṉ nīlakiriyiṉ aṭivārattil paṭivīṭṭil taṅkiyiruntapōtu, makatappēraracāṇṭa karṇariṭam paṇiceyta cañcayaṉ eṉṟa paṭittalivaṉ nūṟu āṭal naṅkiyarkaḷuṭaṉum, irunūṟṟeṭṭup pāṭakarkaḷuṭaṉum, nūṟu kaḻikkūttāṭi kaḷuṭaṉum, nūṟutēr, patiṉāyiram kutirikaḷ, irupatiṉāyiram vaṇṭikaḷ, āyiram meykkāvalar ākiyavarkaḷuṭaṉum vantu maṉṉaṉik kaṇṭāṉ.”
“vāyil kāppōṉ cañcayaṉum avaṉ paṭiyum vantatu aṟivittāṉ. ataṉmītu avarkaḷit taṉ koluvirukkikku varavaḻikkumpaṭic cēramaṉṉaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭaruḷiṉāṉ.”
“cañcayaṉ itaṉpaṭi maṉṉaṉ muṉvantu tali tāḻtti niṉṟu vaṇaṅkik kīḻvarumāṟu ceytiyurittāṉ.”
“nilavulakamāḷum maṉṉavaṉē, nīṭu vāḻka!”
“uṉ neruṅkiya naṭpurimiyuṭiya catakarṇar eṉ mūlamāka intac ceytiyi aṉuppiyuḷḷār :”
“teyvac ciliceyvataṟku uyarnta imayamaliyiliruntu kalpeṟa vāṉavar vēntaṉ tiruvuḷam nērntāl, atu peṟavum kaṅkiyil nīrāṭṭavum nāṅkaḷ utavak kāttirukkiṟōm.”
“cēra aracaṉ cañcayaṉukku maṟumoḻi pukaṉṟāṉ.”
“pālakumāraṉ mintarkaḷākiya kaṉakaṉum, vijayaṉum taṅkaḷ nāviṉ kāvalum kāppum iḻantavarkaḷāyt tamiḻaracar valimi yaṟiyāmal avarkaḷip paḻittuḷḷārkaḷ. ippaṭikaḷ itaṉāl veñciṉattit tam karuvakam koṇṭu muṉṉēṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. itic catakarkaṇarukku aṟivikka! kaṅkip pēriyāṟu kaṭantu eṉpaṭikaḷ cellumpaṭi oru kappaṟpaṭiyi avar tiraṭṭi vikkak kaṭavar!”
“cañcayaṉi aṉuppiviṭṭu, aracaṉ meykkāvalar āyira variyum taṉ koluvirukkiyil vara icivaḷittāṉ. avarkaḷi ṭamiruntu maṇamikka cantaṇakkaṭṭikaḷum muttukkaḷumaṭaṅkiya tiṟiyi ēṟṟāṉ.”
“piṉ taṅkal kalittu maṉṉaṉ paṭiyuṭaṉ kaṅkik kariyi aṭintu, karṇarkaḷ tiraṭṭit tantakappaṟpaṭiyiṉ utaviyāl avvāṟṟik kaṭantāṉ.”
“vaṭakariyil karṇarkaḷē vantu maṉṉaṉi varavēṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṭamiruntu viṭipeṟṟukkoṇṭu cēraṉ piṉṉum vaṭakkēyuḷḷa uttari nāṭu ceṉṟāṉ.”
“iṅkē kuyilāluvam eṉṟa iṭattil kaṉakaṉ, vicayaṉ ākiya āriya aracarum, avarkaḷ nēca maṉṉarāṉa uttaraṉ, vicittiraṉ, uruttiraṉ, piravaṉ, cittiraciṅkaṉ, taṉuttaraṉ, cuvētaṉ ākiyavarkaḷum naṭattiya paṭiyic cantittāṉ.”
“āriya aracariṉ māperum paṭiyik kaṇṭa cēra aracaṉ kaḻipēruvaki eytiṉāṉ. muracu muḻaṅka, caṅkam iyampa, kāḷaṅkaḷ ārpparikka avaṉ paṭi naṭattit tākkiṉāṉ. vil vīrarkaḷum vāḷ vīrarkaḷum nīṭittu mummuramāṉa pōril īṭupaṭṭārkaḷ. paṭukaḷam accantarum kurutikkaḷamāyiṟṟu.”
“nilamuḻuvatum iṟantupaṭṭa vīrar uṭalkaḷum, kutiri yāṉi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uṭalkaḷum nirampikkiṭantaṉa. pōr muṭivil tamiḻar etirikaḷaṉivariyum turattiyaṭittaṉar.”
“kaṉakaṉum, vicayaṉum maṟṟum pala aracarum ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉiṭam ciṟippaṭṭārkaḷ.”
“kaṉakaṉ vicayaṉ ākiyōr maṉṉurimi uṭikaḷic cēraṉ āṇṭi uṭiyāka māṟṟumpaṭi ceytāṉ. piṉ avarkaḷit taṉ amiccaṉ villavaṉ kōtiyuṭaṉum kāvaṟpaṭiyuṭaṉum imayattukku aṉuppiṉāṉ.”
pōr muṭivuṟṟapiṉ maṉṉaṉ kaṅkiyiṉ teṉkariya ṭintāṉ. aṅkē ōr aḻakiya pūṅkāviṉarukil karṇarkaḷ aracaṉukkāka oru ciṟanta araṇmaṉiyiyum, uyar paṇit talivarkaḷum, paṭit talivarkaḷum, taṅkuvataṟkāṉa pala māṭa māḷikikaḷiyum, kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷiyum, kaṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. iv viṭattil vantu maṉṉaṉ pōril vīrac ceyalāṟṟiyavarkaḷukkup paricukaḷum paṭṭaṅkaḷum, pukaḻuṭaṉ māṇṭa vīrarkaḷiṉ piḷḷi kaḷukku naṉmatippuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ.
muppattiraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷ veḷināṭṭil kaḻittataṉpiṉ, maṉṉaṉ vañcikku mīṇṭāṉ. araciyum kuṭikaḷum kōlā kalattuṭaṉ avaṉi varavēṟṟaṉar.
kaṅkik kariyiliruntē cēraṉ āriya aracar kaṉaka vicayar kaḷit taṉ kāvaṟpaṭiyuṭaṉ pāṇṭiya cōḻa aracarkaḷukkuk kaṇ kāṭkiyāka aṉuppiyiruntāṉ. kāvaṟpaṭit talivaṉ nīlaṉ kāvirippaṭṭiṉattukkum maturikkum ceṉṟapiṉ vañcikkut tirumpiṉāṉ.
“irakkattukkuriya āriya aracarkaḷ naṭattappaṭṭa vaki mikak koṭumi vāyntateṉṟu cōḻa pāṇṭiya aracar kaṇṭippatāka nīlaṉ maṉṉaṉiṭam kūṟiṉāṉ. tamiḻaracariṉ ivvuri kēṭṭu maṉṉaṉ maṉam kumuṟiṉāṉ.”
“appōtu aracaṉ muṉṉiliyil irunta pārppaṉaṉ māṭalaṉ maṉṉaṉiṭam ivvāṟu pēciṉāṉ.”
“maṉṉavar maṉṉa! uṉ veṟṟi nīṭuka!”
“kāṭṭāṉikaḷ miḷakuc ceṭiyiṉ niḻalil uṟaṅkum viyalūri nī veṉṟirukkiṟāy. nēri vāyiliṉ kaṭum pōril nī oṉpatu cōḻari muṟiyaṭittirukkiṟāy. iṭumpilil nī peṟṟa veṟṟi peritu. ippōtu mākaṭal kaṭantuceṉṟu nī kaṅkik kariyil uṉṉi etirtta āriya aracari muṟiyaṭittirukkiṟāy.”
“veṟṟip paṭiyiyuṭiya vēntaṉē! aṟivārnta maṉṉaṉē! iṉi nī ciṉantaṇivāyāka. poruniyāṟṟiṉ maṇaliṉum pala āṇṭu nī vāḻvāyāka.”
“eṉ coṟkaḷi ēḷaṉam ceyyātu ciṟitu cevicāyattuk kēṭpāyāka.”
“ippōtu nī aimpatu āṇṭuṭiyāy. āyiṉum ituvari nī uṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum pōrilēyē kaḻittiruk kiṟāy. ettakiya tiruvēḷviyum nī ceytatilli.”
“uṉ paḻami vāynta vīra marapil iṟavātu piḻittōr yāvarum illi. kaṭal naṭuvil kaṭampi eṟintavaṉumcari, imayamalimītu viṟpoṟi patittavaṉum cari; taṉṉip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pārppaṉaṉ vēḷvikaḷic ceyvatiṉ mūlam tuṟakka maṭiyumpaṭi ceytavaṉum cari; kūṟṟuvaṉik kaṭantavaṉum cari, yavaṉar nāṭāṇṭu taṅkam viḷivikkum malikaḷi aṭinta vaṉum cari; vīrappaṭi celutti akappā aḻittavaṉum cari, ayiriyiṉ nīrilum iru kaṭalilum kuḷittōṉumcari; talinakar vañciyil nāṟpūtaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu niṟuvi vēḷvikaḷi naṭattiṉa vaṉum cari-ellārumē māṇṭu maṭintupōyuḷḷaṉar!”
“’celvamum āṭciyum nillā’ eṉpataṉi nī aṟivāy. tamiḻarkaḷip paḻitta āriya aracar tōlviyum ikaḻum aṭintatu nīyē kaṇṭa kāṭci. iḷamiyum nillātu eṉṟu nāṉ kūṟa vēṇṭiyatilli. uṉ mayir narikkat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatē ataṉik kāṭṭum.”
“ivvāṟu toṭarntu, pārppaṉariṉ piṟappiṟappuk koḷkiyi valiyuṟutti viḷakki, irācacūyam eṉṉum māpeṟum vēḷviyic ceyyumpaṭiyum ataṉmūlam varupiṟappukkaḷil taṉ uyirniliyiṉ nalattip pēṇum paṭiyum māṭalaṉ aracaṉi vēṇṭiṉāṉ.”
“maṉṉaṉ itaṉpaṭiyē naṭantukoṇṭāṉ. mikapperiya aḷavil irācacūyam naṭantuvataṟkuriya ēṟpāṭukaḷ toṭaṅkum paṭiyum, attuṭaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kōyil eṭukkumpaṭiyum uṭaṉē kaṭṭaṉikaḷ piṟappikkappaṭṭaṉa.”
“iv viḻākkaḷil koṅka maṉṉarum, māḷava maṉṉarum ilaṅkiyaracaṉ kayavākuvum kalantukoṇṭaṉar.”
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉakkup piṉ yāṉikkaṇ-atāvatu yāṉiyiṉ kaṇpōṉṟa kaṇkaḷi uṭiyavaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭap peyaruṭiya cēy aracurimi ēṟṟāṉ. avaṉ vīramikka aracaṉ. ayalaracarkaḷukku ōyāttolli koṭuttukkoṇṭu avaṉ taṉ āṭciyiṉ ellippuṟaṅkaḷilēyē cuṟṟik koṇṭirun tāṉ. ippaṭi naṭavaṭikkiyiṉ payaṉākavē pāṇṭiya aracaṉ iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ cēranāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭi yeṭuttup pōrttiṟac cūḻccikaḷāl avaṉic ciṟip piṭippatil veṟṟi yaṭintāṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.13 āyiṉum cēraṉ tappic ceṉṟu mīṇṭum taṉ āṭciyi valuppaṭuttik koṇṭāṉeṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.
cōḻa aracaṉ irācacūyamvēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum yāṉikkaṭcēyuṭaṉ aṭikkaṭi pōriṭṭatāka aṟikiṟōm. malāṭṭukkōmāṉ tērvaṇmaliyaṉ ippōril cōḻaṉukkut tuṇi tantatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu.14
pakivarkaḷiṭamiruntu viḷaṅkil eṉṟa nakarik kāttatāka ivvaracaṉippaṟṟik kuṟuṅkōḻiyūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavar pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷār. avar pāṭalakaḷil oṉṟu varumāṟuṣa:-
“uyarnta kollimalikku iṟivaṉē! vīramikka yāṉik kaṭcēey! uṉ paṭi vīṭukaḷ kāval etuvum tēviyillātapaṭi avvaḷavu akalap parappuṭiyaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa. aṅkē kuṭittu viruntāṭṭayarum uṉ vīrarkaḷiṉ āravāram karimītu mōtum kaṭaliṉ muḻakkam pōṉṟatu.”
“kuṭiyaracar celuttiya tiṟikoṇṭu nī uṉṉic cārntavar kaḷi ūṭṭukiṟāy. uṉ variyaṟṟa celvam eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavuka.”
 “uṉṉip pāṭum pulavarkaḷ piṉ vēṟu yāriyum pāṭa vēṇṭuvatilli. avarkaḷ eṉṟum vaṟumiyuṟāta aḷavu nī kivaṇmiyuṭiyāy.”
“uṉṉāl āḷappaṭṭa nāṭu nilavulakil oru poṉṉāṭu pōṉṟatu eṉṟu kēḷviyuṟṟu nāṉ iṅkē vantēṉ. eṉ kaṇkaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻcciyaṭikiṉṟaṉa.”
“eṉṟum ceyalūkkamuṭiya nī uṉ paṭikaḷi eppōtum veḷināṭukaḷil celutti iṟavāp pukaḻiyum veṉṟuviṭa eṇṇūkiṟāy!”
ivvaracaṉ māḷviṉpōtu uṭaṉirunta pulavar kūṭalkiḻār an nikaḻccikku ēḻu nāṭkaḷukku muṉpē, an nikaḻcciyi ōr oḷimikka erimīṉ vīḻcci kuṟittuk kāṭṭiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. it taṟuvāyil avar pāṭiya pāṭṭu taṉicciṟappuṭaṉ nammāl kavaṉikkattakkatu. ēṉeṉil pārppaṉariṉ vēṟākat tamiḻarukku vāṉūlārāycci uṇṭu eṉpatiyum, nāḷmīṉkaḷukkum16 vāṉaka maṉikaḷukkum17 taṉittamiḻp peyarkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatiyum atu teḷivupaṭuttukiṟatu.18
“kuṭṭanāḷaṉṟu (kārttiki nāḷ), katiravaṉ āṭṭiṉ maṉiyil (mēṭattil) irunta camayam naḷḷiravil muṭappaṉi (aṉurātā) mutal viṇmīṉiliruntu kuḷam (puṉarvacu) iṟuti viṇmīṉvari vāṉattil kāṭciyaḷikka paṅkuṉi mātam mutaṟpātiyil uccattilirunta nāḷ mīṉ uccattiliruntu cāyntu vara, eṭṭāvatu nāḷ mīṉ eḻuntataṉ piṉṉum eṭṭavātu nāḷ mīṉ aṭivataṟku muṉṉum vāṉamuḻuvatum oḷivīciya ōr erimīṉ kāṟṟiṉ etirtticiyil aṉaṟpoṟi citaṟiyavaṇṇam vaṭakiḻakku nōkki viḻuntatu. itik kaṇṭa nāṉum maṟṟap pāṇarkaḷum aruvikaḷ niṟinta vaḷamikka nāṭṭiṉ vēntaṉ māḷviliruntu kāppāṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu uḷamāra vēṇṭikkoṇṭavarkaḷāy, ivvaccantarun tīkkuṟiyiṉ payaṉit tuyaramikka uḷḷattu ṭaṉ etirnōkkiyiruntōm.”
“”ēḻām nāḷ kāli muṭivu nāḷ vantu nērntatu. etirikaḷic ciṟippaṭuttuvatil vallavaṉum īkiyil vaṇmiyuṭiyava ṉumāṉa aracaṉ ippōtu vāṉavar ulakam ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ pāriya yāṉi tumpikkiyi nilattil nīṭṭik kiḷarcciyaṟṟuk kiṭakkiṉṟatu. avaṉ periya muracaṅkaḷ kavaṉippāraṟṟu nilattiṉmītu puraḷkiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ veṇkoṟṟak kuṭi piṭiyiṉṟivaṟitē kiṭakkiṟatu. avaṉuṭiya virinaṭik kutirikaḷ vāṭṭattuṭaṉ koṭṭilil niṟkiṉṟaṉa. antō, vāṉavar makaḷir tuṇiyi nāṭiyō em aracaṉ taṉ aṉpukkuriya araciyari maṟantuviṭṭāṉ!”
yāṉikkaṭcēykkup piṉ peruñcēralirumpoṟi aracaṉāṉāṉ. mōcikīraṉār eṉṉum pulavar avaṉ aṉpukku riyavar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. orunāḷ araṇmaṉiyiṉ orupuṟattē mettikaḷiṭṭup putumalarpparappiya paṭukki oṉṟu kiṭakkak kaṇṭu, kaviñar atil ciṟitu cāyntu, cāyntavaṇṇam uṟaṅki viṭṭār. avvaḻicella nērnta aracaṉ kaviñar cōrvuṟṟu viyarttukkiṭappatu kaṇṭu, aṅkirunta oru viciṟi eṭuttu avarukku viciṟiṉāṉ. pulavar eḻuntu aracaṉ taṉakku viciṟuvatu kaṇṭu tiṭukkiṭṭār.
paṭukki uṇmiyil aracaṉ pōrmuracattukkuriya tiruvirukki eṉṟu itaṉ piṉṉarē uṇarnta pulavar kīḻvarum pāṭalip pāṭiṉār.19
“naṟu neyyiṉ nuripōla melliya malarkaḷ parappappaṭṭa intap paṭukkiyik kaṇṭu, atu nīṇṭa mayiliṟakukaḷāl uḻiñiyiṉ poṉ malarkaḷ piṇitta maṇimālikaḷālum aṇivikkappaṭṭuk karuṅkaṇkaḷuṭaṉ aḻakuruvaminta muracattuk kuriyateṉṟu aṟiyāmal atil paṭuttuk kaṇṇayarntuviṭṭēṉ. inta eṉatu tuṭukkut taṉattukkāka nī eṉṉi iru tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭiyirukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl nīyō uṉ vāḷi uṟiyiliruntu uruviṉāy illi. ituvē peritu.”
“tamiḻ nilameṅkum uṉ pukaḻ parappa itu pōtāteṉṟu nī uṉ valimi mikka kikaḷāl tuyilum eṉ neṟṟi kuḷira viciṟiṉāy.”
“naṟceyalkaḷiṉ pukaḻāl ivvulaki niṟittavarkaḷukkē poṉṉulakiṉ anta iṉpavāyil tiṟakkappaṭum eṉṟa uṇmiyi aṟintataṉāl pōlum aruntiṟal vēntaṉākiya nī ivvāṟu ceytāy? eṉṟār.”
avaṉ kuṭimaṉṉaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa eḻiṉi avaṉuk ketirākak kiḷarnteḻuntataṉāl, avaṉ oru perum paṭi tiraṭṭik koṇṭu ceṉṟu avaṉatu takaṭūrkkōṭṭiyi muṟṟukiyiṭṭāṉ. eḻiṉi, atikamāṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭiya mikap paḻamiyāṉa vēḷir kuṭittalivaṉ. avarkaḷ talinakaramākiya takaṭūr cēlamāvaṭ ṭattiluḷḷa taṟkālat tarumapuri eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. eḻiṉi akkōṭṭiyic ceppam ceytu valimip paṭuttit taṉiyāṭciyurimi peṟuvatu allatu ammuyaṟciyil maṭivatu eṉṟu uṟutikoṇṭāṉ. āṉāl cēra maṉṉaṉ nakara muḻuvatum vaḷittu veḷittoṭarpiliruntu atit tuṇṭupaṭuttiṉāṉ.
nakaric cuṟṟiyirunta muṭkāṭu cēra vīrarāl aḻikkappaṭṭatu. akaḻi tūrkkappaṭṭatu. kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷ yāṉikaḷāl piḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. cērarpaṭi kōṭṭikkuḷ pukuntu pāyntatu. uḷḷē naṭanta caṇṭiyil eḻiṉiyum avaṉ paṭittalivar kaḷum vīra aruñceyalkaḷ pala ceytu, etiriyiṉ peruntoki kāraṇamākavē iṟutivari pōriṭṭu ovvoruvarāka maṭittārkaḷ.
muṟṟukiyiṉpōtu aricilkiḻār, poṉmuṭiyār eṉṟa pulavarkaḷ cēra aracaṉ paṭivīṭṭiliruntārkaḷ. iṉṟuvari namakku eṭṭiyuḷḷa avarkaḷ pāṭal pakutikaḷ kōṭṭit tākkutaliṉ cila nikaḻccikaḷi namakku uyirccittiraṅkaḷākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cērar paṭikaḷ takaṭūri aṇūkiyapōtu, eḻiṉiyum avaṉ paṭittalivaṉ perumpākkaṉum kōṭṭikku veḷiyē avarkaḷic cantikkat taṅkaḷ paṭikaḷi veḷiyē koṇṭu vantaṉar. perumpākkaṉ tākkutali etirnōkkit taṉ paṭiyiṉ muṉpu niṉṟāṉ. pulavar aricilkiḻārum poṉmuṭiyārum cēra aracaṉukku avaṉic cuṭṭi kāṭṭi, avaṉ avarkaḷi nōkki ēḷaṉac cirippuc cirippati eṭutturittārkaḷ.20
“mālikaḷiyaṇinta anta vīra iḷiñaṉ ūkkamikunta kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya miṉṉoḷi pāyum aḻakiyatēril ēṟik koṇṭu, nam paṭiyi nōkkit taṉ valimiyil makiḻnta vaṉāy niṟkiṟāṉ. avaṉ veṟi koṇṭu nammit tākkiṉāl, vīṟumikka matayāṉikaḷik kūṭa avaṉāl vīḻttamuṭiyakkūṭum. tākkutali etirnōkkiyavaṇṇam miṉṉiṭum taṉvēli vīciyapaṭi avaṉ taṉ etirikaḷik kaṇṭu nakittu niṟkiṟāṉ.”
“karuntāṭiyuṭiya anta añcā neñcukoṇṭa vīra iḷiñaṉ taṉ pōrkkutirikaḷiṉ kaṭivāḷaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya vaṇṇam oru taṭavi pūrikkum taṉ tōḷkaḷip pārkkiṟāḷ. piṉ taṉ piṉṉē aṇivakattu niṟkum yāṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṇālaḷakkiṟāṉ. yāṉikaḷukkupiṉṉē niṟkum tērvarici kaḷi nōkkukiṟāṉ. taṉ pōrkkutirikaḷi nōkkukiṟāṉ. piṉ paṟantuvarum ampukaḷiyum taṉ kiyiluḷḷa vēliyum kiyiyum pārttu nammi nōkkic cirikkiṟāṉ.”
mutal nāḷ pōril eḻiṉiyiṉ paṭikaḷ kōṭṭikkuḷ turattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭām nāḷil kōṭṭiyic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa muṭkāṭu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ‘muṟṟukiyiṉ vēkampaṟṟi eṉṉa karutukiṟīrkaḷ’ eṉṟu cēra aracaṉ ac camayam pulavarkaḷiṭam karuttucāva, avarkaḷ niliyip pāṭalkaḷil tīṭṭiṉar.21
“nēṟṟu eḻiṉiyiṉ kutirikaḷ māṉkaḷpōl tuḷḷi yōṭiṉa. yāṉikaḷ malikaḷpōl cōrvuṟṟuc cāyntaṉa. cīṟivarum ampumārikaḷāl maṟa vīrarkaḷ kalakalattuk kalintaṉar. pōril putuppuṇpaṭṭu acciṉṉaṅkaḷi uṭalil aṇintuḷḷa aracaṉē! iṉṟu kollappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ ampēṟuṇṭa talikaḷ nam yāṉikaḷāl eṟṟuṇṭu, pātikaḷiṉ arukellām piṟanta paṉampaḻaṅkaḷ pōlak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. cōrvaṟiyāta nam karuṅkaṇ iḷiñarkaḷ kāṭṭiyaḻittu āravārittavaṇṇam etirikaḷik kaṇṭa kaṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷilellām avarkaḷik koṉṟaḻikkiṉṟaṉar.”
“nāṉi muracu muḻaṅka nām kōṭṭikkuḷ celvōm.”
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

3.	cilappatikāram  ōōaiō.

6.	“mūlattiṉ col nayatti moḻipeyarppil kāṭṭuvataritāṉālum ataṉ poruḷ nayam iṅkē tarappaṭukiṟatu” eṉṟa vācakattuṭaṉ ivviṭattil puṟam 8ām pāṭaliṉ āṅkila moḻi peyarppu mūlanūlil tarappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻākkattil tamiḻ mūlamē nūlil tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. moḻipeyarppu varumāṟu.
	“kṣacipāva ṭicab vāyava ayacuhnaḷava yīcaṭiraṉaṭala in vāna ḷamala!
	kṣanāiṉē vāna hṭiṭaḷa vāṭira hṉinaḷava kacaṭiaṭiraca ḷipāva,
	haṉē vāṭira yacava ḷananan le ṉayala yaṉē nē’naca le nipāva
	kūhṭirapā ḷaṭiyacipē in vāna ḷamala ḷaṭi ṟaṉina yaṉē hipā,
	haṭaṭinē vāṭira ṉayacanaḷava nṭiva vaṭi ḷāṭiṟa vāla kayuna,
	kṣarava uṭianaḷava parayacaṉanaṉa le ya vāṭiraḷayaṉē cayalaḷa!
	ḻaṭiṟa uyaḷēva vāṭira eni ṟivā ūhnacayaṭa havāyan bṭiṭaṉa;
	ṟuhṭi ūṭiravēṭanaḷaḷa uhnikaḷa in kanayaṭavala ṉaṭivā hṭiṭaṉa,
	ṟuhṭi manṭiṟaḷa nṭi kanayaca ṭin vāna yevavaṭana kaniṭaṉa,
	haṉē uṭiravēḷa nṭi uṭiḷava vāna ṟanayama yaṉē yīṭiṭica vaṭi ḷāniṭaṉa,
	ṟuhṭi ḷuṭicaḷē ṉieṉinaṉa naayīcina kaṭica hḷi ḷaṟayala,
	haṉē ṭanayaṉaḷa vāna ṭanapiṭiḷē vāyava ḷaavina yaṉē ḷaṭayala?”
7.	cilappatikāram ōōō, aṭikaḷ 171, toṭarcci.
