8. vēḷirum kuṭimaṉṉarum
pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka muṭiyuṭiya vēntarkaḷāyt tamiḻakattil cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṟa aracarkaḷ maṭṭumē iruntuvantaṉar. itaṉ payaṉākavē avarkaḷ oru tokippaṭa mūvēntarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum tamiḻakattilē mikap paranta pakutikaḷi āṇṭa palvēṟu vēḷirum kuṭimaṉṉarum iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ perumpālum mūvaracaruḷ yārēṉum oruvarukkuk kuṭiyurimippaṭṭavarākavē viḷaṅkiṉar. avvappōtu avarkaḷil cilar taṅkaḷ mēlāṭciyit tūkki eṟintuviṭṭut taṉiyurimiyil maṉṉarāka muyaṉṟatuṇṭu. āṉāl avarkaḷ mika virivil kīḻaṭakkappaṭṭārkaḷ.
kuṭi maṉṉaruḷ mutalāvatākak kūṟak kūṭiyavaṉ tiriyaṉ. avaṉ tiriyar atāvatu kaṭalaracar kuṭicārntavaṉ. im marapiyē cōḻarum taṅkaḷ kuṭimarapākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. pukaḻ peṟṟa karikālacōḻaṉ ciṟuvaṉāyirunta kālattil kāñcit tiriyaṉ cōḻa araciyē kippaṟṟi ataṉmītu nīṇṭanāḷ āṭci ceytāṉ. tiriyaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭappeṟṟa perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyiṉ mūlam ivvaracaṉ palvēl tiriyaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattiyum toṇṭimāṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattiyum tāṅkiyiruntāṉeṉṟum perumpukaḻ vīraṉeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm.1
tiriyaṉ uṭaṉaṭiyākat taṉ āṭcip pīṭattic cōḻar kaḷiṉ paṇṭittalinakarāṉa uṟiyūrukku māṟṟik koṇṭi runtāl, avaṉ cōḻa aracurimiyil toṭarntu nīṭittut taṉ marapiṉarukkē ati viṭṭucceṉṟirukka muṭiyum. āṉāl avaṉ kāñciyilēyē taṅkiyiruntāṉ. ākavē karikālaṉ taṉ muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ aracirukkiyip peṟuvatil taṭṅkal mikuti illātu pōyiṟṟu.
cōḻarukku uṭpaṭṭa piṟa kuṭimaṉṉar vēṅkaṭam, malāṭu, miḻalikkūṟṟam ākiyavaṟṟi āṇṭaṉar. vēṅkaṭat talivarkaḷuḷ pulli eṉpavaṉ māmūlaṉār,2 kallil āttiriyaṉār eṉṟa pulavarkaḷuṭaṉ camakālattavaṉ.3
ivvēḷir eppōtum tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭelliyaṭut tirunta vaṭukaruṭaṉ ōyātu pōrilīṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar.
malāṭṭiṉ kōmāṉkaḷiṉ marapup peyar maliyamāṉ eṉpatu maliyamāṉ āṭcip pakutiyil mukkiya nakaram peṇṇi yāṟṟiṉ kariyil taṟpōtu tirukkōyilūr eṉṟaḻikkappaṭum kōval ākum4. muḷḷūrmali eṉṟa akalapparappuṭiya ceḻippāṉa mēṭṭu nilappakuti avaṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭiruntatu. iṅkē kuṭiyēṟiyirunta pala pārppaṉarkaḷukku maliyamāṉ kāri nilaṅkaḷi māṉiyamākak koṭuttāṉ. avarkaḷil perun tokiyiṉar appakkam vantu tiraṇṭu vāḻnta kāraṇattāl, tamiḻk kaviñar ataṉi ‘āriyar nirampiya pukaḻ vāynta muḷḷūr’ eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar5.  pārppaṉap pāṭakarkaḷukku vaḷḷaṉmiyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkiya kāriyip pārppaṉak kaviñar kapilar kīḻvarumāṟu pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
“vīrakkaḻalaṇinta kāri! uṉ nāṭu muttī pēṇum pārp paṉarukkuriyatu. atik kaṭal koḷḷa muṭiyātu. etiri kippaṟṟa muṭiyātu. mūvaracaruḷ oruvar kuṭi eṉṟavakiyil avarkaḷ uṉakkut tarum paricukaḷiyellām nī uṉ kuṭipukaḻum pāṇarka ḷukkuk koṭuttuviṭukiṟāy. vaṭamīṉ pōṉṟa kaṟpuṟutiyuṭiya uṉ tuṇivi tavira, vēṟu uṉ uṭimi eṉa nī etuvum vittukkoḷḷavilli. āyiṉum nī kuṟivaṟṟa iṉpamuṭi yavaṉāy irukkiṟāy.”6
iṉṉoru kaviñar kīḻvarum pāṭalkaḷāl avar ātaravu kōriṉār : 
“etirikaḷiṉ yāṉikaḷuṭiya neṟṟikaḷi aṇi ceyta poṟpaṭāṅkaḷiṉ takaṭukaḷip paṟittu avaṟṟāl poṉṉālāṉa tāmarippūkkaḷ puṉintu pāṇarkaḷiṉ taliyil cūṭṭiyavaṉ vīramarapil vantavaṉē! aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṉūṭāka aruvikaḷ cevikaḷit tuḷikkum āravārattuṭaṉ viḻum muḷḷūr maliyiṉ vīṟuṭik kōmāṉē!”
“uṉṉiyum uṉ kuṭimarapiyum pāṭi uṉ peyari iṟavāp peyarākkiyuḷḷa aṟivārnta pārppaṉaṉiviṭa (kapilaṉiviṭa) aṟiviṟ ciṟantavaṉ ulakil illi. avaṉ pāṭṭiṉ ciṟappukkuppiṉ maṟṟappāṇar uṉṉip pāṭuvataṟku vaḻiyaṟṟavarāyviṭṭārkaḷ.”  
“vāṉavaṉ (cēraṉ) karikkut taṅkam cumantuvarum kappalkaḷ ulavum mēlik kaṭalilē vēṟu enta marakkalamum cella muṭiyātu atupōla (kapilar pāṭiya) uṉṉip pāṭavaki yaṟiyātu tikikkiṉṟōm.” 
“yāṉikaḷuṭaṉum, iṭiyeṉa muḻaṅkum muracaṅkaḷuṭaṉum pōrceyyavanta māṟṟaracarkaḷic citaṟaṭitta peṇṇit tīrattiṉ kāvala! vaṟumiyāl untappaṭṭu, uṉ pukaḻāl īrkkap paṭṭu uṉ aruḷnāṭi varukiṉṟōm.”7
kāri kollimalit talivaṉ ōriyuṭaṉ pōruṭaṟṟiṉāṉ. avaṉip pōril koṉṟu, kollimaliyi ataṉ nēriya urimiyāḷaṉāṉa cēraṉukkē tantāṉ8. maṟṟa vēḷirkaḷi veṉṟa veṟṟikaḷāl ekkaḷippuṟṟu avaṉ taṉiyurimiyuṭaṉ aracāḷa eṇṇi muṭiyaṇintukoṇṭatākat terikiṟatu. itaṉālēyē avaṉ ‘tirumuṭikkāri’ atāvatu muṭiyēṟṟa kāri eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭāṉ.
muṭiyēṟṟa cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ avaṉ talikkaṉatti aṭakka eṇṇiya cōḻa aracaṉ oru perumpaṭiyuṭaṉ malāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttu veṅkurutip pōroṉṟil kāriyi muṟiyaṭittukkoṉṟāṉ.
cōḻaṉ kāriyiṉ putalvarkaḷik koṉṟu maliyamāṉ kuṭimarapi aḻittuviṭa eṇṇiyiruntāṉ. āṉāl kōvūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavariṉ iṭiyīṭṭiṉāl avarkaḷ uyirkaḷ kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.9
iṅṅaṉam māḷviliruntu kāppāṟṟappeṟṟa kāriyiṉ putalvaṉ kaṇṇaṉ taṉ vāḻval cōḻa aracaṉukkuc ceyta kimmāṟu ciṟitaṉṟu ēṉeṉil, cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷḷēyē cōḻaṉ etirikaḷāl mikavum nerukkuṇṭu talinakari viṭṭōṭi aṇūkamuṭiyā uyaramuṭiya muḷḷūr maliyil, taṉ kuṭimaṉṉaṉ kaṇṇaṉ āṭcip pakutiyilēyē aṭikkalam pukavēṇṭi vantatu.
tantiyippōlavē vīrañceṟinta kaṇṇaṉ maṉṉar ātaravāḷariṉ uḷaccōrvakaṟṟik kalinta paṭikaḷiyum tiraṭṭiṉāṉ. etirikaḷi mīṭṭum etirttu muṭiyaṭittuc cōḻaṉ valimiyi maṟupaṭiyum nilināṭṭiṉāṉ. innaṉṟi maṟavātu cōḻamaṉṉaṉ kaṇṇaṉiyē taṉ amiccaṉākki avaṉukkuc cōḻiya ēṉāti eṉṟa uyarpaṭṭam vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ10.
miḻalik kūṟṟatticcērnta pukaḻcāṉṟa talivaṉ māvēḷ evvi. avaṉ vēḷāḷar marapiṉaṉ. avaṉ kuṭimarapu āyirattu eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa annāḷilkūṭap paḻami cāṉṟatu eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu11. avaṉ āṭcip pakuti kāviri yāṟṟiṉ teṉpakutiyiliruntatu. atu nellum karumpum virivākap payirākiya vaḷamikka kaḻaṉikaḷ nirampiya nāṭu. 
avaṉ vaḷḷaṉmiyiṉ payaṉil paṅkupeṟṟa oru pulavar avaṉippaṟṟi ivvāṟu kūṟukiṟār.
“avaṉ māḷiki aṭiyāta vāyil uṭiyatu. viruntiṉar palarōṭaṉṟi avaṉ eṉṟum uṇavuṇṇa amarntatilli.”12
avaṉ nāṭṭi ālaṅkāṉam veṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ paṭittalivarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāṉa atuki paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. evvi taṉpaṭikaḷiṉ talimiyil vīramākap pōrceytu, pōril peṟṟa puṇkaḷāl māṇṭāṉ.13
evviyiṉ uṟaviṉaṉāṉa vēḷpāri paṟampu eṉṟa oru ciṟu nilappakutiyiṉ talivaṉ. āṉāl pulavarkaḷukku ātaravaḷitta māperum puravalaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil tamiḻ moḻiyuḷḷaḷavum avaṉ peyar tamiḻar niṉivil maṅkāmal nilipeṟattakkatu. evviyiṉ vīḻccikkuppiṉ pāri uyarnta paṟampu maliyiyum ataṉic cūḻnta nilaṅkaḷiyum kikkoṇṭu mūvaracarkaḷil evariyum mēlāḷāka ēṟkāmal taṉiyāṭciyāḷaṉāka naṭakkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ.
avaṉ taṅku taṭiyaṟṟu aruñceyal vēṭṭattil muṉinta vīramaṟavaṉ. atē camayam kavaliyaṟṟa iṉpa vāḻviṉaṉ. tūya uḷḷamum vaḷḷaṉmiyumuṭiyavaṉ. kavitiyil avaṉ ārvam peritāyiruntatu. nāṭōṭiyāka vanta ovvoru pāṇaṉum avaṉ araṇmaṉiyil aṉpuṭaṉ ātavu peṟṟu viruntaḷikkap peṟṟāṉ.
añcā neñcam, vaḷḷaṇmi, enta iṭarilum kalaṅkātu makiḻntu niṟkum vīṟuvaki-kaviñarkaḷ tam pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉukku iruppatākap pukaḻntu pāṭum paṇpukaḷ oṉṟu kuṟiyāmal avaṉiṭam iṇintiruppati avarkaḷ kaṇṭārkaḷ. pāriyiṉ ippaṇpukaḷik kaviti moḻikaḷil pukaḻntum avaṉ koṭittiṟatti viyantu pārāṭṭiyum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar avikkaḷaṅkaḷilēyē pulavarkaḷ pāṭiṉar. itu mūvaracar poṟāmiyik kiḷaṟiṟṟu. avarkaḷ paṟampu maliya muṟṟuki yiṭat tam paṭikaḷi aṉuppiṉārkaḷ.
pāriyiṉ tuṇiyāṭkaḷukku malikkaṇavāykaḷiṉ mēṭupaḷḷaṅkaḷ yāvum naṉku terintiruntaṉa. avaṟṟi avarkaḷ tiṟampaṭak kāttuvantārkaḷ. pōril iru tiṟaṅkaḷiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu peritāyiruntālum cila nāṭkaḷ pāri paṭikkala vāyppilum tokiyilum mikavum mēmpaṭṭa etirikaḷit taṉ taṉi vīrattāl kāttu niṉṟāṉ. āṉāl iṟutiyil etirikaḷ paṭippaṭiyāka malimītu pōriṭṭu muṉṉēṟi, pāriyi muṟiyaṭittuk koṉṟaṉar.14
pāriyiṉ keḻutaki naṇparāṉa kapilar mūvaracar paṭikaḷum paṟampi muṟṟukiyiṭṭa camayam kīḻvarum pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉār.
“mūvaracarum tam oṉṟupaṭṭa paṭikaḷuṭaṉ muṟṟuki yiṭṭālum, paṟampuṭiliyi velvatu aritu. vaḷamikka paṟampu nāṭṭiṉ ūrkaḷ munnūṟu uṇṭu. munnūṟum ippōtu pulavar kaḷukku uriyaṉa. nāṉum pāriyumtāṉ iṅkē irukkiṟōm. eṅkaḷ paṟampuliyum itō irukkiṟatu. pāṇarāka eṅkaḷip pāṭa varuvīrāṉāl, vāruṅkaḷ.”15
“pārikkuriya paṟampi vellavatu eḷiteṉṟā eṇṇū kiṟīrkaḷ?” muḻaṅkum muracuṭiya mūvaracarum muṟṟuki yiṭṭālum, akkālattilum atu uḻaviṉṟiyē naṉku māvaḷaṅkaḷ tarum. mutalatu melliliyuṭiya mūṅkil tarum arici. iraṇṭāvatu palāmaraṅkaḷ tarum iṉiya kaṉikaḷ. mūṉṟāvatu vaḷḷikkoḻuṅ koṭikaḷ tarum iṉiya kiḻaṅkukaḷ. nāṉkāvatu tēṉ kūṭukaḷiṉ mītu mantikaḷ tāvuvataṉāl kuṉṟukaḷil vaḻintōṭum tēṉ.”
“vāṉaḷāva vaḷarntakaṉṟatu paṟampumali ucci. atiluḷḷa naṟunīrc cuṉikaḷō vāṉattu mīṉkaḷpōla mikappala. ovvoru paṇṇiyilum oru tēriyum, ovvoru marattarukilum oru yāṉiyiyum nīṅkaḷ kāval vittālum, uṅkaḷ vīrarāl ati muṟṟukiyiṭamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ vāḷkaḷ ataṉ vaḻiyākap pāti uṇṭupaṇṇi muṉṉēṟavum iyalātu.”
“maliyik kippaṟṟum vakiyi nāṉ aṟivēṉ. ciṟiya yāḻiṉ narampukaḷi uḷarntu iṉṉiciyeḻuppi, uṅkaḷ peṇṭiruṭaṉ āṭippāṭi varuvīrāṉāl, avaṉ uṅkaḷukkut taṉ nilaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum maliyiyum taruvāṉ.”16
pāriyiṉ vīramikka uṟaviṉarkaḷilē avaṉ pātukāppi lēyē ovvoruvarum vīḻntupaṭṭaṉar. avaṉ muṟiyaṟṟa maṭivukku varunta avaṉ iru putalviyarkaḷē mīntaṉar. avaṉ celvattiyē tam celvamākak koṇṭu vāḻnta pāṇar kulam iṟantupaṭṭa tam talivaṉukkāka vāyviṭṭaraṟṟiṟṟu. tuṇiyaṟṟut tavitta iru putalviyar kaṇṇīruṭaṉ avarkaḷ kaṇṇīr kalantatu.
pāriyiṉ aṉpukkuriya pāṇar kapilar pāriyiṉ putalviyar kaḷip pēṇum poṟuppi mēṟkoṇṭār. paṟampiviṭṭup piriyum camayam avar ammaliyi nōkkik kīḻvarumāṟu pulampiṉār :-
“māṇpumikka paṟampē! nī orukāl eṅkaḷ aṉpukkuriya poruḷāka iruntatuṇṭu. appōtu matukkuṭaṅkaḷ tiṟantiruntaṉa. āṭukaḷ kollappaṭṭaṉa. nāṅkaḷ virumpumaḷavu uṇṇumpaṭi kaṟiyuṭaṉ camittacōṟu vaṭṭikkappaṭṭatu. ippōtu pāri iṟantuviṭṭataṉāl, tuṇiyaṟṟavarāy, tuyaruṟṟavarāy, kaṇkaḷi liruntu nīr aruviyāka oḻuka, nāṅkaḷ uṉṉiviṭṭu viṭi koṇṭu celkiṟōm. naṟuṅkūntaluṭaṉ vaḷiyaṇinta kiyiṉarāṉa pāriyiṉ putalviyarukkēṟṟa kaṇavarik kāṇūmāṟu celkiṉṟōm.”
ivvāṟu araṟṟiyavaṇṇa kapilar peṇkaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻakattiluḷḷa vēḷirkaḷuḷ viccikkō, iruṅkōvēḷ ākiya iruvariyum mutalil aṇūkiṉār. iruvarum avarkaḷi maṇantu koḷḷa maṟuttapiṉ avar avarkaḷip pārppaṉarkaḷukkē koṭuttār.17
ceyyāṟṟut tīrattil ceṅkaṇmāviṉ kōmāṉāṉa naṉṉaṉ cōḻa araciṉ iṉṉoru peyarpeṟṟa talivaṉ. peruṅkucikaṉār eṉṟa pulavar avaṉip pukaḻntu malipaṭukaṭām eṉṟa pāṭalip pāṭiyuḷḷār. ippāṭaliliruntu avaṉ āṭcip pakutiyil naviram eṉṟa mali uccimītu kāriyuṇṭik kaṭavuḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkiya civaperumāṉiṉ kōyil oṉṟu iruntatākat terikiṟatu.18
pāṇṭiya aracar mēlurimi ēṟṟa mukkiyamāṉa vēḷir āy, porunaṉ, paḻayaṉmāṟaṉ ākiyavarkaḷē.
ivarkaḷil māvēḷāy eṉpavaṉ vēḷāḷar marapiṉaṉ. potiyamalikkum ataṉic cūḻnta nilaṅkaḷukkum uriyavaṉ. avaṉ āṭcip pakutiyiṉ talinakar āykuṭi19. āykuṭiyi āṇṭavarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāṉa āy aṇṭiraṉ orukāl koṅkar kaḷi muṟiyaṭittu avarkaḷi mēlikkaṭalukkut turattiya tākat teriyavarukiṟatu.20 āy aṇṭiraṉiṉ piṉṉurimiyāḷaril oruvaṉāṉa titiyaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉi etirtta kūṭṭil oruvaṉākac cērttu ālaṅkāṉap pōril muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.21
porunaṉ eṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷiriṉ kuṭippeyar. itu potiyamaliyiṉ mēṟkuppuṟattiluḷḷatu.22 ippakuti iṉṟum nāñcil nāṭu eṉṟē aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu taṟkālat tiruvāṅkūr araciṉ oru pakutiyāy uḷḷatu.
mōkūrt talivaṉ paḻayaṉ māṟaṉ paṇṭi māṟar kuṭiyiṉ talivaṉ. avarkaḷ potiyamali yarukil kuṭiyēṟiya varkaḷ. pāṇṭiya aracar kuṭiyum immūla marapukkuriyatē. pāṇṭiyaṉ mutalām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āṭciyil paḻayaṉ māṟaṉ pāṇṭiya aracil maṉṉarkkaṭutta uyarmatippuṭi yavaṉā yiruntāṉ.23
cōḻa aracaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ perumpaṭiyuṭaṉ maturiyi muṟṟukiyiṭṭa camayam, paḻayaṉ māṟaṉ virinaṭik kutirivīrarum, veñcīṟṟamuṭiya yāṉikaḷum aṭaṅkiya oru perumpaṭiyuṭaṉ avaṉit tākkic cōḻarpaṭiyi muṟṟilum nilikuliyac ceytāṉ.24
cēraṉukku uṭpaṭṭa orutalivaṉi avamatittataṟkākap paḻayaṉ māṟaṉit taṇṭikka eṇṇi, cēra aracaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ avaṉ āṭcip pakutiyip paṭiyeṭuttut tākkiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.25
aḻumpil, katirimali, pāli, takaṭūr ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vēḷir cēra aracaṉukku uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ.
aḻumpil vēḷ aḻumpil nakariṉ talivaṉ. annakaram peritum kuṭṭanāṭṭic cērntatāyirukkavēṇṭum. pāṇṭiyaṉ mutalām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ cēranāṭṭip paṭiyeṭuttapōtu, aḻumpilvēḷi etirttuttākki muṟiyaṭittu avaṉ āṭcip pakuti muḻuvatiyum pāṇṭiya aracuṭaṉ cērttukkoṇṭāṉ.26
vallamimikka avvaracaṉāṭcikkuppiṉ aḻumpilvēḷ annilap pakutiyi mīṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aracavip perumakkaḷil avaṉ mīṭṭum talimi yiṭam vakikkakkāṇkiṟōm.27
kutirimalit talivaṉāṉa piṭṭaṉkoṟṟaṉ cērarpaṭit talivaṉāyiruntāṉ.28
vēṇmāṉ pāli vēḷiriṉ kuṭippeyar. pāli eṉpatu vināṭṭukkum vaṭakiḻakku malapārukkum vaṭakkē taṟpōtu kuṭaku eṉṟaḻikkappaṭum pakuti muḻuvatum uḷḷaṭakkiya curaṅkat toḻiṟpakutiyiluḷḷa araṇ amippuṭiya oru nakaram29. im maṇṭalattiluḷḷa vēṟu iraṇṭu nakaraṅkaḷ arayamum viyalūrum ākum.30
pālivēḷāṉa naṉṉaṉ vēḷmāṉ kolipurintavaṉ eṉṟa koṭumpeyarukku āḷāṉāṉ. ciṟu kuṟṟam ceyta oru ciṟu peṇṇukku, avaḷ uṟaviṉar avaḷ eṭiyiṉ oṉpatu maṭaṅku taṅkam maṉṉippīṭākat taruvatākak kūṟiyum kēḷāmal avaṉ avaḷik kolit taṇṭaṉikku uṭpaṭuttiṉāṉ.31
im marapukkuriya talivaṉ oruvaṉ cēra aracaṉ kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēralāl peruntuṟiyil naṭipeṟṟa pōril muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.32
atiyamāṉ maḻavarkuṭit talivaṉ, avaṉ iṉṟiya cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tarmapuri eṉṉum takaṭūr āṇṭavaṉ33. avaṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil karumpup payirik koṇṭu vantārkaḷ.34 cēra aracaṉ peruñcēralirum poṟiyiṉ āṭcik kālattil atiyamāṉ kuṭittalivaṉ neṭumāṉ añci eṉpavaṉ. avaṉ malāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu maliyamāṉ talinakarāṉa kōvalūric cūṟiyāṭiṉāṉ35. ittaṟuvāyil paraṇar eṉṉum pulavar  avaṉ vīrappukaḻ pāṭiṉār.
pēravāvum vīramum vāynta talivaṉātalāl, avaṉ taṉ āṭcip pakutiyi virivupaṭutta virumpiṉāṉ. kutirimali mēliruntu kaṇṭāl kaṇṇukkut terikiṟa ammali cūḻnta nāṭu muḻuvatum avaṉukkuttarac cēra aracaṉ muṉvariṉum, avaṉ amiyātu mēlum kōriṉāṉ. itaṉāl pōr mūṇṭatu. cēra aracaṉ paṭiyuṭaṉ avaṉi aṭakkap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. takaṭūr muṟṟukiyiṉpōtu neṭumāṉ añci paṭupuṇpaṭṭu virivil māṇṭāṉ. cēra aracaṉ peruñcēralirumpoṟi āṭciyi viritturikkumiṭattil im muṟṟuki paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.36
atiyamāṉ aviyil avaṉ pēraṉpukkum, matippukkumuriya pulavarāyirunta avviyār avaṉiyum avaṉ vīrariyum paṟṟi aṇṭiyayalilirunta talivarkaḷ koṇṭirunta accam kuṟittuk kīḻvarum pāṭalil viritturittuḷḷār :-
“etirikaḷiṉ kōṭṭikaḷit tākki muṉi maḻuṅkiya tantaṅkaḷiyuṭiya uṉ yāṉippaṭikaḷik kaṇṭavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōṭṭi vāyil katavukaḷip piṇikkum vallirumpuk kampikaḷip putuppikkiṉṟaṉar. kālaṭiyil tuvittaḻitta etirikaḷiṉ kuruti tōynta kuḷampukaḷiyuṭiya uṉ kutirip paṭikaḷik kaṇṭōr taṅkaḷ kōṭṭivāyilkaḷukkut tiṇṇiya muḷ marakkaṭṭikaḷāl vāyil vaḻiyi aṭikkap pārkkiṉṟaṉar. vāḷpuṇ ēṟṟa taḻumpukaḷ pala tāṅkiya uṭal kaḷiyuṭiya uṉ koṭuñceyal vīrarkaḷik kaṇṭōrellām tam amputtūṇiyiluḷḷa ampukaḷi vīṇākkātu pēṇukiṉṟaṉar.”
āṉālnīyō, tam kōṭṭivāyiliṉ uḷḷē uṉ paṭikaḷit taṭuttu niṟuttuvataṟkāka uṉ etirikaḷ ataṉ muṉ erikkum aiyaviyiṉ naccuppukiyip poruṭpaṭuttāmal muṉṉēṟik kūṟṟuvaṉpōla avarkaḷik kippaṟṟi māykkiṟāy.
“antō, aliyāṭum katirmaṇikaḷ niṟinta vayal kaḷiyuṭiya uṉ etiriyiṉ nilaṅkaḷi iṉip pātukāppavar yār?”
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

9. camūka vāḻvu
mūvaracarkaḷiyum avarkaḷukkuk kīḻ uḷḷa vēḷirkaḷiyum paṟṟiya mēṟkaṇṭa varalāṟṟiliruntu avarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi pōriṭṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum, avarkaḷ kuṭikaḷ pōrkaḷiṉ naṭuvē, pōr muḻakkaṅkaḷiṭiyē tāṉ vāḻntuvantaṉar eṉṟum kāṇalām. ittakiya niliyil amiti kālak kalikaḷ eppaṭit taḻikka muṭiyum eṉṟu kēṭkavum tōṉṟum. itaṟku viṭi eḷitu. paṭittuṟip payiṟcipeṟṟa vīrarmaṭṭumē pōrilīṭupaṭṭaṉar. maṟṟa makkaḷ matilnakarkaḷil vāḻntu. tolliyiṉṟit tattam toḻilkaḷi naṭattiṉar.
talinakarkaḷiliruntu tolivilirunta ciṟṟūrkaḷil vāḻnta ēḻimakkaḷtām aṭikkaṭi ayalaracar tākkutalukku ilakkākik kaṭum tuṉpaṅkaḷukku āḷāki vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaṉṟukālikaḷ koṇṭēkappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷ eri yūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cila camayam avarkaḷ tam ūrkaḷiliruntu veḷiyē turattappaṭṭō, mikac ciṟitaḷavu etirppuk kāṭṭiṉāl kūṭak kollappaṭṭō avatiyuṟṟaṉar. āṉāl maturi, karūr, kāvirippaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa periya nakarkaḷiluḷḷa peruntokiyāṉa makkaḷ pōriṉ aṭṭūḻiyaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭṭat taṭṭa muḻu viṭutali peṟṟē vāḻntaṉar.
valimiyāṉa araṇkāval vāynta mukkiya nakaraṅkaḷil palavaṟṟiṉ makkaḷum itupōlavē etiripaṭikaḷatu vallāṭciyiṉ tīya viḷivukaḷiliruntu peritum pātukāp paṭintiruntaṉar. kalittoḻilāḷarkaḷukkum potu makkaḷukkum nalamtarum vāṇikamum kittoḻilkaḷum peritum ittakiya araṇ kāvalvāynta nakarkaḷilēyē naṭipeṟṟaṉa.
vāṇikariṉ cāttukkaḷ nakarukku nakar paṭivīrariṉ pātukāppuṭaṉ payaṇam ceytaṉa. uḷnāṭṭiluḷḷa mukkiya vāṇikakkaḷaṅkaḷ maṉṉaṉ paṭivīrarkaḷāl kāval ceyyap paṭṭu vantaṉa. aracapātikaḷil cuṅkavarikaḷ pirikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āṭcimuṟi vaṉ muṭiyāṭciyāy illi. pēraḷavu kuṭiyāṭcippaṇpu amintiruntatu. ituvum potu nalattukkup peritum ukantatāyiruntatu.
araciyaliṉ talivaṉ marapurimiyuṭiya ōr aracaṉē. āṉāl avaṉ āṟṟal aintu kuḻukkaḷiṉ āṟṟalāl variyaṟuk kappaṭṭiruntatu. iviyē aimperuṅ kuḻāṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.1 akkuḻukkaḷ muṟiyē kuṭikaḷ, kurumār, maruttuvar, kaṇikaḷ, allatu kuṟikārar, amiccar ākiyōriṉ pērāṭkaḷ aṭaṅki yaviyāyiruntaṉa. ippērāṭkaḷ maṉṟam kuṭimakkaḷiṉ urimi kaḷiyum kāppurimikaḷiyum pātukāttu vantaṉa.
kurumār ellā viṉimuṟikaḷiyum iyakkiṉar. maruttuvar maṉṉaṉ uṭalniliyiyum makkaḷ uṭalnalaṅ kaḷiyum pātikkum ellāc ceytikaḷiyum kavaṉittuvantaṉar. kaṇikaḷ potuviṉi muṟikaḷukkuriya kālaṅkaḷi variyaṟi ceytatuṭaṉ, mukkiya ceytikaḷi muṉṉaṟivittaṉar. amiccarkaḷ varivaruvāyp piriviyum celaviyum aṟamuṟiyiyum kaṇkāṇittaṉar.2
ik kuḻāṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟukkum talinakaril taṉittaṉi iṭaṅkaḷ variyaṟukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa3. mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷiṉ pōtum, aracaṉ aracirukkiyil amarntu kolumaṉṟam kūṭum camayaṅkaḷilum, maṉṉurimi ūrvalaṅkaḷilum avarkaḷ kalantu koṇṭaṉar.
inta aimperum kuḻāṅkaḷippaṟṟi iṉṉum vivaramāṉa ceytikaḷ aṟiya nām virumpuvatu iyalpu. āṉāl namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa paṇṭi ēṭukaḷil ivaṟṟiṉ mikutiyāṉa takavalkaḷ akappaṭavilli.
araciyal āṭci urimikaḷ yāvum aracaṉiṭamum aimperu’ kuḻāṅkaḷiṭamumē poṟuppāka viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarākiya mūṉṟu aracarkaḷum oruvari oruvar cārāta taṉiyaracarāṉālum, mūvaracukaḷilum itē muṟi tāṉ kiyāḷappaṭṭateṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakka ceyti ākum. ākavē mūvaracukaḷiyum niṟuviyavarkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil vantu kuṭiyēṟumuṉ vāḻnta mūlat tāyakamākiya makatappēraracilirunta muṟiyiyē iṅkum koṇṭuvantu piṉpaṟṟiṉar eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
aracaṉ tirumuṉpum araca cūḻalum perumatippukkum pukaṭṭāravārattukkum uriyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. peruntoki yāṉa paṇimakkaḷ avaṉukku ūḻiyam ceytaṉar. avarkaḷē aṭikkaṭi eṇpērāyaṅkaḷ eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ vivaram varumāṟu :-
maṇapporuḷāḷar, mālikkārar, aṭippakkārar, pākkuppaṇiyāḷar, kavacakkārar, āṭiyaṇi puṉivōr, pantamēntikaḷ, meykāvalar.
aracaṉ maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟup poṉṉālāṉa muṭi aṇintiruntāṉ. kikaḷil poṉ kāppukkaḷum valatu kālil taṅkakkaḻalum, kaḻuttil muttu allatu maṇimāliyum avaṉāl aṇiyappaṭṭa aṇimaṇikaḷ. muttukkōvikaḷil oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭa cīriya kuṭi oṉṟu, avaṉ araṇmaṉi yil tavacilamarntirukkum camayattilum araṇmaṉikku veḷiyē pōkumiṭaṅkaḷilum avaṉ talikku nērākap piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
potuvāka aracaṉ yāṉimītō, kutirimītō, nālu kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēriṉmītō ivarntu ceṉṟāṉ. kāliyilum māliyilum araṇmaṉi vāyilil periya muracaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkiṉa. nāḻiki vaṭṭilkaḷāl kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭa ovvoru nāḻiki muṭiviyum aṟivippataṟkeṉṟu araṇmaṉiyil nāḻiki vāṇar amarvu peṟṟiruntaṉar.4
aracikkum avaḷ paṇippeṇṭirukkumāka araṇmaṉi yil oru taṉippakuti otukkappaṭṭiruntatu. aracurimiyi avaḷ taṉ vaḻiyurimiyākak koṇṭiruntālaṉṟi avaḷ muṭi aṇivatilli. ellā maṉṉurimip potu amayaṅkaḷilum aracaṉuṭaṉ avaḷ vīṟṟiruntāḷ. āṉāl araṇmaṉiyil araciyiṉ urimiyi ṭaṅkaḷ āṭavar pukamuṭiyātaviyā yiruntaṉa.5 peruṅkuṭip peṇṭiraṉṟik kuṟaḷkaḷum, kūṉkaḷum alikaḷum, avaḷic cūḻntu paṇiceytaṉar.6
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ araci vēṇmāḷ nilaveṟitta ōr iravil araṇmaṉi nilāmāṭattil taṉ kaṇavaṉik kāṇacceṉṟa camayam avaḷic cūḻntuceṉṟa paṇippeṇṭir cilappati kārattil kīḻvarumāṟu varuṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar7:-
“muḻavum yāḻum iyakkikkoṇṭu cila peṇkaḷ vantaṉar. kūṉum kuṟaḷum nāṉam cantaṉam ākiyavi tāṅki vantaṉar. pēṭiyar peṇkaḷ uṭiyil naṟumaṇac cāntum poṭiyum koṇarntaṉar. cilar kiyil naṟumaṇa neykaḷum naṟumpu kiyum mālikaḷum mellaṇikaḷum ēntiṉar. cilar nilik kaṇṇāṭikaḷum āṭikaḷum aṇimaṇikaḷum cumantu vantaṉar. mēlum cilar viḷakki ēntiyavarāy, avviḷakkiṉ oḷiyil tāṅkaḷ aṇinta vaḷikaḷ miṉṉumpaṭi naṭantuvantu” nīṭu vāḻka araci eṉṟu vāḻtteṭuttaṉar.
araciyaliṉ mukkiya paṇiyāḷar talimi kuru, talimi kaṇi, amiccar, paṭittalivar ākiyōr8. muṟinaṭuvar, taṇṭalāḷar kaṭamikaḷic ceyyat taṇip paṇiyāḷar iruntaṉar. āṉāl ellā urimi vaḻakkukaḷilum kuṟṟavaḻakku kaḷilum aracaṉē kaṭici nilikkuriya ucca uyarnaṭuvarā yiruntāṉ. naṭuvarāyamarntiruntavar muṟimaṉṟattiluḷḷa maṟṟa ellāp paṇiyāḷariṭamiruntum taṉittu vēṟu pirittaṟiyum vakiyil taṉippaṭṭa taliyaṇi aṇintiruntār.9
vaḻakkāṭikaḷukku ilavacamākavē muṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. āyiṉum taṇṭaṉikaḷ mikak kaṭumiyāka iruntaṉa. kuṟṟamum itaṉāl arunikaḻvākavē iruntatu. kiyil toṇṭiyuṭaṉ piṭippaṭṭa tiruṭaṉ kolit taṇṭaṉi tarappaṭṭāṉ10. takāp puṇarccic ceyalil piṭipaṭṭavaṉ koli ceyyappaṭṭāṉ11. avveṇṇattuṭaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉ vīṭṭil nuḻintavaṉ kālkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa12. kuruṭṭu nampikkikaḷ cārnta accamē cila camayam nītiyiṉ peyarāl aracaṉic ceyalkaḷ ceyvittaṉa. makkaḷ vāḻvu kuṟitta aracaṉ āṇikaḷ yāṉi mītēṟic ceṉṟa paṇi mutalvarkaḷāl talinakarameṅkum muracaṟintu terivikkappaṭṭatu.13
tīrvi, cuṅkam, nilavari ākiyaviyē vari varumāṉattil mukkiya pakutikaḷ, tīrvikaḷ ellāk kaṭal tuṟimukaṅkaḷilum pirikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭa carakkukaḷ yāvum araca ciṉṉam tāṅkiya muttiri poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. tīrvi celuttap paṭṭapiṉpē carakkukaḷ vaṇikariṉ paṇṭa cālikaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa14. cuṅkaṅkaḷ vaṇikac cāttukkaḷ celvataṟkariya araca pātikaḷilum ovvoru araciṉ ellikaḷilum teṇṭappaṭṭaṉa15. nilavari paṇamākavō carakkākavō koṭuppavaṉatu viruppappaṭi koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
kuṭimaṉṉarum vēḷirum koṭukkum tiṟi, ellip puṟap pōrnaṭavaṭikkiyil peṟṟa koḷḷi varuvāy, muttup paṇṇikaḷ, kāṭṭiyāṉi, kāṭṭuvaḷam ākiya aracar taṉik kaḷaṅkaḷil varum ātāyaṅkaḷ ākiyavi aracaṉ varuvāyil perumpakutiyāyamintaṉa. nilavariyil āṟil orupaṅku nērmi vāynta aracaṉ paṅkāyiruntatu. araciyalārāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa taṇṇirukkīṭākat taṉippaṭa uḻavarkaḷiṭamiruntu taṇṇīrvari pirikkappaṭṭatu.
aracaṉ araciyalukku maṭṭumaṉṟic camutāyattukkum talivaṉāyiruntāṉ. vaḻakkamāṉa avaṉ meykāvalar mutaliya paṇiyāḷarkaḷ cūḻavē ceṉṟālum, anniliyilēyē makkaḷuṭaṉ taṅkutaṭiyiṉṟip paḻakiṉāṉ. talinakariṉ viḻākkaḷil avaṉ talimi vakittāṉ. pañcam, perunōy kālaṅkaḷil nōṉpukaḷ, vēḷvikaḷāṟṟuvatilum avaṉ mutaliṭam tāṅkiṉāṉ. makkaḷ iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷi avaṉ pakirntāṉ kuṟinta aḷavil aracavi marapukaḷ aṅṅaṉam pakirumpaṭi avaṉi valintu kaṭṭāyap paṭuttiṉa.
cila aracariṭam makkaḷ vaḻakkattukku mikutiyāṉa paṟṟuk kāṭṭiṉar. paṭiyeṭuppāḷar taṅkaḷ ūrkaḷik kippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷil, ūr makkaḷ muḻuvatumē taṅkaḷ vīṭu kuṭikaḷi viṭviṭṭut tam aracaṉ irunta pakutiyilēyē ceṉṟu kuḻumiya ceytikaḷ teriyavarukiṉṟaṉa.16 viḻākkālaṅkaḷil, potu ampalaṅkaḷilum cari, taṉi illaṅkaḷilum cari, eṅkum makkaḷāl maṉṉaṉ veṟṟiyum nīṇṭa vāḻvum kuṟittu vāḻttu vaḻipāṭu ceyyappaṭṭatu.
kōyililiruntu mīṇṭa orunaṅki taṉ tōḻiyiṭam pēcumpōtu vaḻakkamāṉa ivvāḻttik kēṭkiṟōm. “(em aracaṉ) ātaṉ vāḻka, (em aracaṉ) aviṉi vāḻka eṉa eṉ tāy vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. attuṭaṉ nāṉum ōcipaṭāmal uḷḷattuk kuḷḷāka (eṉ kātalaṉ) ceḻuṅkaḻaṉiyi uṭiyāṉ virintu ūr tirumpuka eṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭēṉ” eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟukiṟāḷ.17
āriyar piṉpaṟṟiya pirama, kṣattiriya, viciya, cūttirar eṉṟa nāl varuṇap pākupāṭu tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyil iruntatilli. tirunūl aṇiyappeṟṟavarkaḷukkellām āriyarkaḷāl irupiṟap pāḷar eṉṟa ciṟappuppeyar vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṇṭit tamiḻilak kiyattil atu pārppaṉari maṭṭumē kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. itiliruntu tirunūlaṇinta kṣattiriyarum, viciyarum, tamiḻakattil irukka villi eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
tūya taṉittamiḻiṉattavariṭiyē mikavum uyar matippuk kuriyavarkaḷ aṟivar allatu cittarkaḷē. avarkaḷ kālattiṉ mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷiyum atāvatu iṟantakālam, nikaḻkālam, varuṅ kālam mūṉṟiyum aṟintavarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ otuṅkiya camaya vāḻvu vāḻntatāl, perunakaraṅkaḷukku veḷiyilēyē taṅkiyiruntaṉar. pārppaṉar āṭal naṅkiyaruṭaṉum kūttiyaru ṭaṉum ūṭāṭat tayaṅkavilli.18 kātalariṭiyē tūtuvarāyc cellavum tayaṅkavilli. āṉāl aṟivarkaḷ ivaṟṟiliruntu muṟṟilum vilakiyiruntaṉar.19
aṟivarukkaṭuttapaṭi matippuṭiyavar uḻavar allatu vēḷāḷar. aṟivar tuṟavikaḷē. ākavē camutāyam paṟṟiya maṭṭil, iv vēḷāḷarē ucca uyarvakappiṉarāvar. avarkaḷē nāṭṭiṉ uyarkuṭip perumakkaḷ allatu nilakkiḻār marapiṉarāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ veḷḷāḷar atāvatu veḷḷatti āṇṭavarkaḷ eṉṟum, kārāḷar allatu mukili āṇṭavar eṉṟum aḻikkap paṭṭaṉar. ip paṭṭaṅkaḷ peruveḷḷaṅkaḷi aṇiyiṭṭut taṭuttu uḻavut toḻilukkāka atit tēkkivippatil avar kaḷukkirunta tiṟattik kāṭṭiṉa.
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷum tamiḻaka vēḷiril perumpālāṉavarkaḷum inta vēḷāḷar marapic cērnta varkaḷē. ciṟitaḷavē nilamuṭiya ēḻi vēḷāḷar kuṭiyiṉar potuvāka ‘vīḻkuṭi uḻavar’ allatu vīḻcciyaṭinta vēḷāḷar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. maṟṟa vēḷāḷarkaḷ celvak kuṭiyiṉar eṉpatiyē itu kuṟittirukkakkūṭum.20
perumpukaḻk karikālaṉ aruvāḷari veṉṟu avarkaḷ nāṭṭit taṉ aracuṭaṉ cērttukkoṇṭapōtu, avaṉ veṉṟa  nāṭukaḷit taṉ vēḷāḷar talivarkaḷukkup pakirntu koṭuttāṉ21. it talivarkaḷiṉ vaḻivantavar iṉṟum taṅkaḷ nilaṅkaḷuṭaṉ araciyalāriṉkīḻ22 nēraṭiyāṉa urimipeṟṟa varāy ciṟunilak kiḻārāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar23. iṉṟum avarkaḷ mutalikaḷ allatu mutal vakuppiṉar eṉṟē aḻikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar.
vaṭukam allatu taṟkālat teluṅku nāṭṭi veṉṟa vēḷāḷa kuṭiyiṉar vēḷamar eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. aṅkuḷḷa perunilakkiḻār iṉṟum iṉṉum ivvēḷamar vakuppiṉarē.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil ivvēḷāḷar peḷḷāḷamarapi niṟuvip pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka nāṭāṇṭaṉar.
vēḷāḷarkaḷukkuk kaṅkakulam allatu kaṅkavamcam eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. ēṉeṉil avarkaḷ piḷiṉi, ṭālami ākiyavar kaḷāl kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaṅkit tīrattiluḷḷa vallamivāynta kaṅkariṭē eṉṟa māperuṅkuṭi marapiliruntu taṅkaḷ kulamarapi varaṉmuṟiyākakkoṇṭaṉar.
vēḷāḷarkaḷē perumpālum niṟinta micūriṉ oru pakuti ki. pi. 10,11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil kaṅkavāṭi eṉṟaḻikkap paṭṭatu.
immarapic cērnta maṟṟoru kuṭivaḻi kaṅka vamcam eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ 11,12-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil oricāvi āṇṭatu.
āyar allatu iṭiyarum, vēṭṭuvar allatu vēṭarum uḻavarukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ.24 poṟkollar, karumāṉ, taccar, kuyavar mutaliya kalittoḻilāḷar āyarkaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ. avarkaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ paṭiyāṭciyar allatu paṭikkalam mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷ. valiyar allatu mīṉ toḻilāḷarum puliyar allatu tōṭṭikaḷum kaṭicip paṭiyiliruntārkaḷ.
uyarvakuppiṉar teruvil ceṉṟapōtu, tāḻ vakuppiṉar avarkaḷukku vaḻiviṭṭaṉar25. perumakaṉik kaṇṭa puliyaṉ allatu tōṭṭi vaḻipaṭupavaṉpōla iru kikaḷiyum kūppit talivaṇaṅkiṉāṉ.26
aṭimittaṉam tamiḻariṭi iruntatilli. appaḻaṅ kālattil avarkaḷ aṭintirunta uyar nākarikappaṭiyi itu kuṟittuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyayēyuḷḷa mēṟkāṭṭiya vakuppumuṟi mekāsteṉis kaṇṭu tīṭṭiya paṇṭi makatappēraraciluḷḷa makkaḷ niliyip peritum ottuḷḷaṉa.
avar kuṟittuḷḷapaṭi makkaḷtokuti ēḻu vakuppukkaḷākap pirivupaṭṭiruntatu. mutal vakuppu aṟivar vakuppē. āṉāl tokiyil ivarkaḷ mikamikak kuṟivāṉavarkaḷē. taṉippaṭṭa maṉitarkaḷāl avarkaḷ vēḷvikaḷilum maṟṟat toṭakkattil aracaṉ avarkaḷit taṉ pēravikku āṇṭiṉ pañcapparuvaṅkaḷ, maḻi, araciyalkōḷāṟukaḷ ākiyavipaṟṟiya muṉṉaṟivuri nalkiṉar.
inta aṟivarkaḷ āṭiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruntārkaḷ. kuḷirkālattil avarkaḷ veyilkāyumpaṭi veṭṭaveḷiyi lēyē taṅkiṉārkaḷ. vēṉiṟkālattil avarkaḷ pulveḷi kaḷilum tāḻnilaṅkaḷilum maraṅkaḷiṉaṭiyil taṅkiṉārkaḷ.
iraṇṭāvatu vakuppu uḻavar vakuppu. atu taṉ nēra muḻuvatiyum nilam paṇpaṭuttuvatilēyē celaviṭṭatu. avarkaḷ tam uḻippiṉ nāṉkilorupaṅki aracaṉukku iṟiyākat tantaṉar.
āyarkaḷum vēṭarkaḷum mūṉṟām vakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kālnaṭikaḷip pēṇiyum kūṭāraṅkaḷil taṅkiyum nāṭōṭi vāḻvu vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ kālnaṭikaḷiyē variyākat tantaṉar. vayalkaḷil vititta payirkaḷi aḻikkum paṟavikaḷiyum vilaṅkukaḷiyum akaṟṟi nilattip pātukāp pataṟkāka aracaṉiṭamiruntu avarkaḷ paṭi nel peṟṟaṉar.
nāṉkām vakuppu toḻil vakuppu. poruḷkaḷ vāṅki viṟṟal, kūlivēli ceytal avarkaḷ vēli. paṭikkalat toḻilāḷarum kavacat toḻilāḷarum, ellāvakippaṭṭa kalittoḻilāḷarkaḷum ippakuppiṉar.
pōr vīrar aintām vakuppāyiṉar. avarkaḷ aracaṉ celavil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷukkup paṭittuṟi vēli tavira vēli illi. amitikkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nērattik kuṭittuc cōmpittirivatilēyē kaḻittaṉar.
āṟām vakuppu mēṟpārvi ceytatu, nāṭṭilum nakarilum naṭappati oṟṟaṟintu aracaṉukkō taṇṭalāḷarukkō aṟivappatu avarkaḷ kaṭami.
ēḻāvatu vakuppu taṉṉāṭciyuṭiya nakarkaḷil potu āṭcik kāriyaṅkaḷil maṉṉaṉ allatu taṇṭalāḷarkaḷukku aṟivuri kūṟiya maṉṟattār aṭaṅkiyatāyiruntatu.
nāṭṭiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkac caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷ vakuppukku vakuppu kalappumaṇattik kaṇṭittatu. evarum oru vakuppiliruntu maṟṟoru vakuppukku māṟavum icivaḷikkap paṭavilli. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, ōr uḻavaṉ iṭiyaṉāka māṟavō, allatu iṭiyar vakuppu allatu kalit toḻil vakuppiliruntu peṇkoḷḷavō muṭiyātu.
aṟivar vakappukkumaṭṭum iccaṭṭatiṭṭam muḻuvilak kaḷittatu. avar enta vakuppiṉarākavum irukkalām. ēṉeṉil avar vāḻvu eḷitāṉa oṉṟalla. attuṭaṉ avarkaḷil mikac cilarē muṉṉaṟiviṉtiṟam vāykkappeṟṟaṉar.27
paṇṭi makatampaṟṟiya mēṟkūṟiya vakuppup piriviṉi viḷakkattil oṟṟarkaḷiyum mēṟpārviyāḷarkaḷiyum aṟivuriyāḷarkaḷiyum taṉi vakuppukkaḷāka mekastaṉis karutiyatu tavaṟu eṉpatu teḷivu. maṟṟa vakuppukaḷellām tamiḻari ṭiyilum makatattilum muṟṟilum icikiṉṟaṉa.
itiliruntu iru iṉaṅkaḷumē mikattol paḻaṅkālat tilēyē nākarikamaṟṟa niliyiliruntu vilaki nīṇṭa kālamākak kuṭivāḻvum niliyāṉa āṭcimuṟiyum uṭiyavarāy iruntaṉar eṉṟum, pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap pōrum muraṭṭup pūcalum avarkaḷiṭiyē illātiruntaṉa eṉṟum teriya varukiṟatu. avarkaḷ nākarikam āriyar nākarikattiviṭa mikavum paḻami yāṉatu eṉpatil aiyamilli. ēṉeṉil āriyarkaḷiṭiyē pōrvīrarpaṭi kurumārpaṭikku aṭuttāyiruntatu. nērmāṟākat tamiḻariṭiyē vēḷāḷarē camayakurumāruk kaṭuttapaṭiyāyi runtaṉar. pōrvīrar vakuppu avarkaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟi, āyarka ḷukkum uḻavut toḻilāḷarukkam kūṭak kīḻppaṭṭiruntatu.
nām kuṟippiṭum kālattukkuk kuṟintatu aintu allatu āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntē pārppaṉar tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tamiḻarmītu taṅkaḷ cāti varuṇamuṟiyic cumanta arumpāṭupaṭṭaṉar.
namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa mikap paḻamiyāṉa tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tiṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyar eṉṟa oru pārppaṉar.28 avar ki. mu. 1-vatu allatu 2-vatu nūṟṟāṇṭukkuriyavar. avar aṭikkaṭi aṟivar allatu cittarkaḷippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟār.29 āṉāl camutāya vakuppukkaḷik kuṟikkum iyalil āciriyar aṟivarippaṟṟiya pēcciyē eṭukkavilli. tirunūlaṇinta pārppaṉariyē30 mutal vakuppiṉarākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār.31
mikunta tiṟami nayattuṭaṉ  pōrvīrari vilakki aracari maṭṭum avar teyvika vakuppil cērttukkoḷkiṟār. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarākiya mūvaracarmaṭṭum oru vakuppāka amiyap pōtiyavareṉṟu avar karutiṉār pōlum! vāṇikattāl vāḻpavarē mūṉṟām vakuppu eṉa avar kuṟittār. āyiṉum aracarō vaṇikarō tirunūl aṇintatāka avar kuṟikkavilli!
itaṉpiṉ avar vēḷāḷarit taṉiyākap pirittu avar kaḷukku uḻavallatu toḻililli eṉṟu vakukkiṟār. iṅkum avar vēḷāḷar cūttirar eṉṟu kuṟikkavilli. āyiṉum potunili vēḷāḷar cūttirarākak kaṇikkappaṭa vēṇṭiya varkaḷ eṉṟa toṉipaṭak kuṟikkiṟār. atēcamayam aracarāka amarnta vēḷāḷar kṣattiriyarākak karutappaṭavēṇṭu meṉṟum pirikkiṟār.
tamiḻarit taṅkaḷ cāti varuṇamuṟikkuk koṇṭuvarap pārppaṉar ceyta mutal muyaṟci ituvē. āṉāl, tamiḻakattil kṣattiriyar, viciyar, cūttirar ākiya pirivukaḷ illāta niliyil avarkaḷ veṟṟiyaṭiya muṭiyavilli. ēṉeṉil iṉṟaḷavum taṉṉi kṣattiriyaṉ eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum oru paṭiyāṭciyiṉ vīṭṭil allatu taṉṉi viciyaṉ eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum oru vaṇikaṉ vīṭṭil veḷḷāḷaṉ uṇavukoḷḷavō kuṭikka nīrpeṟavō ceyvatilli.
tolkāppiyar tam ilakkaṇattil āyar allatu iṭiyarippaṟṟiyum, vēṭuvar allatu vēṭarippaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. āṉāl vakuppup pirivupaṟṟiya iyalil avar maṟavar, valiyar, puliyar mutaliya vakuppukkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟavilli.32 ēṉeṉil pārppaṉīya cātimuṟiyuṭaṉ muraṇ paṭātapaṭi avarkaḷippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭa avarāl muṭiyavilli.33
tamiḻmakkaḷ aṇinta āṭi camutāyattil avarkaḷ paṭimuṟiyiyum avarkaḷ marapiṉaṅkaḷiyum poṟuttup palvaki vēṟupāṭuṭiyatu. tūya taṉittamiḻariṭiyē naṭuttara vakuppiṉar potuvāka ariyic cuṟṟi muḻantāḷvari paṭiyum paṭi oṉṟum taliyic cuṟṟit taḷarvākak kaṭṭiya oṉṟumāka iraṇṭu āṭikaḷē aṇintārkaḷ.34 avarkaḷ, talimayiri veṭṭuva tilli. ati iyaṟki vaḷarcciyaḷavukkē vaḷaraviṭṭaṉar allatu orupuṟamāka atil oru periya muṭiyiṭṭuk kaṭṭiṉar. intat taliyiṉ muṭiyikkaṭṭa uyar vakuppiṉar paḷapaḷappāṉa nīlamaṇikaḷ kōtta niṟamārnta iḻikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iḻikaḷ kuñcampōl toṅkaviṭappaṭṭaṉa.35
nākamarapucārnta maṟavar talivaṉ ariyāṭiyi nīlaniṟa vārāl iṟakkik kaṭṭiṉāṉ avaṉ taliyil mayiliṟakukaḷ cerukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.36
pārppaṉar tam talimayirik kuṟukattaṟittaṉar. āṉāl taliyucciyil oru kuṭumiyi viṭṭuvittaṉar. oru tamiḻppulavar kutiriyiṉ taliyil uḷḷa kuṟumayirukkup pārppaṉa iḷiñar talimayiri uvami kūṟiyuḷḷār37. potu vītikaḷik kāvalkātta vīrarum aracaṉ araṇmaṉi yiluḷḷa paṇiyāṭkaḷum caṭṭikaḷ aṇintārkaḷ.38
ip paṇṭikkālattil muḻumēṉi uṭi oru talivaṉ ciṉṉamāyamiyavilli, oru vēlikkāraṉ ciṉṉamākavē karutappaṭṭatu. perumakkaḷ veppamāṉa vaḷiniliyil mikunta vāyppukkēṭiṉṟi enta aḷavu uṭi aṇiyamuṭiyumō anta aḷavu kuṟinta uṭiyē aṇintaṉar.
tamiḻppeṇṭiriṉ potunili āṭiyil tōḷum kiyum iṭuppuvariyuḷḷa uṭalum muṟṟilum veṟumiyākavē iruntaṉa. āṭi eṉpatellām muḻuvatum ariyiliruntu toṭaṅkik kaṇikkālvariyilumē iruntaṉa.39 tiṟantirunta uṭaṟpakuti potuvākac cantaṇa mutaliya naṟumaṇac cāntukaḷāl oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu40. nākappeṇkaḷō amarāvatic cetukkuk kaliyil kāṇappaṭuvatupōlak kiṭṭattaṭṭa āṭiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruntatākat terikiṟatu.
āṭal naṅkiyar aṇinta melliya malmal āṭi ariyi liruntu tuṭi naṭuvarikkumē mūṭiṉa. atuvum mikanēriya tāyiruntatāl atu mēṉiyi oru ciṟitum maṟikkavilli41. malaṅkuṭi maṅkiyar taṅkaḷ ariyic cuṟṟik kaṭṭik kayiṟṟil māṭṭappaṭṭa taḻikkottukkaḷiyē aṇintaṉar42. pāṇarkaḷiṉ ūr cūḻvaraliṉpōtu avarkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟa avarkaḷ maṉiviyar muṟṟilum āṭiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar43. uṇmiyil paṇṭiya intiyāvil ammaṇattaṉmi ōr iḻipaṇpākak karutappaṭṭatākat teriyavilli.
tamiḻppeṇṭir taṅkaḷ kūntalip putumivāynta muṟikaḷil puṉintaṉar. ati avarkaḷ aintu pakutikaḷākap pirittu, ovvoṉṟiyum taṉittaṉiyākac cuṟṟi allatu piṉṉi aintu taṉittaṉi muṭikaḷuṭaṉ vaṉappu nayamuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu karutap paṭṭavakikaḷilē muṭippukkaṭanta pakutiyi taliyiṉpiṉ toṅkaviṭṭaṉar. kūntalip pēṇuvatilum avarkaḷ mikunta kavaṉam celuttiyatākat terikiṟatu. ēṉeṉil iḷam peṇkaḷ taliyōṭṭiṉ meṉṟōl teriyumaḷavu taṅkaḷ tali mayirik  kattirikaḷāl oṭṭak kattirittu, aintu muṭikaḷ maṭṭum oṉṟiliruntu oṉṟu akalvāka viṭuvatum, peṇkaḷ vaḷarcci yuṭaṉ vaḷarcciyāka im muṭippakuti paṭippaṭiyāka virivupaṭuttap paṭṭu iṟutiyil talimuḻuvatum uṭkoḷḷuvatum vaḻakkameṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.44
kūntali ivvāṟu muṭikkumuṉ aimpālākap pakukkum vaḻakkam iṉṉum parmiyariṭiyē irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kēḷvip paṭukiṟōm. itu eṉṟum āriyappeṇṭirāl mēṟkoḷḷap paṭavilli. taṟpōtu itu tamiḻariṭamiruntum muṟṟilum maṟintuviṭṭatu.
āṭavarum, peṇṭirum, ellā vakuppiṉarum, tam talikku eṇṇeyiṭṭaṉar. peṇṭir talikōti muṭikkum pōtu aṭikkaṭi maṇap poruḷkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyatuṭaṉ palvaki malarkaḷālum aṇimaṇikaḷālum ati oppaṉi ceytaṉar.
irupālārum palavaki naṟuneykaḷāl mēṉikku maṇa mūṭṭiṉar, cenniṟam, poṉṉiṟam koṇṭa palvakip poṭi kaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiṉar45. mātar kaṇṇimikaḷukkuk karuniṟa mi tīṭṭiṉar46. uyar vakuppiṉar illaṅkaḷil cāmpirāṇi mutaliya maṇappukip poruḷkaḷ pukippaṭṭaṉa.47
āṭi aḷavu kuṟivāyiruntālum tamiḻ makkaḷ aṇi maṇikaḷi ērāḷamākap puṉintārkaḷ. kaḻuttu, ki, iṭuppu ākiyavaṟṟil aṇinta palvaki aṇimaṇikaḷē avarkaḷ mēṉi oppaṉiyil mikac ciṟappuvāynta pakutiyāyiruntatu.
vēḷirum irunilakkiḻavarum viliyuyarnta maṇimāli allatu muttumāli aṇintaṉar. kikaḷil paḷuvēṟiya keṭṭik kāppup pūṇṭaṉar. araca kuṭiyiṉarum ciṟukuṭi maṉṉarum itaṉuṭaṉ taṉicciṟappuppaṭak kālil kaḻal pūṭṭiṉar.
vēḷir kuṭimakaṉ oruvaṉ aṇimaṇi kīḻvarumāṟu varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu :-
“avaṉ kālkaḷil iraṭṭip poṉṉiḻip piṉṉaluṭaṉ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa ciṟu maṇikaḷiṉ varici vāynta kaḻal kiṭantatu. avaṉ ariyic cuṟṟip poṉ kācukaḷ niri niriyākat tūkkiya pavaḷakkoṭi ariñāṇ toṅkiṟṟu. ataṉmītu avaṉ iṭuppil mika nēriya oru malmal āṭi uṭuttiyiruntāṉ. naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷpōla aḻakuṟap puṉiyap paṭṭa piṉṉal vēlip pāṭuṭiya aḻakiya tōḷvaḷikaḷ tōḷi aṇi ceytaṉa. kaḻuttil toṅkiya iraṭṭip paṭṭip poṟ caṅkiliyiliruntu maḻu vāḷ paṭikaḷiṉ ciṟṟurukkaḷum atiṟkōtta erutiṉ uruvāynta oru patakkamum toṅkiṉa. avaṉ talimītu mup purikaḷāka muṟukkiya muttup pavaḷaṅkaḷālāṉa talimāliyum ataṉmēl oru pūmāliyum oḷimikka nīlamaṇikaḷkōtta iḻiyāl oruṅku piṇikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.”48
“tāḻnili vakuppiṉariṭiyē mātar caṅku vaḷikaḷ, veṇmaṇi, nīlamaṇikaḷālāṉa kaḻuttumāli, mutaliya malivāṉa nakikaḷ aṇintaṉar. naṭuttara vakuppiṉar perumpālum taṅka nakikaḷē aṇintārkaḷ. ēṉeṉil intak kālattil intiyāvil veḷḷi mikavum aritāṉa oru poruḷ. celvariṉ nakikaḷ mika viliyēṟiyatāka iruntaṉa. potu āṭalmakaḷir pūṇkaḷē mikap pakaṭṭāravāramāṉaviyāy iruntaṉa.”
“āṭalnaṅki yaṇinta pūṇkaḷippaṟṟic cilappati kārattil tarappaṭum kīḻvarum varuṇaṉi nakiyaṇi maṇivakiyil irunta āravāra iṉpa vāḻvik kāṭṭavallatu.”
“avaṉ kūntali maṇaneyyil tōyttāḷ. anta ney muppattiraṇṭu vakic ceṭikaḷiliruntum aivaki maṇap poruḷ kaḷiliruntum pattuvakit tuvarpporuḷkaḷiṉālum puṉinti yaṟṟappaṭṭatu. akiṟpukiyil kūntali ulartti piṉ avaḷ ataṉi aimpālākap pakuttu kattūri kalanta naṟuney pūciṉāḷ. kālaṭikaḷi avaḷ cempañcikkuḻampāl cevvaṇṇam tōyat tīṭṭiṉāḷ. kāliṉ ciṟu viralkaḷukkup pīlikaḷ māṭṭiṉāḷ.”
“avaḷ kālaṭikaḷil kaṇikkāliliruntu peruviral vari oṉṟaṭuttoṉṟu itaḻ virittāṟpōṉṟu paṭarum kālaṇi vakiyaṇintāḷ. kaṇikkālil maṇic cilampu, cataṅki mutaliya nālvakik kaḻalkaḷaṇintāḷ. tuṭikaḷil tuṭi māṭṭikaḷ ēṟiṉa. vaṉappamiya maṭitta mika nērttiyāṉa nīlaniṟa malmal āṭiyi avaḷ iṭuppil cērttu ataṉmītu muppattiraṇṭu maṇikaḷuḷḷa mēkali aṇintāḷ.”
“muḻaṅkikkumēlāka mika uyarnta muttumaṇikaḷālāṉa poṉpotinta tōḷvaḷikaḷum, muṉkikaḷil paḷapaḷappāṉa valampuri, pavaḷa vaḷikaḷuṭaṉum, cila poṉ vaḷikaḷum cila ariya vēlippāṭaminta vayira marakatamaṇikaḷ patitta vaḷikaḷum pūṭṭiṉāḷ. kiviralkaḷukkup paṟpala vakippaṭṭa mōtiraṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. oṉṟu makaravāy pōṉṟiruntatu. oṉṟu naṭuvē marakatamum cūḻa vayiraṅkaḷum uṭiyatāyiruntatu.”
“kaḻuttil (vayiṟṟu naṭuvarivantu maṟikkumpaṭi)” palavaki poṉmālikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. oṉṟu taṅkac caṅkiliyākavum oṉṟu muṟukkiya poṉṉiḻi vaṭivākavum maṟṟoṉṟu poṉtaḻi māliyākavum iruntatu. kaḻuttiṉ piṉṉē mālikaḷ aṉit tiyum cērttu oru nilippaṭuttip piṭikkumpaṭi mikap pakaṭṭāṉa tōṟṟamuṭiya orucērt tiṟukki aṇiyappeṟṟatu.
“tōṭukaḷil māṟimāṟi vayiram nīlamum iṭam peṟṟaṉa.”
“taliyil avaḷ cūṭṭiya aṇimaṇip piṉṉalkaḷ maṟṟa ellā aṇimaṇikaḷiyum orucēra vellat takkaviyāy iruntaṉa.”49
peṇṭir vīṇpakaṭṭillāmalē vāḻkkit toḻil kaḷaṅkaḷilum iṉpap poḻutu pōkkukaḷilum piṟaruṭaṉ tārāḷamākap paḻakiṉar. nakaraṅkaḷilum paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷilum ēḻi vakuppup peṇṭir teruviṟpaṉiyāḷarkaḷākavum, ciṟu vaṇika rākavum, kaṭikkārarākavum, celvar vīṭukaḷil paṇiyāṭ kaḷākavum toḻilīṭupāṭu, koṇṭiruntaṉar. ciṟṟūrkaḷil avarkaḷ vayal kaḷilum tōṭṭaṅkaḷilum āṭavaruṭaṉ ottu vēliceytu avarkaḷ kaṭṭanaṭṭaṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷ pakirntukoṇṭaṉar.
uyarvakuppu mātar peritum vīṭṭilēyē vāḻntaṉar āṉāl avarkaḷum camūkattiliruntu muṟṟilum tuṇṭupaṭṭu viṭavilli. aracimutal ellā uyarkuṭimātarumē kōyil kaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. māli nēraṅkaḷil avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṭṭumāṭi muṟṟaṅkaḷil vantiruntu teruvil kāṭcikaḷip pārttiruntārkaḷ. viḻakkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ ūrvalaṅkaḷil kalantukoṇṭārkaḷ. naṇparkaḷ, uṟaviṉarkaḷukku aḻippuk kūṟavum avarkaḷ ceṉṟārkaḷ.50
peṇṭirukkirunta taṅkutaṭiyaṟṟa viṭutaliyurimi kāraṇamāka, iḷamparuvattiṉar tirumaṇattiṟku muṉpē kātalilīṭu paṭuvataṟku vāyppiruntatu. ōr iḷamaṅki kātalaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉ pōkkup peṟuvatukūṭa, piṉṉāl avarkaḷ uṟaviṉariṭam mīṇṭuvantu maṇavāḻvil īṭupaṭuvatāṉāl, takātateṉṟu karutappaṭavilli.
aracavi, paṭivīṭu, cōli ākiya ellā iṭaṅkaḷilumē annāḷ poṉṉiṉ pēravā āḷavilli. aṉpē āṇṭatu. irupālārum oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar parimāṟikkoḷḷum muṟi iṉṟiruppativiṭa iṇakka nayamum paṇpunayamum uṭiyatā yiruntatu. inniliyil annāḷiya kavitiyil perumpakuti kātalpaṟṟiyatāyiruppatilum, kātal pākkaḷ eḻutuvatupaṟṟiya ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ amintirunta tilum viyappilli.
iḷamparuvattiṉar maṇavāḻvukku muṉ kātal kūrtal tamiḻariṭiyē aṉṟē mikap paḻamivāynta nilipeṟṟuviṭṭa oru vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. eṉavētāṉ annāḷil tiruvaḷḷuvar perumāṉ iyaṟṟiya pukaḻmikka aṟamuṟic camutāya oḻukka nūlil51 nūliṉ mūṉṟu pālkaḷil oru pāl kātalpaṟṟiya tāyuḷḷatu. ivvaḻakkam iṉṉum tamiḻakattil muṟṟilum aḻintupaṭṭu viṭṭateṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. ēṉeṉil atu iṉṉum maliyāḷakkariyil nīṭikkiṟatu. irupālāruk kiṭiyilum irunta toṭarpip paṇṭik kaviñar coṟkaḷālēyē kūṟuvatu nalam.
kātalpaṟṟiya cila pāṭalkaḷiliruntu kīḻvarum karuttuk kaḷit tarukiṟōm :- 
oru peṇ naṭantukoṇṭa vakipaṟṟi avaḷ tōḻi peṇṇiṉ kātalaṉiṭam kīḻvarumāṟu pēcukiṟāḷ :- 
“eṉṉik kaṉivuṭaṉ nōkki, eṉ aṇimaṉikaḷit tirutti, cantaṇakkuḻampāl eṉ tōḷkaḷil toyyil eḻutukiṟāṉē - antap peruntaṉmiyuṭiya iḷiñaṉukkē, eṉ ayalār kūṟukiṟapaṭi nāṉ eṉ neñcip paṟikoṭuttu viṭṭēṉā eṉṟu nī kēṭkiṟāyākkum? aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ nāṉ colvatik kēḷ.”
“nī kūṟukiṟapayē, kaṭaṟkariyilē nāṉ avaṉuṭaṉ nīṇṭa nēram tiṇṇiya kaṭal kōrikaḷip paṟṟi iḻuttup paṟittuk koṇṭē ulavuvatuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam paṟippatāl kikaḷellām civantuviṭum. āṉāl itellām eṉakkāka avaṉ ceytutanta kōrip pommikkākattāṉ. ayalārkaḷ kaṭṭiviṭukiṉṟa katikaḷiyellām itaṟkāka nī nampiviṭuvatu viyappukkuriya tākavē yirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉil inta ayalārkaḷukku ūrvampu pēcātapōtu maṉa amitiyē yiruppatilli pōlirukkiṟatu.”
“viyappārvattuṭaṉ nōkkum uṉ kaṇ pārviyil paṭuvataṟku añci nāṉ eṉ vīṭṭiliruntu tirumpi naṭantēṉ. avaṉ nāṉ tirumpi varuvatukaṇṭu, catuppu nilattil vaḷarntu kiṭanta cila malarkaḷip paṟittu avaṟṟi oru māliyākak kaṭṭi ati eṉakkut tantāṉ. avaṉ eṉṉiṭam kāṭṭiya intac ciṟitaḷavu naṭpurimikkākavā, ittakiya pōlikkatikaḷuṭaṉ uṉṉi eṉṉiṭam aṉuppiyavarkaḷik kaṇṭittuk kēṭkāmal, eṉṉiṭam kēḷvikēṭka vantirukkiṟāy?”*
“eṉ tōḷkaḷil avaṉ cantaṇakkuḻampāl karumpiṉ taṇṭiṉi aḻakākattāṉ eḻutiṉāṉ. eṉakku appaṭi eḻuta varāteṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē eḻutiṉāṉ. intac ciṟiya aṉpā taravukkākavā eṉ viḷiyāṭṭut tōḻiyar kūṟiya cōmpēṟi vampurikaḷukkuc cevikoṭuttuk kēṭṭuk koṇṭu eṉṉit tontaravu paṭuttukiṟāy.”52
kuṟumpaṉāṉa kaṭṭaṟṟa iḷiñaṉoruvaṉ kaṭikātalaṉāka māṟuvatiyum avaṉippaṟṟip peṇ taṉ tōḻiyiṭam pēcuvatiyum kīḻē kāṇkiṟōm.
“nāṉ kūṟuvatik kēḷ, tōḻi!”
“nam ciṟu vīṭukaḷi aḻittu, nam taliyiluḷḷa mālikaḷik kulittu, nām teruvilvittu viḷiyāṭum pantip paṟit tōṭi namakku elliyillāta tolli koṭukkum antak kuṟumpuc ciṟuvaṉi nī aṟivāy allavā?”
oru nāḷ, nāṉum eṉ tāyum vīṭṭu vēliyilīṭu paṭṭirukkumpōtu, avaṉ vīṭṭukku vantu nīr vēṭkiyāy irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. ‘avaṉukku oru kalattil nīr koṭu’ eṉṟu eṉ tāy ēviṉāḷ. nāṉum avaṉ kuṟumpukaḷi maṟantu oru kalan taṇṇīrkoṇṭu ceṉṟu koṭuttōṉ. avaṉō eṉṉik kiyip piṭittu aṇikka muyaṉṟāṉ. ‘aiyiyō! inta iyiñaṉ eṉṉa ceykiṟāṉ pār!’ eṉṟu kūviṉēṉ.
“kūkkuralikkēṭṭu eṉ aṉṉi nāṅkaḷ niṉṟa iṭattukku ōṭi vantāḷ. nīr kuṭippatil avaṉ vikkiṉāṉ eṉṟu nāṉ orupoy aḷantēṉ. eṉ tāy avaṉ mutukit taṭavik koṭuttāḷ. appōtu ak kaḷvaṉ makaṉ eṉṉik kuttiviṭu pavaṉpōl nōkki eṉṉic cirikkavittāṉ.”53
kuṉṟaṅkaḷilum mulliccōli nilaṅkaḷilum varum puttārvak kātal kāṭcikaḷuḷ kīḻvaruvataṉiviṭap puttārva muṭiya etiyum kāṇamuṭiyātu.
“nāṉum eṉ taliviyum putuveḷḷattāl poṅkip puraṇṭōṭiya kāṭṭāṟṟil kuḷittukkoṇṭiruntōm. āṟṟiṉ naṭu nīrōṭṭattarukē talivi vaḻukki, ōṭṭattil nilikoḷḷamāṭṭāmal mitantu ceṉṟāḷ. avaḷ iṭar nilikaṇṭa mālikaḷaṇinta oru vīra iḷavaracaṉ avaḷi ātaravāka eṭuttuk karikku koṇṭuvantu cērttāṉ. accamayam avaḷ tiraṇṭa mārpakaṅkaḷ avaṉ tōḷkaḷmītu poruntik kiṭantaṉa eṉṟu arukiluḷḷōr kaṇṭu pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ. itukēṭṭu eṉ talivi, ‘iṉi nāṉ inta iḷiñaṉiṭamē eṉ uḷḷattip patiya viṭṭu viṭukiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu cūḷurittāḷ. ”
“avaṉ malaṅkuṟavariṉ oru vallamivāynta kuḻuviṉ talivaṉ.”
“nāṉ avarkaḷukku eṭuttuk kūṟiṉēṉ. ‘kuṟavar kuṭippeṇ taṉ kātalaṉi maṟantu naṭantatum kiṭiyātu. kuṟavar eyta ampu taṉ kuṟiyil tappuvatum kiṭiyātu. ākavē malaṅkuṟavarākiya nīṅkaḷ marapu poyppīrkaḷāṉāl, vaḷḷikkoṭikaḷ kiḻaṅku vīḻttamāṭṭā. tēṉīkkaḷ tēṉiṟāl kaṭṭamāṭṭā. uṅkaḷ malaṅkaḻaṉikaḷ payirvaḷampeṟamāṭṭā’ eṉṟēṉ.”
“eṉ coṟkaḷkēṭṭa tāy, atit tantiyariṭam54 kūṟiṉāḷ. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ viruppattukkuriya maṟṟōr iḷiñaṉi avaḷuṭaṉ maṇam puṇarvikka virumpiyiruntārkaḷ. ākavē itukēṭṭu avarkaḷ ciṉam mūṇṭatu. kaṇkaḷ aḻaṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ villum ampum tērntaṉar. orunāḷ muḻuvatum paḻicceyalkaḷilēyē avarkaḷ eṇṇam ceṉṟatu.”
“āṉāl iḷiyōr irucārāriṭattum kuṟṟamilli eṉpatik kaṇṭapiṉ avarkaḷ kōpam taṇintatu. tam makaḷ avaḷ kātalaṉi maṇappataṟku avarkaḷ icivaḷittaṉar.”
“itaṉpiṉ kōtta kikaḷuṭaṉ nāṅkaḷ kuravik kūttāṭiṉōm.”
“piṉṉar kulattiṉ mūttōr ūrp potuvil kūṭi, aṟivaṉ talimiyil maṇaviḻā ayara muṟpaṭṭaṉar.”55
kaṟpuneṟiniṉṟa aṭakkamikka oru naṅkiyiṉ kātal varalāṟu mēlē tarappaṭṭatu. kātal muṉippuṭiya akkālattiya kuṟumpukkārap peṇ oruttiyiṉ ceyti kīḻē tarappaṭukiṟatu.
“kāyāmpū otta karuviḻi naṅkāy, kēḷ!”
“aḻakiya māliyaṇinta vil ēntiyavaṉāy ētō vēṭṭiyit toṭarntu vantavaṉpōla ōr iḷiñaṉ eṉmuṉ varuvāṉ. vantu kaṉivuṭaṉ nīṇṭanēram eṉṉi nōkkuvāṉ. āṉāl oru colkūṭac collāmal ceṉṟuviṭuvāṉ. avaṉip paṟṟiya eṇṇam eṉ uṟakkattiyē keṭuttatu. nāṉ uḷḷūrat tuyaraṭintēṉ. avaṉ taṉ kātalik kaṇṇiṉāl terivittā ṉēyaṉṟi, nāvāl urippatilli. nāṉum peṇṇāy iruppatiṉāl nāṇam kāraṇamāka avaṉi evvaḷavu kātalikkiṟēṉ eṉṟu avaṉiṭam kūṟamuṭiyavilli.”
“maṟivāṉa eṉ kātaliṉ tuṉpam poṟukkamāṭṭāmal nāṉ ceyta oru ceyal ippōtu niṉittāl kūṭa nāṉ veṭkappaṭat takkatāyirukkiṟatu. oru nāḷ nāṉ nam paṇṇi yarukil ūñcalil uṭkārntukoṇṭiruntēṉ. avaṉ vaḻakkam pōl eṉ muṉ vantu niṉṟāṉ. nāṉ avaṉi aḻittu, “aiya eṉṉi ūñcalil ciṟitu āṭṭiviṭu” eṉṟēṉ. “makiḻcciyuṭaṉ appaṭiyē ceykiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉ ūcalāṭṭiṉāṉ. appōtu nāṉ ūcaliliruntu naḻuvi viḻupavaḷ pōla naṭittu avaṉ tōḷkaḷmītu viḻuntēṉ.”
“avaṉ eṉṉi uṭaṉē taṉ kikaḷāl iṟukappiṭittāṉ. nāṉum uṇarviḻantavaḷpōla avaṉ tōḷmītu kiṭantēṉ. avaṉ eṉṉi mēlum iṟuka aṇittukkoṇṭāṉ. āṉāl nāṉ kaṇ viḻittatum kaṉivuṭaṉ taṭavikkoṭuttup pōkumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭṭāṉ. āṉāl eṉṉi ārvamākat tāṉum kātalippati avaṉ ceyal uṟutiyākak kāṭṭiviṭṭatu.”56
paṇṭik kātal pāṭalkaḷil oru putumi vāynta vaḻakkam kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atuvē maṉamuṟivuṟṟa kātalaṉ taṉ kātali veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟi naṭutteruvil taṟkoli muyaṟciyil iṟaṅkum paḻakkam ākum. oru peṇṇiṭattil nīkka muṭiyāta kātalkoṇṭu, avaḷ kātalit tāṉ peṟātu pōy viṭṭapōtu, avaṉ paṉiyiṉ karukkumaṭṭikaḷi eṭuttu avaṟṟik koṇṭu kutiri uruvam oṉṟu ceyvāṉ. atil maṉikaḷum mayiliṟakukaḷum vittu oppaṉi ceytu, kaṭivāḷam pōla oru kayiṟṟi ataṉmītu  māṭṭik koṇṭu teruvaḻikāya ataṉ mītu ūrvāṉ. ūrum pōtu taṉ kātalimītu kātaṟpāṭṭukkaḷip pāṭikkoṇṭē celvāṉ57.
ik kāṭci kaṇṭu cila camayam kātaliyē iraṅki maṉamāṟu vatuṇṭu. allatu avaḷ uṟaviṉar avaṉi maṇam ceyyumpaṭi avaḷit tūṇṭuvar. āṉāl kātali inta aḷavu veḷip paṭuttiyum kātali avaṉiṭam kaṉivu kāṭṭavilliyāṉāl, avaṉ ceṅkuttāṉa kuṉṟiliruntu vīḻntu taṉ uyiri māyttuk koḷvāṉ.
ovvoru nakarilum ūrilum potumakaḷir teru oṉṟu iruntatu. periya nakaraṅkaḷil kalvi kēḷvikaḷilum kalikaḷilum tērccipeṟṟa āṭal aṇaṅkukaḷ iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ celvamikka peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ kātaṟ kiḻattiyarāyiruntaṉar. ivvāṭal naṅkiyarukku aracaṉ taṉi matippum pātukāppum aḷittāṉ. avarkaḷ tērilum civikikaḷilum cellavum, maṉṉaṉ pūṅkāk kaḷil ulavavum, poṉṉālāṉa veṟṟilic celvaṅkaḷum kavarimāṉiṉ veṇmayirk kaṟṟi viciṟikaḷum vaḻaṅkavum, illaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷic cellumpōtu uṭivāḷaṇinta meykāvalar kuḻuviṉ kāppuṭaṉ cellavum icivaḷikkappaṭṭaṉar.58
piṟar maṉiviyaruṭaṉ ūṭāṭuvatu peruṅkuṟṟamākak karutappaṭṭut taṇṭikkap paṭṭālum, potumakaḷiri allatu āṭal naṅkiyari aṇukuvatō, avarkaḷuṭaṉ pūṅkākkaḷil ulavuvatō, potu nīrāṭṭutturikaḷil avarkaḷuṭaṉ kaḷittu viḷiyāṭuvatō avamatippeṉṟu karutappaṭavilli.
iḷiñarkaḷ 16-lum iḷanaṅkiyarkaḷ 12-lum maṇap paruvameytiyavar eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉar59. an nāḷiyakātaṟ pāṭalkaḷil pommiyum viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum tuṟakkāta paruvattilēyē peṇkaḷi iḷiñar kātalāṭum nikaḻcci kaḷukkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭalām. ic ciṟuparuva maṇaviyit tāṉ mekastaṉis kuṟippiṭukiṟārāṉālum, ‘pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ āṟuvayatilēyē kuḻanti peṟṟu viṭukiṟārkaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟum pōtu avar uyarvu naviṟciyilīṭupaṭukiṟār eṉpatil aiyamilli.60
maṇaviṉikkuc cila nāṭkaḷukku muṉṉāl, aṇiyiḻi pūṇṭa peṇkaḷ yāṉi mītēṟi naṇparkaḷiṭamum uṟaviṉar kaḷiṭamum ceṉṟu tirumaṇa viḻāvukku varumpaṭi aḻippuk kūṟiṉar. peṇ illattiṉmuṉṉē akalamāṉa uyarnta kāvaṇam amikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ mēltaḷameṅkum pūmālikaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭaṉa. maṇaviṉikkākak kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, pārppaṉak kurukkaḷ vantu kāvaṇattaṭiyil tirukkaṉal ēṟṟiṉāṉ. muracum kuḻalum caṅkum iṉṉici iyampiṉa maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum tirukkaṉalic cuṟṟi mummuṟi valam varumpaṭi iṭṭuc cellappaṭṭaṉar.
ēṟṟiya viḷakkukaḷuṭaṉ naṟumpuki, malarkaḷ, maṇap poṭikaḷ ākiyavi ēntiyavaṇṇam peṇṭir maṇamakkaḷ mītu malar tūvip pāṭalkaḷuṭaṉum vāḻttukkaḷuṭaṉum avarkaḷi maṇavaṟikku iṭṭēkiṉar.61
nakaraṅkaḷil ēḻi vakuppiṉarum, malaṅkuṭiyiṉarum, aṉṟu pārppaṉar celvākkukku uṭpaṭavilli. ākavē tirumaṇa viṉi aṟivar allatu tamiḻp pārppaṉarkaḷālēyē nikaḻttap paṭṭatu.62
tirumaṇa mutaliya kuṭumpa viḻākkālaṅkaḷ tavira, maṟṟac camayaṅkaḷil potuvāka uṇavu nērattil kuṭumpam kūṭuvatilli. ovvoru kuṭumpa uṟuppiṉarum avaravarukku vāyppāṉa camayattil tam uṇavi uṭkoṇṭu vantaṉar.
uṇavu eppōtum eḷiya uṇavākavē iruntatu. uṇaviṉ perumpakuti ariciyē. pāl, veṇṇey, tēṉ mikap potup paṭiyākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
vevveṟu vakuppiṉarum uyirvāḻkkikku uṭkoṇṭa uṇavu vakikaḷi oru nāṭucūḻvāṇar kīḻvarumāṟu viritturikkiṟār:-
“eyiṉar allatu vēṭar cenniṟamuṭiya muraṭṭu ariciyuṭaṉ uṭumpiṉ iṟicci kalantu tēkkiṉ akaṉṟa iliyil iṭṭup parimāṟiṉar. āyar pālil vēkavitta cōḷamum mocciyum, cāmiyum, nalkiṉar. uḻavu nilaṅkaḷil toḻilāḷar kōḻiyiṟiccip poriyaluṭaṉ veḷḷi arici uṇavu vaṭṭittamittaṉar. kaṭaṟkariyil mīṉ paṭavar paṉi olip paṭṭikaḷil poritta mīṉuṭaṉ cōṟiṭṭaṉar.”
“pārppaṉar māṅkāy ūṟukāyuṭaṉ cōṟum, maṇamuṭiya karuvēmpiṉ iliyuṭaṉ veṇṇeyil venta mātuḷam piñcum tantaṉar. uḻavar allatu vēḷāḷar iṉiya tiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum, palāppaḻaṅkaḷum, vāḻippaḻaṅkaḷum tēṅkāyiṉ kuḷircci poruntiya nīrum vaḻaṅkiṉar. kaḷḷuk kaṭikaḷil palanāḷ aricimā uṇaviṭṭuk koṭṭililēyē taṅkik koḻukka vikkappaṭṭa āṇpaṉṟiyiṉ vaṟuvaluṭaṉ kaḷḷum vaḻaṅkiṉar.”63
toḻilāḷar, paṭivīrar, nāṭucūḻvāṇar ākiya ēḻi vakuppiṉarkaḷāl teṉṉiyiliruntu iṟakkiya kaḷ parukap paṭṭatu64. celva vakappiṉar arici, tātakippū65 mutaliya maṇap poruḷkaḷiliruntu vaṭikkappaṭum naṟumaṇap mūṭṭappaṭṭa tēṟal vakiyi vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ66. yavaṉar kalaṅkaḷil koṇṭu varappaṭṭa kuḷirnaṟun tēṟal vakikaḷ mika viliyēṟiyaviyāy iruntirukkavēṇṭum. iviyē maṉṉariṉ viruppattukkuriya kuṭivakikaḷāy iruntaṉa.67
kāṭic caṇṭikaḷ, āṭalpāṭalkaḷ, iciyaraṅkukaḷ, camaya viḻākkaḷ ākiyaviyē potumakkaḷiṉ taliyāya poḻutu pōkkukaḷāka iruntaṉa. mātar vaḷḷippāṭṭum ammāṉip pāṭṭum pāṭumpaṭi kiḷikaḷukkuk kaṟpittum, ūñcalil ūcalāṭiyum, tāyam, kaḻaṅku, pantu mutaliyavi yāṭiyum  vīṭṭil poḻutu pōkkiṉar.
tāyam eṉpatu taṟpōkiya cokkaṭṭāṉ āṭṭamē. āṉāl cokkaṭṭāṉkāykaḷ ippōtiruppatu pōla nīḷ catukkamāṉa kaṭṭikaḷāy illi. karumpuḷḷiyiṭṭa naṇṭiṉ tōṭupōla vaṭṭavaṭivamāyiruntaṉa. kaḻaṅkāṭalil ovvoṉṟum orupākkaḷavāṉa ēḻu ciṉṉañ ciṟu kaḻalkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭuvōr uṭkārn tiruntapaṭiyē kaḻalkaḷi oṉṟoṉṟāka, iraṇṭiraṇṭāka eṉa ēṟṟi ēḻēḻvari mēlē eṟintu viḻātu uḷḷaṅkiyilō, puṟaṅkiyilō tāṅka muyaṉṟaṉar. pantāṭ ṭattil ikāyal toṭarntu aṭitta vaṇṇam pantucellum ticiyil muṉṉākavō, piṉṉākavō, cuḻaṉṟō ceṉṟāṭiṉar.68
paṇṭit tamiḻariṭiyē kaviṉ kalikaḷ mika uyarnta niliyil vaḷarccipeṟṟiruntaṉa.
potuk kalvimuṟiyil icittuṟi ōr iṉṟiyamiyāk kūṟāy iruntatu. uḷḷattil meṉmiyum iṇakka nayamum vāynata uṇarccikaḷi eḻuppum iṉimiyāṉa paṇkaḷil tamiḻar mēmpaṭṭiruntaṉar. icippaṭṭiyil ēḻu karaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi tāram, uḷi, kural, iṉi, tuttam, viḷari, kikkiḷi eṉpaṉa69. mukkiyamāṉa paṇkaḷ allatu icikkuralkaḷ nāṉku. avi pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi eṉpaṉa70. ovvoru paṇṇilum pala kiḷit tiripukaḷ iruntaṉa. aviyaṉittum cērntu 103 taṉippaṇ kūṟukaḷ71 annāḷiya ici nūlkaḷil vakuttuṇarappaṭṭaṉa.
iciyiṉ pakutikaḷākiya tāḻkural, iṭik kural, uccak kural ākiyavi aṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āṉāl avi oruṅkē pāṭappeṟavilli.72 oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākap pāṭap paṭṭaṉa.
vaḷikkaruvikaḷil (tuḷik karuvikaḷil) palvēṟu vakip paṭṭavi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. āḻnta muḻakkamuṭiya caṅkukaḷ kōyilkaḷilum, tirumaṇa viḻākkaḷilum, cāviḻākkaḷilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. pullāṅkuḻalil eṭṭut tuḷikaḷ iruntaṉa. atu palvaki icinayaṅkaḷi veḷippaṭuttat takkatāyiruntatu.
orumuka muracamum, irumuka muracamum, palvēṟuvaki ūtu kompukaḷum, ovvor iciyaraṅkilum, kuḻaluṭaṉ iṇintaṉa.
yāḻil nāṉku vakikaḷ iruntaṉa. mikac ciṟitum potuvāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatum ēḻu narampukaḷuṭitāyiruntatu.73 maṟṟa yāḻ vakikaḷ muṟiyē patiṉāṉku, patiṉēḻu, irupattoru narampukaḷ uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa74. vācikkappaṭum pōtu, yāḻ iṭatu kiyil paṟṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉ nāṉku viralkaḷ muṟukkāṇikaḷiṉ mītu paṭintaṉa. narampukaḷ peruviral tavira valatu kiyil maṟṟa viralkaḷil ētēṉum oṉṟāl uḷarappaṭṭaṉa.75
tamiḻ iciyum, āriya iciyum, taṉittaṉiyākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi oṉṟuṭaṉoṉṟu toṭarpaṟṟa iru vēṟumuṟikaḷ eṉpatiyum; iciyil muḻuniṟivupeṟa virumpu kiṟavarkaḷ iraṇṭiyum kaṟṟaṉar eṉpatiyum itu kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
āṭalum oru kaviṉ kaliyākap payilappaṭṭatu. akkālattil vaḻakkilirunta palavaki āṭalkaḷukkum vitikaḷ vakuppaṭṭiruntatuṭaṉ, akkalikkuriya ilakṇa nūlkaḷ    ēṟkeṉavē amintiruntaṉa. ivi iṉṟu kiṭṭavilli. āṉāl āṭalippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ cilappatikāratti liruntu teriyavarukiṉṟaṉa. innūliliruntē āriyak kūttu, tamiḻkkūttu eṉṟu iruvakiyāṉa āṭalvakikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
āriyak kūttil patiṉoru vakikaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi kīḻvarum nikaḻccikaḷic cittarittaṉa76 :-
(1)civaperumāṉ tiripuram erittal.
(2)tiripurattukkuc cella nāṉmukaṉ koṇarnta tēril civaperumāṉ ēṟutal.
(3)kañcaṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭa yāṉiyik kaṇṇaṉ aḻittal.
(4)kaṇṇaṉ pāṇācuraṉuṭaṉ maṟpōriṭṭu avaṉik kollutal.
(5)kantaṉ ilaṅkiyil acuraṉi muṟiyaṭittal.
(6)kantaṉ acurar paṭiyuṭaṉ pōriṭal.
(7)kaṇṇaṉ pāṇācuraṉ kōṭṭiyi aḻittal.
(8)kāmatēvaṉ aliyuruvil taṉ makaṉ aniruttaṉik kāppāṟṟap pāṇācuraṉ kōṭṭiyil pukal.
(9)koṟṟavi acurarkaḷi aḻittal.
(10)tirumakaḷ acurari ēmāṟṟal.
(11)intiraṉ, maṉivi ayirāṇi pāṇācuraṉ kōṭṭi yiṉ vaṭakku vāyilmuṉ tōṉṟuvatu.
tamiḻk kūttukkaḷil palavakikaḷ iruntaṉa. cila teyvaṅ kaḷiyō, aracariyō, avarkaḷ vīrac ceyalkaḷiyō pukaḻntaṉa. cila maṉitariyō, vilaṅkukaḷiyō avinayittaṉa. cila kātaṟ kāṭcikaḷic cittarittaṉa. iviyaṉṟik kuravik kūttum makkaḷ virumpiya āṭal vakiyāy iruntatu. itil ēḻu, eṭṭu allatu oṉpatupēr vaḷiyamāka oruvaruṭa ṉoruvar kikōttu āṭuvar. ivvāṭalil āṭavarum peṇṭirum kalantukoṇṭaṉar. ataṉuṭaṉ kātal allatu pōrpaṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa.77
ikkūttukkaḷil palavum āṭalaṇaṅkukaḷ allatu kūttiyarāl nikaḻttappaṭṭaṉa.
āṭalaṇaṅkiṉ kalippayiṟci mika iḷamiyil aintu vayatilēyē toṭaṅkappaṭṭu ēḻāṇṭukaḷ toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟatu.
avaḷ payiṟcippāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu kalippayiṟci niṟivuṭiya naṅkikkukkūṭap perumitarattakkatākum.
āṭavum, pāṭavum, meṉṉaya naṭiyuṭiya paḻakkaṅkaḷ payilavum avaḷ kaṟpikkappaṭṭāḷ. itallāmalum yāḻum kuḻalum muḻavum kiyāḷavum, ayalmoḻikaḷil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷip pāṭavum avarkaḷ paḻakiṉar. ōviyam varital, nīrviḷiyāṭṭu, pakaṭṭāṉa palvaṇṇat tūḷkaḷāl mēṉi oppaṉi ceytal, aḻakiya pūmālikaḷ toṭuttal, pūṇaṇi maṇipuṉital, paḷḷiyaṟi ciṅkārittal, kālakkaṇip puṇartal, paruvattiṟkēṟṟa ceyal takuti yuṇartal, palvēṟu kali iyal tuṟikaḷiṉ ceytikaḷpaṟṟi vācittal, putirkaḷ pōṭutal, putirkaḷ viṭuttal, piṟar karuttukkaḷi uyttuṇarntu kūṟal ākiya ittaṉiyum, avarkaḷ muḻu niṟipayiṟciyiṉ kūṟukaḷāy iruntaṉa.78
curukkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl āṭavarkaḷukkup poḻutu pōkkum iṉpakkaḷippum taruvaṉa eṉṟō, avarkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷil viyappārvam, kavarcci ākiyavaṟṟi ūṭṭuvaṉa eṉṟō karutattakka yāvaṟṟiyum āṭalaṇaṅku payiṉṟāḷ.
avaḷatu paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatil, aracaṉ nakarp perumakkaḷ ākiyōr muṉṉiliyil avaḷ araṅka vāḻvil aṭi eṭuttu vittāḷ.
camayattukkēṟpa uṭaṉaṭip pāṭal pāṭavalla oru kaviti yāciriyaṉum, akkaviñaṉ pāṭalukku uṭaṉaṭiyākap paṇṇa mittup pāṭavalla iciyāciriyaṉum, oru yāḻāciriyaṉum, oru kuḻalāciriyaṉum, oru taṇṇumiyāciriyaṉum āṭalaṇaṅ kuṭaṉ eppōtum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ovvoruvarumē avaravar tuṟikaḷil tuṟipōkap payiṟcipeṟṟavarā yiruntaṉar.
araṅkamēṭi iraṇṭaṭi uyaramum patiṉāṉkaṭi akalamum patiṉāṟaṭi nīḷamum uṭiyatāyiruntatu. mēṭiyil ēḻaṭi uyarattil tūṇkaḷiṉ ātārattiṉmēl oru mēṟkaṭṭi iṭap paṭṭiruntatu. im mēṟkaṭṭiyiṉ mēl kāval teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ōviyan tīṭṭappaṭṭa eḻiṉikaḷ (tirikaḷ) araṅka mēṭiyiṉ nāṟpuṟamum  toṅkaviṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
āṭṭam viḷakkuvikkum nērattilēyē toṭaṅkap paṭṭatu. araṅkamēṭi muḻutum oḷimayamākkappaṭṭatu.
mēṭiyiṉ akalattil āṟaṭi āṭalaṇaṅkiṉ āṭṭattuk keṉa otukkappaṭṭiruntatu. avaḷukkuppiṉ avaḷ āṭṭattiṟku vēṇṭiya tūṇṭutalkaḷ tara iraṇṭu mūṉṟu mutiya āṭalaṇaṅkukaḷ niṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukkuppiṉ āṭalaṇaṅkaḷukku ōyvukoṭuttu makkaḷik kaḷippikkum muṟiyil iṭi nēraṅkaḷil pāṭavalla cila pāṭakar iruntaṉar.
taṉ ciṟappaṇimaṇiyuṭaṉ āṭalaṇaṅku araṅkattil ēṟiṉāḷ. viḷakkoḷiyil meyyākavē cuṭariṭṭoḷi vīcum niliyil, merukiṭappaṭṭa poṉṉum, paṭṭi tīrnta maṇikaḷum nirampiya avaḷ aṇimaṇikaḷiliruntu āyira oḷikkatirikaḷ nāṟpuṟamum vīcuvatu pōṉṟiruntaṉa.
āṭalit toṭaṅkukiyil āṭalnaṅki taṉ veḷḷi maṇik kuralil taṉṉik tīṅkukaḷiliruntu kāttu aruḷ pālikkumpaṭi teyvaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkit teyvavāḻttup pāṭiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṉ muṉpē tērnteṭuttut tiṭṭam ceyyappaṭṭa āṭalpāṭal kaḷi ovvoṉṟāka āṭiṉāḷ.
avaḷ uṭalum, uṭaluṟuppukaḷum maṉita uṭal koḷḷattakum aḻakiya palavakip pāvaṅkaḷum tōṟṟa ellā vaḷivu, neḷivu kuḻikaḷum kāṭṭiṉa. avaḷ iḷamiyum, eḻilum, avaḷ māya iciyum maruṭciyūṭṭum iṉṉaya nili kaḷum, vaṇṇa oḷi viḷakkaṅkaḷum iṉṉici yalikaḷum kāṇpōrik kaḷimayakkattil āḻttiṉa.
aracaṉ avaḷukku āyiratteṭṭup poṉ kācu peṟumāṉamuḷḷa poṉmāliyi attakiya taṟuvāykaḷil paricāka aḷittāṉ.79
ōviyam, ciṟpam ākiya kalikaḷilum, tamiḻar pōtiya aḷavu, uyartērcci peṟṟiruntaṉar. teyvaṅkaḷ, maṉitar, vilaṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷ, taṉimaṉitar illaṅkaḷ, kōyil, araṇmaṉi pōṉṟa potuviṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ matilkaḷ palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷāl tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.80 paṭukkiyaṟit tirikaḷilum nāṭaka araṅkattirikaḷilum yāḻ mutaliya icikkaruvikaḷuk kuriya tuṇi uṟikaḷilum pūkkaḷum pūṅkoṭikaḷum meyvaṭivuṟa aḻakāka variyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.81
marattālum melliya kiṭiyālum mika aḻakiya patumikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa82. kōyilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum tēva tēviyar uruvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. taṅkaḷ vēṇṭukōḷ kaḷic cevimaṭuttu varamaḷikkum āṟṟal oviyar kittiṟattāl appatumikaḷukku aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu mūṭa nampikki yuṭiya potumakkaḷ eḷitil nampiṉar. anta aḷavukku mika nērttiyāṉa muṟiyil avi uyirttōṟṟam uṭiyaviyāy mey vaṇṇam tīṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa. āyiṉum kal, ulōkam mutaliya uṟutivāynta poruḷkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷ, cilikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippu etuvum annāḷiya ilakkiyattil illi.
7 allatu 8-m nūṟṟāṇṭil kaṭṭamikkappaṭṭa māmallapuram ciṟpaṅkaḷiviṭap paḻamivāynta entac ciṟpak kalic ciṉṉamum tamiḻakattil kiṭikkāmaliruppataṉ kāraṇatti itu viḷakkukiṟatu.
ēḻi eḷiya vakuppiṉariṉ vīṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. kūrikaḷ pul vēyappaṭṭaṉa. allatu teṉṉi ōliyō paṉi ōliyō pāvappaṭṭaṉa. cuvarkaḷil cemmaṇ pūcappaṭṭatu. nakaraṅkaḷiluḷḷa vīṭukaḷ perumpālum ceṅkalāl kaṭṭappaṭṭu ōṭaṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cuvarkaḷ cuṇṇāmpu tīṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. māṉ kaṇ pōla vaṭivamuṭiya palakaṇikaḷ vaḻiyāka uḷḷaṟikaḷukkuḷ oḷiyum kāṟṟum pukuntaṉa.
vāyil mukappu allatu pūmukam eppōtum vīṭṭiṉ oru muṉippāṉa pakutiyāy amintiruntatu. atil ēṟuvataṟkut teruviliruntu paṭivaricakiḷ iṭappaṭṭu, irupuṟamum oṭuttiṇṇikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟilēyē varukiyāḷar allatu vīṭṭu makkaḷ ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil taṅki yiruntaṉar.83 vāyil mukappup potuvākak kaṭṭaṭattiliruntu vilakiyiruntatu. iṭiyēyuḷḷa muṟṟattil vīṭṭukkuriya vaṉuṭiya āṭu māṭukaḷ mutaliya kālnaṭikaḷukkut toḻukkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
celvar māḷikikaḷukkuk kārikkaṭṭu mettikaḷ, kūṭakōpuraṅkaḷ, vēṉiṟkālattukkum maḻikālattukkum, vēṟu vēṟāṉa taṉippaṭukkiyaṟikaḷ ākiyavi iruntaṉa. uyar irukkikaḷ, kaṭṭilkaḷ, mēṟkaṭṭikaḷ, aṉṉattiṉ tūvi niṟinta mettikaḷ, ciliyuruvaṅkaḷāl ēntappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷ, peṇṭir kuḻantikaḷ iṉpap poḻutu pōkkuk kāṉa ūñcalkaḷ mutaliya palvēṟuvakippaṭṭa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum vaḻakkattiliruntaṉa.
kōyilkaḷum araṇmaṉikaḷumkūṭac ceṅkalālēyē kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. akkāla ilakkiyattil kal kaṭṭaṭamāka etuvum kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli. oruvēḷi periya kōṭṭikaḷiṉ matilkaḷum, ñāyilkaḷum kōpuraṅkaḷum maṭṭum arikuṟi vēlippāṭuṭiya kaṟkaḷum, nīṟum koṇṭu kaṭṭap paṭṭirukkakkūṭum.
kiṭṭat taṭṭa ellā nakarkaḷum, ūrkaḷum pakivar kaḷiṭamiruntum tiruṭarkaḷiṭamiruntum pātukāppāka araṇ ceyyappaṭṭatu. ovvoru ūrum muḷmaraṅkaḷiṉ ceṟinta vēliyāl kākkappaṭṭiruntatu. ovvoru nakarattic cuṟṟilam oru matilum akaḻiyum, akaḻiyic cuṟṟilum mikunta akalamuṭiya muḷmarakkāṭṭu vaḷiyamum iruntaṉa. maturi. karūr, kāñci ākiya peru nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kōṭṭikaḷiṉ ñāyilkaḷil muṟṟuki yiṭupavarkaḷmītu ampukaḷ vīcavum, kaṟkaḷ eṟiyavumvalla poṟikaḷ iruntaṉa.
matilarukē varuvōrip paṟṟi iḻuttuk kollat takka tūṇṭil muṉpōṉṟa poṟikaḷum, irumpu viralkaḷāl paṟṟik kiḻikkum poṟikaḷum iruntaṉa. tavira, matilēṟivara muyalpavar kaḷmītu kotikkum neyyiyum, urukkiya īyattiyum koṭṭavalla koti ulikaḷ pēṇappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷik kuttit taḷḷuvataṟ kuriya nīṇṭa īṭṭikaḷum, vēl kampukaḷum iruntaṉa.84 kōṭṭiyiṉ paṭikkalaccāliyil uṭivāḷkaḷ, vāḷkaḷ, vil amputtūṇi nirikaḷ, kēṭayaṅkaḷ, mārpuk kavacaṅkaḷ, tērkaḷ, kutiric cēṇaṅkaḷ, yāṉip paṭāṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa cēmittu vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
kōṭṭi muṟṟukivakiyil tamiḻar piṉpaṟṟiya muṟip paṭi, mutalil kōṭṭicūḻnta kāṭṭil oru perum piḷavu ceytu vaḻi uṇṭupaṇṇappaṭum. ataṉpiṉ akaḻi tūrttup paṭikaḷ matili aṇūka iṭam uṇṭupaṇṇappaṭum. iṟutiyil ēṇikaḷ mūlam matil ēṟi uṭcellavō allatu araṇ takarkkumpaṭi payiṟcitarappaṭṭa yāṉikaḷ mūlam vāyilkaḷi muṟikkavō muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyappaṭum.
pōr aṇi vakuttu niṟuttappaṭṭapōtu, yāṉikaḷ muṉṉaṇiyilum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ tērkaḷum, kutiri vīrarum, iṟutiyilēyē kālāṭpaṭiyiṉarum niṉṟaṉar. potumuṟik kālāḷvīrar iṭatukiyil māṭṭut tōlāl ceyyappaṭṭa periya kēṭayaṅkaḷum, valatu kiyil īṭṭi allatu pōrk kōṭarikaḷum tāṅkiṉar. villavar iṭatukiyil nīṇṭa villum, tōḷil toṅkalāka amputtūṇiyum tāṅkiṉar. tavira, īṭṭi vīrar, villavar ākiya irutiṟattārum iraṇṭumuḻa nīḷamuḷḷa akaṉṟavāḷ aṇintiruntaṉar.
kālāḷkaḷ tāṅkiyativiṭak kutirivīrar kuṟinta paḷuvuṭiya paṭikkalaṅkaḷum ciṟiya kēṭayaṅkaḷum tāṅkiṉar. paṭikaḷi naṭattiya talivarkaḷ tērilum yāṉikaḷilum ēṟic ceṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ eppōtum uyarkuṭiyāḷarāy iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ īṭṭiyum vāḷum ēntiṉar. poṉ vēlippāṭuṭiya kēṭayam tāṅkiṉar. iruppuk kavacam aṇintaṉar. pōrukkuriya tērkaḷ paḷuvaṟṟaṉavāy, uyaram kuṟinta iraṇṭu uruḷi kaḷiṉ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tēriṉ taṭṭic cuṟṟilum muraṭṭuppāy vēyntiruntatu. ataṉuḷ pākaṉ vīraṉ ākiya iruvarukku maṭṭumē iṭam iruntaṉa.
ovvoru tērum iraṇṭu kutirikaḷāl iḻukkap paṭṭatu nāṉku allatu ēḻu kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya peruntērkaḷ ūrvalaṅ kaḷilum viḻākkaḷilum tāṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
etirikaḷit tākkumpōtu, villāḷikaḷ ampukaḷi etirikaḷiṉ yāṉikaḷiṉmītum, avaṟṟiṉ mītu ivarnta talivarkaḷ mītum vīciṉar. villāḷikaḷ kitērntavar kaḷākavum, peruntiraḷākavum iruntāl, mutal ampu māriyilēyē etiri yāṉip paṭiyi maruṇṭōṭa vittu avarkaḷiṭiyē kuḻappam uṇṭu paṇṇuvar. āṉāl yāṉikaḷ tākkutali etirttu niṉṟu viṭṭāl, kutiri vīrarkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mītu vēlkaḷālum, kālāḷ vīrar nīṇṭa īṭṭikaḷālum tākkuvar. itaṉpiṉ neruṅkiya kikalappil avarkaḷ etirikaḷiṉ vāṭpaṭi, pōrkkōṭarip paṭi kaḷuṭaṉ maṟpōriṭuvar.
yāṉip paṭiyē oru paṭiyiṉ mikap periya valimi vāynta pakutiyāyiruntatu. ākavētāṉ oru vīrariyō mikap pala vīrarkaḷiyō koṉṟu vīḻttuvativiṭa oru yāṉiyik kolvatō, pōrukkatavātapaṭi ceyvatō mikapperiya aruñceyalākak karutappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻariṭiyē paṭivīrar vakuppic cārnta pōrvīrar tam maṉṉariṭam mikavum uṇmip paṟṟuṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar. oru pulavar tam talivariṭam ōr iḷavīraṉic cuṭṭikkāṭṭik kūṟuvatāvatu: “aḻakiya tērum yāṉip paṭiyum koṇṭa vēntē! nīr parukum kaḷḷi inta iḷiñaṉuṭaṉ paṅkiṭṭup parukuvīrāka. ēṉeṉil ivaṉ tanti koṭumpōril um tantiyiṉ tanti uyirik kākkat taṉ uyiri viṭṭāṉ. um pakivar tam īṭṭikaḷi ummi nōkki vīcumpōtu vīramikka ivviḷiñaṉ etirikaḷukkum umakkum iṭiniṉṟu pōr ceyvāṉ.” eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.85
maṟavariṭamiruntu itē vīram maṟakkulap peṇṭiriṭamum karukkoṇṭiruntatu. oru pulavar kūṟṟu iti viḷakkukiṟatu.
“ā, eṉṉē ivaḷ tuṇiccal! maṟavar kulattukkut takuti vāynta tāyē ivaḷ! nēṟṟu muṉṉāḷ tāṉ ivaḷ tanti pōrkkaḷattil oru yāṉiyi vīḻttit tāṉum kaṭum pōr ceytu vīḻntāṉ: nēṟṟu ivaḷ kaṇavaṉ oru yāṉip paṭi muḻuvatiyum kulittāṉ. āyiṉum iṉṟu pōrmuracu kēṭṭa vuṭaṉ avaṉ niṟi makiḻcci koṇṭuḷḷāḷ. avaḷ taṉ makaṉukku veḷḷāṭi uṭuttut talivīci, kiyil īṭṭi tantu kaḷattukku aṉuppukiṟāḷ.”86
iṉṉoru maṟakkulattāypaṟṟi maṟṟum oru pulavar viritturikkiṟār.
“naṭuṅkum uṭalum vaṟṟiya kikaḷum uṭiyavaḷ tāṉ ivaḷ! āṉāl makaṉ pōrkkaḷattiliruntu ōṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kēṭṭatumē, avaṉ uṇmiyilēyē puṟaṅkāṭṭiyiruntāl, avaṉukkup pālūṭṭiya mārpakaṅkaḷi veṭṭi eṟivēṉ eṉṟa cūḷuri kūṟikkoṇṭu, kiyil vāḷēntik kaḷam ceṉṟāḷ. kuṟṟuyirākak kiṭanta makaṉuṭal kaṇṭu, avaṉip peṟṟapoḻutu aṭinta makiḻviviṭak kaḻi makiḻveytiṉāḷ.”87
pōril iṟantāl vīrarkaḷukkeṉa vāṉulakil otukkap paṭṭirunta taṉiyiṭattukkut taṅkaḷ uyirkaḷ cellum eṉṟu vīrarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. immūṭa nampikkiyil avarkaḷuk kirunta uṟuti kāraṇamāka, tammaṉṉar cēviyil uyirviṭa avarkaḷ ciṟitum tayaṅkavilli. maṉṉarkaḷukkum itē mūṭanampikki iruntatu. pōril mikak kaṭumpuṇpaṭṭu vīḻnta pōtum cari, mutukiyālō, nōyālō iṟakka nērntapōtum cari, avarkaḷ pārppaṉar parappiya tiruvārnta aṟukiṉmītu kiṭattappaṭṭu, vīra māḷvupeṟumpaṭi vāḷāl piḷakkappaṭṭu iṟappatiyē virumpiṉar.88
aṇṭiyayalāruṭaṉ ōyātu naṭipeṟṟa pōrkaḷ paṭivīrarukku iṭiviṭāp payiṟci tantu avarkaḷ vīra uṇarvi vaḷarttuppēṇiṉa. maṉṉar āyaccuṟṟattiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa pāṇarum, pulavarum avarkaḷiṭiyē vīrap pukaḻārvatti uṇṭupaṇṇavum ati vaḷarkkavum mikap peritum utaviṉar. amitik kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ maṉṉaṉukkum avaṉ vīrarkaḷukkum avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷ paṟṟiya kātikaḷ kūṟi makiḻvittaṉar. pōrkkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ paṭiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu mūtātiyariṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷip piṉpaṟṟam paṭi tam pōrp pāṭalkaḷāl vīrarkaḷiyum talivarkaḷiyum ūkkiṉar.
tamiḻppāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṟapaṭi pulavarkaḷil nāṉku vakuppiṉar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷē pāṇar, kūttar, porunar, viṟaliyar eṉpavarkaḷ.
pāṇarkaḷ mikat tārnta vakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ nakarp puṟaṅkaḷil potumakaḷiruṭaṉ vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ nakarukkunakar ceṉṟapōtum avarkaḷ maṉiviyarum piḷḷi  kaḷum avarkaḷuṭaṉ uṇavukkalamum, camayaṟkalaṅkaḷum, tūkkik koṇṭu puṟappaṭṭaṉar. kūttarkaḷ naṭikarkaḷ āvar. avarkaḷ āṭiyavi  muṟiyāṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ alla. ici nāṭakamuṟi cārntavi. ati avarkaḷ pāṭiyē naṭittaṉar.
porunar allatu pōrppāṭakar maṉṉaṉ allatu talivaṉ paṭiyiṉ oru pakutiyē yāvar. avarkaḷ oru ciṟu muracu ēntic ceṉṟaṉar. atil oru kuṟuṅkōl kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntatu. ataṉutaviyāl avarkaḷ muracaṟintaṉar.
cōḻa aracaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷiyippāṭum pulavar kōvūr kiḻār kūṟuvatāvatu : “eṉ muracil kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kuṇil koṇṭu nāṉ muraciyaṭikkum ovvoru taṭaviyum, uṉpakivar naṭuṅkukiṉṟaṉar” eṉṟu avar kuṟikkiṟār. itiliruntu porunaṉ muracu aṟiyum pōtellām, atu paṭiyeṭuttuc cellutaṟku oru aṟikuṟi eṉṟu terikiṟatu.89
iv vakuppiṉ peṇpulavar viṟaliyar eṉap paṭṭaṉar.
appaṇṭik kālattiya ilakkiyattiṉ oru perum pakutikkuriya āciriyarkaḷ intap pōrppāṭakarēyāvar.
akkālap pulavarkaḷ viṭṭucceṉṟa pāṭalkaḷiliruntu namakku maturimā nakariṉ oru viḷakkamāṉa muḻu uyir ōviyam kiṭittuḷḷatu. anta oviyamē tamiḻar camutāya vāḻvu kuṟitta inta iyaliṉ niṟimuṭivākak kūṟattakkatāyamikiṉṟatu.90
“viṭiya oru yāmattukku muṉpē, pārppaṉa iḷamāṇa varkaḷ maṟivācakaṅkaḷi ōtattoṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. iciva lārkaḷ tam yāḻi mīṭṭi atil pāṭaṟpayiṟci toṭaṅku kiṉṟaṉar. appa vāṇikar eḻuntatum kaṭikaḷiṉ nilattaḷattit tuppuravākka muṉikiṉṟaṉar. kaḷviṉiñar munti varukiṉṟa tam vāṭikkikkārari eṇṇik kaṭikaḷit tiṟakkiṉṟaṉar.”91
“pāṇar tam kāli vāḻttukkaḷip pāṭiyavaṇṇam valam varukiṉṟaṉar. kōyilkaḷilum, tuṟaviyar maṭaṅkaḷilum, pāṇṭiyaṉ araṇmaṉiyilum caṅkukaḷ muḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iṭiyōcipōla muracukaḷ iyampukiṉṟaṉa92. kālik katiravaṉ iḷaṅkatirkaḷ kōṭṭiyiṉ kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ uccikaḷukkup poṉmulām pūci nakariṉ pāriya nīḷa akalaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.”
“peru vītikaḷ āṟukaḷpōṉṟa nīḷa akalamuṭiyaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ irumaruṅkilum uḷḷa uyariya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ perum pālum mēlmāṭikaḷiyum pala māṭip palakaṇikaḷiyum uṭiya viyāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.”93
“ovvoru kōyililum oru koṭi paṟakkiṟatu. ovvoru kaḷ viliyakattilum oru kaḷitukil āṭukiṉṟatu. ovvoru vāṇikakkaḷattiṉ paṇṭacāliyilum oru taṉikkoṭi tikaḻkiṉṟatu. ataṉuṭaṉ aracaṉ paṭikaḷpeṟṟa ovvoru veṟṟi kuṟittum ovvoṉṟākap palvaṇṇat tukiṟkoṭikaḷ alivīcukiṉṟaṉa. ikkoṭikaḷiṉ tiraḷum pala vaṇṇaṅkaḷum cērntu, nakaram oru viḻāvukkāka aṇiceyyappaṭṭatō eṉṟa tōṟṟam aḷikkiṟatu.”
“pakip pulaṅkaḷil cūṟiyāṭiya maṉṉaṉ paṭip pirivukaḷ nakarukku vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kutirikaḷ, yāṉikaḷ, muṟṟukiyiṭṭuveṉṟa kōṭṭikaḷiṉ aṇimaṇivāyikaḷ pōṉṟa veṟṟic ciṉṉaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ koṇarkiṉṟaṉar. vēṟucila paṭip pirivukaḷ iravil eriyūṭṭip pakivar ūrkaḷi liruntu kavarnta āṉirikaḷi, īṭṭikaḷiyē kōlākakkoṇṭu turattikkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. maṉṉaṉukkuk kāṇikkiyākat tarak kuṭi maṉṉar paricukaḷuṭaṉ avarkaḷip piṉtoṭarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.”
“ciṟitu nērattukkuḷ utirip pūkkaḷum mālikaḷum niṟinta kūṭikaḷi ēntiya pūvāṇikar, maṇattūḷ, pākku veṟṟili ākiyavaṟṟiṉ viṟpaṉiyāḷar terukkaḷil ulaviyum uyar māṭaṅkaḷiṉ niḻalil otuṅki iḷippāṟiyum kāṭci tarukiṉṟaṉar cuvi tarum iṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum iḷamaṇa malarkaḷum koṇṭu cellum mutiya peṇṭir vāyiltōṟum ēṟi iṟaṅki vīṭṭi luḷḷavarkaḷiṭam poruḷkaḷi viṟpaṉi ceykiṉṟaṉar.”
“celva vakuppiṉar kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya tērkaḷil allatu taṉippayiṟcipeṟṟa kutinaṭipōṭum pōrk kutirikaḷ mītu celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“avvappōtu oru matayāṉi taṉ caṅkilip piṇippi aṟuttukkoṇṭu kāvalarik koṉṟu vītivaḻiyē pāyntōṭu kiṉṟatu. atukaṇṭu ēḻi viṟpaṉiyāḷarum ciṟu vaṇikarum accamum kiliyum koṇṭu aṅkumiṅkum paraparappuṭaṉ ōṭukiṉṟaṉar. yāṉiyiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum kāvalmakkaḷ ōṭukiṉṟaṉar. teruviluḷḷa makkaḷi eccarikkum paṭi avarkaḷ caṅkukaḷi muḻakkikkoṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“avvappōtu kuṭittu veṟikoṇṭa pōrvīrar teruviṉ amiti keṭukkiṉṟaṉar.”
“akaṉṟa catukkaṅkaḷil amintuḷḷa cantikaḷil pal vakipporuḷkaḷum viṟpaṉikku aṭukkiyuḷḷaṉa.”94
“vaṇṭikaḷ, tērkaḷ, tēriṉ aṇimukaṭukaḷ; vāḷāl tuṇikkamuṭiyāta kavacaṅkaḷ, iruppu arik kackikaḷ; tōl mitiyaṭikaḷ, kavariyiṉ veḷḷiya vāl mayirāl ceyta viciṟikaḷ; yāṉi ūkkum aṅkucaṅkaḷ, mukapaṭāttiṉ kāppuk kēṭayaṅkaḷ; cempu pittaḷiyāl ceyta palavaki uruppaṭikaḷ; aḻakiya pū mālikaḷ, pūcucāntam, pukiccāntam mutaliya collutaṟkariya palavakip poruḷkaḷ accantikaḷil koṭṭappaṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.”
“aṇimaṇikārar vītiyil annāḷiya vaṇikarkaḷē vakiyaṟintirunta nālvaki vayiraṅkaḷ, marakataṅkaḷ, māṇikkaṅkaḷ, nīlaṅkaḷ, puṭparākaṅkaḷ, viṭūriyaṅkaḷ, kōmētakaṅkaḷ, oḷitarum muttukkaḷ, cempavaḷa maṇikaḷ ākiyavi viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.”
poṉ vāṇikar oru taṉi vītiyil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ cātarūpam kiḷicciṟi, āṭakam, cāmpūnatam eṉṟa peyarkaḷik koṇṭa nālvakippaṭṭa taṅkattiyum viṟṟaṉar.
“paruttiyālum kampaḷiyālum paṭṭālum, iyaṉṟu pal vaṇṇamum palvakip paṭivaṅkaḷum koṇṭa āṭikaḷ pakaṭṭāka niriniriyāka maṭittaṭukkappaṭṭu ovvor aṟuvik kaṭiyilum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāka viṟpaṉikkuk kāttuk kiṭantaṉa.”
“miḷakuc carakkukaḷ, nel, cāmi, koḷḷu, payaṟu, eḷḷu mutaliya patiṉāṟuvakik kūlaṅkaḷiṉ kuviyalkaḷ kaṭikaḷilum vītikaḷilum nirampikkiṭantaṉa.”
“kiyil eḵku muḻakkōlum paṭiyaḷavikaḷum ēntiyavāṟu tarakarkaḷ eṅkum cuṟṟittirikiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ vāṅkum miḷakiyum, kūlavakikaḷiyum, vaṇikar aḷantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.”
“aṟaṅkūṟaviyaṅkaḷil kaliniṟi naṭuvar talimi yiṭattil vīṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṭuñciṉamō mikuti kaṇṇōṭṭamō iṉṟi, irutiṟaccāṉṟukaḷiyum oppa eṭi pōṭṭup pārttu, accamō calukiyō iṉṟic caṭṭamuṟi kaḷi āynturikkiṉṟaṉar.”*
“amiccaraviyil kāviti eṉṉum uyar paṭṭam peṟṟa pukaḻuṭiya maṉṉar, matiyamiccarkaḷ vīṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tam kūriya aṟivuttiṭpattāl maṉṉaṉukkum avaṉ āḷum makkaḷukkam nērakkūṭum tīmikaḷi muṉṉaṟintu taṭuttum, naṉmikaḷi aṟintu perukkiyum ceyalāṟṟu kiṉṟaṉar.”
matilcūḻnta oru pērakal vaḷivukkuḷ pāṇṭiyaṉ araṇ maṉi tikaḻkiṉṟatu96. ataṉ vāyil mika uyaramāka iruppataṉāl, yāṉikaḷ tammītuḷḷa koṭikaḷit tāḻttāmalē uḷḷē nuḻiya muṭikiṟatu.
oru kaviñar vāyilip pāṭiyuḷḷār.
“vāyilmītuḷḷa kōpuram oru mali pōṉṟatu. ataṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa vāyil mali kuṭintu tiṟantu uruvākkiya turappuvaḻi pōṉṟirukkiṟatu.”
“vāyil katavukaḷ maṭakkuk katavukaḷāy, irumpu viciṟi iṇikkappaṭṭu, tiṇṇiya kaṉatta irumpukkampikaḷum tāḻkaḷumiṭṭu valivūṭṭappaṭṭaviyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iviyāvum ceṅkurutivaṇṇattil tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vāyil tūṇkaḷil kāval teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.”
“uḷmuṟṟa veḷiyil maṇal parappappaṭṭuḷḷatu. am maṇalil nīṇṭa vālmayiruṭiya kavarimāṉum, kuṟuṅkālkaḷi yuṭiya aṉṉaṅkaḷum viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṟṟattiṉ maṟṟorupuṟattil maṉṉaṉ kutiri ilāyaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil maṉṉaṉ ciṟappurimikkuriya uyariya tōṟṟamuṭiya pōrkkutirikaḷ enta nēramum āyattamāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa.”
“araciyārukkum araciyāriṉ āyattārukkum taṉippaṭṭa vīṟumikka māṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ elliyil maṉṉaṉ tavira maṟṟa enta āṭavarum nuḻital kūṭātu. avaṟṟil vāṉavilliṉ ēḻu niṟaṅkaḷum koṇṭa koṭikaḷ āṭukiṉṟaṉa. cuvarkaḷ merukiṭṭa pittaḷi niṟamākat niṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭiyiṭiyē veḷḷipōla miṉṉum tiṟattuṭaṉ avi nīṟiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cuvariṉmītu tīṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya pūṅkoṭi ōviyaṅkaḷum, tūṇkaḷiṉ nīla niṟamum cērntu mika aḻakiya ōviyakkāṭciyāy amikiṉṟaṉa.”
“kolumaṇṭapattil pāṇṭiyaṉ aḻakuṭaṉ vīṟṟiruk kiṟāṉ. avaṉ iṭuppil muṟukalākat tēyttumaṭiyiṭap paṭṭa orē oru āṭi aṇintirukkiṟāṉ.97 ataṉ mītu maṇipatitta kacci iṟukak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.”
“muḻaṅkikkumēl, mūṭāta mēṟkimītu, aḻakiya vēlip pāṭaminta poṉṉālāṉa kiyaṇikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.”
“avaṉ akaṉṟa tōḷkaḷ iṉiya maṇamuṭiya cāntaṇiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ataṉmītu pū māliyuṭaṉ oḷimikka muttumāli oṉṟum puraḷkiṉṟatu.”
“aracaṉic cūḻntu pala araṇkaḷik kippaṟṟiyum, pala yāṉakiḷip pōril vīḻttiyum pukaḻpeṟṟu, pala kalaṅkaḷil nintu muṟivuṟṟa porumiyuṭiya kavacamaṇintavāṟu avaṉ nampikkikkuriya uramikka vīrarum paṭittalivarkaḷum niṟkiṉṟaṉar.”
“kūttarum, pāṇarum, yāḻppāṇarum, maṉṉaṉ muṉṉili yil taṅkaḷ tiṟaṅkaḷkāṭṭit tērum yāṉikaḷum paricilkaḷākap peṟukiṉṟaṉar.”
“kuḷirnta māli nēraṅkaḷil, perumakkaḷ kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya tērkaḷil ēṟic celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kālāḷvīraṉ avarkaḷuṭaṉ tēriṉ arukilēyē ōṭivarukiṟāṉ98. avarkaḷ civanta āṭi aṇikiṉṟaṉar. poṉṉāl iḻikkap paṭṭa uṟikaḷil, vilāppakkamāka avarkaḷ vāḷkaḷ toṅkukiṉṟaṉa.”
“peruṅkuṭip peṇṭir kālil cilampum kiyil poṉ vaḷikaḷum aṇintu māḷikiyiṉ māṭaṅkaḷil vantu niṉṟu ulavukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ mēṉimītuḷḷa naṟumaṇam terukkaḷil paravukiṉṟatu.”
“potu vītikaḷil caṅkaṟuppōr, maṇikōppavarkaḷ, poṉ viṉiñar, kaṉṉār, tiyal vēlikkārarkaḷ, necavu vēliyāṭkaḷ, ōviyar, pūvāṇikarkaḷ, maṇapporuḷ vāṇikar mutaliya palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa makkaḷ miṭintu neruṅkit tattam poruḷkaḷi viṟṟavaṇṇam celkiṉṟaṉar99.”
“uṇavakaṅkaḷum, aruntakaṅkaḷum, ippōtu varuvōr pōvōr tiraḷkaḷāl nirampiyuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷ māṅkaṉiyum palākkaṉiyum, kaṟkaṇṭum, pacuṅkāykaḷum, kāyccaṟkiḻaṅkum carkkarip poṅkalum iṉiyavimalinta kaṟiyuṇavukaḷum uṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.”
“palvaki aravaṅkaḷi aṭakki mēleḻuntu muracukaḷum icikkaruvikaḷum tiṭumeṉa muḻaṅki, makkaḷi māli vaḻipāṭṭu kkuk kūviyaḻikkiṉṟaṉa. miṉṉum aṇimaṇiyāṭi puṉinta peṇṭir tattam kaṇavaruṭaṉum makkaḷuṭaṉum naṟumpukiyum malarum tāṅkip putta maṭaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“kuṭinta pāṟikaḷiluḷḷa kukikaḷ pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum otukkiṭaṅkaḷil pārppaṉat tuṟavikaḷ tattam camayakkiḷi kaḷukkuriya mantiraṅkaḷi muṇumuṇukkiṉṟaṉar. ammaṭaṅkaḷiṉ matilkaḷ celvaṇṇam pūcappaṭṭu vāṉaḷāviyuyarntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi yaṭuttu aḻakiya pūntōṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.”
“vēṟu cilar pārppaṉac camayak kōyilkaḷukkac ceṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷukku māli vēḷviyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.”
“at teyvaṅkaḷil mutaṉmiyāṉavar aimpūtaṅkaḷiyum paṭittu āṟṟal cāṉṟa maḻuppaṭi ēntiya teyvam (civaperumāṉ) āvar.”100
“māli niḻaṟpaṭalaṅkaḷ neruṅkuntōṟum, ovvoru vīṭṭilum viḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṉṉiṟa uṭiyaṇinta iḷiñarkaḷ orupuṟam, malarkaḷum aṇimaṇiyiḻikaḷum pūṇṭa potumakaḷir; maṟṟorupuṟam vītikaḷil ulavat toṭaṅku kiṉṟaṉar. teruvil yāṉikaḷ cellāmal taṭuppataṟkākak kavikkaṟkaḷ pōṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟic caṭṭi paṇṇāmal taḷḷāṭiyavaṇṇam avaṟṟiṉmītē kuṭiveṟiyar celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“iḷantāyarāṉa naṅkiyarkaḷ viḷakkukaḷuṭaṉum uṟaviṉarkāvaluṭaṉum yāḻum muḻavum uṭaṉiṇiya iṉiya paṇ pāṭikkoṇṭu, tam kuḻantikaḷukkut tīṅkaṟṟa nalvāḻvu aruḷumpaṭi tēviyi vaṇaṅkuvataṟkāka aviyuṇavum kāṇik kikaḷum koṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉar. pūcinaṅkiyuṭaṉ avarkaḷ 
avvaruḷuṇavip pakirntuṇkiṉṟaṉar.
“puṟañcērikaḷil ēḻi vakuppiṉar kaṇiyariṉ āṇippaṭi murukaṉukku viḻāveṭukkiṉṟaṉar. kallā muraṭṭup paṇkaḷuṭaṉum āravārattuṭaṉum pāṭṭu muḻakkattuṭaṉum avarkaḷ kikōttu āṭukiṉṟaṉar. cilliṭṭu ārpparikkum tāḷaṅkaḷum muracaṅkaḷum cērntu muraṇici muḻakkukiṉṟaṉa.”
“paṭippaṭiyāka āṭalpāṭaṟ kuḻukkaḷ kalikiṉṟaṉa. ciṟu kaṭikkārarkaḷ tam kaṭikaḷi aṭikkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉuṇā vāṇikar tattam kaṭi muṉṟililēyē tuyir koḷkiṉṟaṉar. nakaril aravam muṟṟilum ōykiṟatu.”
“iravuk kāvalar ippōtu terukkaḷil kāval varukiṉṟaṉar. piḻiyā ilakkuṭiya villampēntiyavarkaḷāy, añcāta neñcuṭaṉum, tuyilāta kaṇkaḷuṭaṉum, avarkaḷ nakar cuṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. maḻipoḻiyum iruṇṭa iravukaḷil teruvellām veḷḷakkāṭāka ōṭumpōtum avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṭamikaḷil ciṟitum tavaṟuvatilli.”101

29.	tolkāppiyam aiaiai marapiyal.
30.	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ tolkāppiyar āriyar eṉṟa karutti maṟuttār. nūlāciriyarippōla avar āriyar eṉṟu karutupavar iṉṉum cilar uṇṭu. āyiṉum ikkaruttu iṉṟu valuviḻantē varukiṟatu. akattiyar tolkāppiyar katiyiyiṉṟi itaṟku ātāram illi. akkatikkō tolkāppiyattil oru ciṟitukūṭa ātāram kiṭiyātu. āyiṉum tolkāppiyatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu āciriyar kāṭṭum āriya - tamiḻiṉap paṇpu vēṟupāṭukaḷ ivvaṭippaṭit tavaṟutalāl valiviḻakkavilli. tolkāppiyar kūṟum antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar piriviṉi cāti vēṟupāṭu aṉṟu, toḻil vēṟupāṭē, atuvum nāṭṭiṉ naṭuppakutiyākiya marutattiṇiyi maṭṭumē atu kuṟittatu. nālu pirivukaḷum vēḷāḷar vakuppiṉ pirivukaḷēyākum. ēṉeṉil vēḷāḷarē marutanilakkuṭikaḷ.
31.	tolkāppiyar oru āriyap pārppaṉar eṉṟa karuttit toṭarntu eḻuntatē intak karuttum. tirunūl aṇital mutalāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ āriyap pārppaṉarukkē uriyaṉa eṉṟa karuttu itaṉuṭaṉ iṇikiṟatu. āṉāl tirunūl aṇital tamiḻp pārppaṉar allatu kurumāriṭamiruntu kurumār toḻili mēṟkoṇṭa āriyar kikkoṇṭa vaḻakkam eṉpatil aiyamilli. ēṉeṉil āriyar intiyā varumuṉ nūlum nūṟṟalum uṭiyavarallar. cilar āriyar varumpōtu atit tōlāl aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟum, atu avarkaḷ vēṭṭuva nilik kālattil ampuk kūṭukaḷ toṅkaviṭuvataṟkāka iṭṭa vārē eṉṟum, iṉṟum pūṇūlil māṉtōl tuṇṭu iṇippatu ammarapiṉ ciṉṉamē eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
	iṉṟum pārppaṉar maṭṭumiṉṟi, kammiyar mutaliya kalit teḻilāḷarum tirunūlaṇikiṉṟaṉar. cilar karutuvatupōla itu putu vaḻakkeṉṟu tōṉṟavilli. nūlaṇital nūlaṟiviṉ kuṟiyātaliṉ, taṉittuṟi nūlaṟivu iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭṭa toḻil tuṟiyiṉar yāvarum toḻiṟ kuḻukkaḷ aminta kāliyil ati mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. tamiḻp pārppaṉar ākama nūlaṟivukāṭṭa aṇinta ataṉiyē tam vēta aṟivukāṭṭa āriyap pārppaṉar mēṟkoṇṭaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉṉalām.

10. tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟaḷ
akkālac ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷuḷ pala muḻu vaṭivilē iṉṟu namakku vantu cērntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṭiyē taliciṟanta makkaṭ pukaḻ uṭiyatu vaḷḷuvarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa muppāl allatu kuṟaḷ ākum. piṉvanta talimuṟikaḷaṉittilumē ataṉ celvākku mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar vāḻkkiyippaṟṟi namakku ārāycci muṟikku otta cāṉṟukaḷ etuvum kiṭṭavilli. āṉāl avar taṟpōtu ceṉṉimānakariṉ pēṭṭikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyirukkum paḻamivāynta mayilāppūril piṟantavar eṉṟu potuvāka nampappaṭukiṟatu. tam peru nūlukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ ātaraviyum pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya caṅkappulavar opputaliyum peṟum nōkkattuṭaṉ avar maturikkuc ceṉṟār.
ip pulavarkaḷil cilar maturiyiṟ piṟantavarkaḷ. maṟṟum cilar cōṇāṭṭiluḷḷa uṟiyūriliruntum, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉat tiliruntum vantavarkaḷ. vēṟu cilar koṅku nāṭṭiluḷḷa cellūriliruntum, toṇṭināṭṭiluḷḷa vēṅkaṭattiliruntum, tamiḻakattiṉ ēṉiya pakutikaḷiliruntum vantavarkaḷ.
‘iṉiya nāṟpatu’ eṉṟa ciṟiya nūliṉ āciriyar pūtaṉcēntaṉār; aṟaneṟi vācakaṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya tirikaṭukattiṉ āciriyar nallātaṉar; kalittoki tokutta nallantuvaṉar; iṉpattuṟi atāvatu akapporuḷ ilakkaṇam tanta iṟiyaṉār; kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, iṉṉā nāṟpatu ākiya ciṟumaṇic celvaṅkaḷi aḷitta kapilar; ālaṅkāṉappōr veṟṟikaṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ; veṟṟivākiyi maturik kāñciyāl viṟal paṭap pāṭiya māṅkuṭi marutaṉār; neṭunalvāṭi, tirumuru kāṟṟuppaṭi mutaliya kaviṉuṟu ciṟu kāviyaṅkaḷiyaṟṟiya pulamicāl nakkīrar; cuvimikka putta peruṅkāviyamākiya maṇimēkaliyi uruvākkittanta puttap perum pulavar cīttalic cāttaṉār ākiyōr appulavar peruṅkuḻuvil iruntaṉar.
ivarkaḷallāmal, maruttuvam, kaṇitam, ilakkiyam ākiyavaṟṟil taṉicciṟappaṭinta perumpērāciriyarkaḷum iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷukkuriya taṉic ciṟappu nūlkaḷ nammivan teṭṭavilli.
annāḷiya taliciṟanta kaviñar, pulavar ākiyavar                            kaḷiṭiyē, tamiḻakattiṉ pulavarkaḷi ātarikkum puravalarkaḷil taliciṟantavaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirapperuvaḻutiyiṉ oppu taliyum etirnōkki niṉṟirukkavēṇṭum.
nūliṉ peyarē kuṟittukkāṭṭukiṟapaṭi, muppāl mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷāy iyaṅkiṟṟu. avi aṟam allatu naṟkuṇam, poruḷ allatu celva vāḻvu, iṉpam atāvatu kātal eṉpaṉa. cūttirap paṇpiṉ maṇikcurukkacceṟivōṭu kavitip paṇpiṉ iḷimiyum oruṅkēkoṇṭa nīti vācakaṅkaḷiṉ tokuti atu.
āciriyar nikaṇṭaneṟi cārntavareṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu1. iṅṅaṉam tam nikaṇṭa neṟiyil uṟutiyāṉa paṟṟuṭiyavarā yiṉum, avar tiṟanta cintiyarākak kāṇappaṭukiṟār. meyyaṟiveṉpatu meyyiṉpattukkuriya aṟiveṉṟu avar karutiṉār.
“ītali ēṟpatu, ēṟpavaṉ kuṟikaḷik kaṭṭāyam nīkkum. āyiṉum ēṟṟal nallataṉṟu” eṉṟār avar. atumaṭṭumaṉṟu “ītaliṉ payaṉākat tuṟakkam eṉṟu oṉṟu kiṭṭātiruntālkūṭa, ītal nallatē” eṉṟu avar valiyuṟuttiṉār.
“añcāta vīra uḷḷam uyarvuṭiyatē. āṉāl iṉṉalil uḻalpavar nilikaṇṭu urukum īraneñcam ataṉiṉum ciṟanta tākum” eṉpatu avar karuttu.
iṉpam niṟinta illaṟavāḻviṉ aruñciṟappukkaḷi avar iṉiya vaḷamikka coṟkaḷāl pārāṭṭukiṟār. kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyi avar kaṭavuḷ taṉmiyuṭiya oruvarākap pōṟṟukiṟār.
“tām īṉṟeṭutta ciṟuvar maḻali iḷañcoṟkaḷik kōḷātavartām pullāṅkuḻaliṉ ici iṉiteṉpar; yāḻici iṉiteṉpar.”
“kaṇavaṉiyaṉṟi pēṟu kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkāta kārikiyariṉ viruppattukku vāṉamum iṇaṅki maḻi poḻiyum.”
“potumakaḷir toṭarpi nāṭuvōri avar ikaḻuk kuriyōr eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. “”vilimakaḷ muyaṅkum poy muyakkam iruṭṭaṟiyil taṭavip piṇam taḻuviya aṉupa vattukku oppākum.”
māntar ceyyattakka toḻilkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vēḷāṇ toḻil ceypavariyē avar uyarvumikkōrākak koṇṭaṉar. “uḻutuṇṭu vāḻpavarkaḷ tām uṇmiyil iṉpa vāḻvu vāḻpavarkaḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ vāḻvellām piṟarip piṉpaṟṟi niṉṟu toḻutu vāḻum vāḻvēyākum.”
ceyalaṟṟu aḻutaṟiyum ūḻvātikaḷikkaṇṭu avar ēḷaṉa naki nakikkiṟār. ettaku peruṅ kāriyattiyum muyaṟci yālaṭital kūṭum eṉa avar ūkkukiṟār. “ayarātu ūkkattuṭaṉ uḻippavar ūḻiyum velvatu uṟuti” eṉpatu avar kōṭpāṭu.
“oru ceyal mikak kaṭumiyāṉatu eṉṟeṇṇi maṉan taḷarvaṭiyātē peru veṟṟikaḷ yāvum iṭi viṭā muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉkaḷē!”
kalviyi avar ellārkkum uriya potuvurimiyākap parāviṉār. kalviyillāta vāḻvu vāḻvataṟkuriya ciṟappuṭiya taṉṟu eṉak kūṟiṉār. “kalviyaṟṟavarkaḷum vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl atu eṉṟum viḷiviyāta vaṟṟaṟpāli pōṉṟa vāḻvu.” “kalvikallātavarkaḷukkum kalvi kaṟṟavarkaḷukkum iṭiyē uḷḷa vēṟupāṭu vilaṅkukaḷukkum makkaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭē! meyyaṟivuṭiyavarkaḷ,              ellāp poruḷkaḷum uṭiyavarkaḷē! aṟivillāta varkaḷō, etu iruntālum, oṉṟum illātavarēyāvar.”
